WINDOWS NATIVE · 已验收实验源码快照 · 2026-10-26

Live Photo 07 / 08 / 09
方法区别与对应代码 · 公开脱敏版

07 是保留原视频关键帧时间的 JPG 对照；08 改写原机关键帧时间并输出 JPG；09 使用与 08 相同的视频流程，输出 HEIC。用户已确认 09 在 iPhone 上成为单一 Live Photo，使用所选新封面，画面与长按声音均正常。

这份文件记录什么
实现来源严格固定为保存修复1 2.0-experiment.2。下方包含全部 14 个本地运行源码文件、3 个相关验证源码文件、已脱敏的构建来源清单示例及随包许可文本。这是一份源码快照和说明，不是完整可执行环境，也不是新构建；未嵌入 FFmpeg、ExifTool、HEIC 编码器、Python 等二进制。本文件没有 JavaScript、外部字体、图片、样式或联网资源引用，可离线阅读，点击折叠项查看代码。
方法区别真人结果处理流程09 集成计划版本与哈希运行依赖完整源码许可原文

三个方法的实际差异

方法图片MOV 与关键帧时间作用
07
keep_timing		所选帧 → 真 JPEG，quality 100保留原 MOV 的轨道、样本与旧 still-image-time；ExifTool 仅更新共享配对 ID。检查完整原机元信息与新静态图的导入兼容性。它不承诺把视频关键帧时间移至所选帧。
08
original_marker		所选帧 → 真 JPEG，quality 100沿用原机 marker 声明及轨道外壳，以精确 PTS 调整 edit list；随后更新共享 ID。实现所选新关键帧，并比较 JPG 容器。
09
original_marker		所选帧 → 真 HEIC；使用既有 HEIC 编码器与 08 调用同一个 rewrite_original_marker；只有图片输出格式不同。已获本次用户完整手机验收，作为产品接入依据。

三个方法都会生成新的共同 UUID，写入图片 Apple MakerNote 的十进制 tag 17 与 MOV 的 ContentIdentifier。它们都保留完整 MakerNote 的非 ID 字段，以及白名单相机信息、拍摄日期 / 时区 / 子秒和原始 GPS 字段；输出新封面方向为 1，尺寸和修改时间更新。拍摄 EXIF 时间与视频所选帧 PTS 是不同概念，不互相替代。

三者均在新目录发布，不覆盖原件。声音、视频和辅助轨道不重新编码；写后检查编码样本及时间。HEIC / JPEG 封面是所选视频帧的新编码，不能以“视频编码不变”推称图片无损或与 Apple ImageIO 字节相同。

真人结果与保留的限制

  • 07：用户确认能导入为 Live Photo。由于程序保留旧 marker 时间，这不构成“新关键帧时间已更新”的验收。
  • 08：一次操作成功；另一次在保存阶段报“所选时间不是唯一有效视频呈现帧”。这是保存拒绝，不应写成所有结果都成功，也不应写成那次结果已生成后被手机拒绝。
  • 09：用户确认导入后是单一 Live Photo，封面就是所选帧，画面正常，iPhone 长按播放有声音。随后专项确认 JPG 输入转换为 HEIC 也满足单一 Live、新封面、画面与声音正常。
  • 声音记录纠正：此前“无声音”来自手机处于静音，用户已明确排除音频故障；该误判撤回，不以它继续修改音轨。

以上为本会话用户真人反馈的整理，适用于本次试验与所选样本，不是所有 iPhone、导入器和任意 MOV 的兼容性保证。自动读回、编码数据 SHA、合成测试只能证明各自检查的属性，不能代替手机验收。

版本边界
此文件未混入后续 fix2 的 CTTS 兼容修改，完整保留已验收 fix1 的解析器与失败边界。源码 / 构建旧记录中的“尚未测试手机”是生成记录时的真实状态，历史语义保留；本公开版已替换路径并标注示例记录，上面的真人结果是后续补充。旧版本曾在保存阶段遇到元信息不完整错误，保存修复1 已改为原始 typed EXIF 精确保留，不再用浮点容差掩盖 GPS 差异。

实现路径与关键约束

lab_app.py → methods.get_method → engine_round2.Session.export
  共用：原组 ID 一致 + 源指纹 → FrameReader 精确 PTS / 实际帧 → 新 UUID
  图片：engine_image.write_cover → JPEG 或 HEIC → 完整 MakerNote / raw EXIF → 独立读回
  07：copy_movie_with_identifier → 保留旧 marker 时间
  08 / 09：rewrite_original_marker → set_movie_identifier → 所选精确 marker 时间
  最后：图片 / MOV 同 ID + 单 marker + A/V 编码数据相同 + 源未变 → 发布新目录

图片：完整信息与精确 GPS

engine_image.py 有界读取原 EXIF 的类型、数量和原始值字节，重建只包含允许字段的新 EXIF 块；保留原始字节序与 GPS 有理数分子 / 分母，保留 GPSVersionID 的原有存在或缺失状态。它不把旧缩略图、旧像素尺寸或任意不在白名单内的 EXIF 一并带入。完整 Apple MakerNote 单独保留，支持 LONG8 等字段；只改 tag 17 配对 ID，不猜写 LivePhotoVideoIndex，也不把拍摄时的其他 MakerNote 值当成新帧测量。

ExifTool 接收 ASCII 启动参数与 UTF-8 标准输入参数文件，避免 Windows 中文 / 空格 / emoji 路径被 ACP 损坏。写后同时核验原始 typed capture 字段、ExifTool 数值读回、MakerNote 非 ID 字段、尺寸 / 方向 / ID；元数据改写前后的新封面解码像素必须相同。允许合法 offset / padding 重排，不把 MakerNote 物理块完全相同误当成唯一有效保存方式。

MOV：保留原机声明与时间轨属性

engine_movie_round2.py 要求原组有唯一支持的 still-image-time 轨和唯一 int8 -1 标记，所选 Fraction 时间必须是唯一实际呈现视频帧，且能由原 movie 时钟精确表示。纯标记轨复用原轨 ID；混合元数据轨保留其他 key / 数据 / 时间，并从原标记声明克隆一条只含标记的新轨。保留原机 handler、flags、引用、样本描述扩展和 marker 持续时间等约束，以 edit list 表达起始延迟；不改成任意新模板，也不猜改 marker 值。

需要保留原数据偏移时，旧 moov 改作 free，再追加新 mdat 和单个活动 moov；若 moov 原本位于文件尾，直接用新索引替换该尾部布局。写后核验原始 mdat 编码数据与非 marker 数据 / 时间、非元数据轨；再由 ExifTool 只更新 staged MOV 的 ID，并核对全部轨样本、时序、去除必要 chunk-offset 后的轨属性与非 ID 全局元数据。

精确帧与事务范围

FrameReader 读取实际整数时间戳和 time_base，构成 Fraction。没有精确时间时拒绝保存，不以平均帧率估算冒充所选帧 PTS。实验工具只向新建 staging 目录写入，发布前复验两原件与取消状态；它不包含 LiveViewer 覆盖原文件所需的崩溃恢复日志。下方 engine.py / engine_movie.py 中的历史 export / 其他实验函数为了完整依赖而原样收录；07 / 08 / 09 的实际出口是 engine_round2.Session.export，不是基类旧 export。

09 接入 LiveViewer：计划与尚待定案部分

用户已授权接入已验收 09，并删除覆盖确认中的原句“覆盖源文件可能会造成 Live Photos 无法导入手机”。这里记载的是集成计划，不宣称本 HTML 对应的独立实验已包含产品改动或完成产品回归。

  1. 将已验收的精确图片元信息与原机 marker 流程接入产品 staging 阶段；保留原件指纹、同 ID / 单 marker / 精确时间 / 编码数据校验，以及取消和外部改动保护。
  2. 保留 LiveViewer 现有两文件保存事务和恢复日志。独立实验的新目录 rename 不能直接替代产品 overwrite 的备份、回滚与崩溃恢复。
  3. 输出格式已确认：用户在 JPG→HEIC 专项手机验收通过后，确认产品统一采用 09，所有可编辑输入均保存为 HEIC，并为新图和 MOV 使用新的共同 UUID。创建副本保留全部原件；覆盖成功后删除同目录全部同名 JPG/JPEG，仅保留 HEIC + MOV。转换必须真实编码，日志区分原源路径、新目标与全部待清理候选，遇到故障须恢复原组，不能仅修改后缀。
  4. 保存后根据新结果与磁盘实际存在的候选更新候选图集合、额外项、缩略图缓存和当前选择；已删除的同名 JPG/JPEG 不再显示，仍存在的其他候选保留。PNG 当前不是产品可编辑的 Live 配对路径，不能在文档中虚构已有支持。
  5. 保持原事务负例断言：两文件中途失败、取消、进程崩溃、恢复重试、同名竞争、外部修改不被覆盖。合法测试组升级为具有独立构造 Apple MakerNote 和原机 marker 的合成组；缺失真实配对前提另作清晰失败用例，不删除原断言或以 skip 代替。
  6. 按照本次已确认的 09 规则，创建副本和覆盖均生成新 UUID；源组合法旧 ID 用于确认输入配对，不能因旧 ID 不同于新 UUID 就误判源组已被修改。产品事务与界面集成仍需独立回归，实验手机验收不代替产品保存验收。
产品适配与验收边界
LiveViewer 保留原有严格封面尺寸校验，因此产品图片写入层在已验收流程上补写正确的 IFD0 ImageWidth / ImageHeight；继续保留 ExifImageWidth / ExifImageHeight、方向与实际 HEIC 解码尺寸核对。此适配没有删除旧校验，也不改 Apple MakerNote、GPS、配对或原机 marker 的约束。产品另提供最终 MOV 验证入口：ExifTool 更新 ID 后可重排 stco/co64 块地址，但音视频轨集合、其余轨道属性、逐样本编码数据与时间仍须严格一致。原机 marker 重写阶段仍使用原先的整条音视频轨字节一致检查。

因此产品接入并非逐字节照搬实验 writer。下方完整源码块来自已验收 fix1 快照，仅对本机路径作公开脱敏，不混入这些产品适配或 fix2。本次用户手机验收针对独立 09 实验输出；产品仍需完成独立保存、恢复、冻结与回导验证，不能以 demo 验收替代。

运行依赖与许可边界

运行源码需要 Windows x64 原生 Python 与相应组件；此快照使用 Python 3.12.14、Pillow 13.0.0、pi-heif 1.4.0、Tcl/Tk 8.6.12，构建工具是 PyInstaller 6.22.3。读取 / 导帧使用固定 FFmpeg 和 FFprobe，HEIC 编码使用 LiveViewer.HeicEncoder 及其 DLL，元信息写入使用 ExifTool 13.38 与完整 exiftool_files。本文件没有安装、联网下载或执行这些组件。

组件来源 / 位置许可说明

Python / Tcl / Tk上述冻结版本，随包 _internalPSF 及各自原文；包含 OpenSSL、libffi 和 Python 第三方许可。

FFmpeg / FFproberuntime/tools/ffmpeg以本包 LICENSE / PROVENANCE / SOURCES 为准；不能因项目 MIT 就把外部编码库说成 MIT。
HEIC 编码器runtime/tools/heic本地 helper + libheif / Kvazaar 及 Windows 运行库；按随包许可与 provenance 追溯。
ExifTool13.38，runtime/tools/exiftool保留原 Windows 发行包 LICENSE、说明及 Strawberry Perl 许可证压缩包；未将其重新许可为 MIT。
PyInstaller6.22.3，仅构建随包 COPYING，包含 bootloader 例外。

历史外部二进制清单及 SHA 见下方已脱敏 source-manifest 示例的 runtime 字段；其历史哈希不校验本示例。下方完整收录许可目录中的所有 UTF-8 文本；Strawberry Perl 的许可证 ZIP 仅列路径、大小与 SHA，不把压缩包或二进制塞入 HTML。完整第三方源码、构建工具链、合成媒体 fixture 和运行时二进制不在本文件中，因此不能声称仅凭本页即可重建同一 EXE。

完整本地源码与依赖模块

所有代码均全文转义显示，未裁剪或混入 fix2；本机路径已替换为明确示例，算法与断言保留。每个折叠项区分历史原文件 SHA 与当前公开正文 SHA，并列出示例来源路径与许可说明；空的 __init__.py 也保留。

  • methods.py — 唯一方法表：07 / 08 / 09、输出格式与 MOV 路线
  • engine_round2.py — 本轮真实入口 Session.export；新 UUID、两文件核对与发布
  • engine_image.py — 精确 EXIF / 完整 Apple MakerNote、ExifTool 写入与 MOV ID 修改
  • engine_movie_round2.py — 沿用原机 marker 声明、轨道属性与 edit list 的 08 / 09 改时实现
  • engine.py — 共用输入配对、精确帧读取、源指纹、输出目录和取消；旧 export 被子类覆盖
  • engine_movie.py — 共用样本 / 非 ID 元数据验证；包含本轮未选择的历史实验方法
  • core/live_photo_movie.py — 完整 ISO-BMFF / QuickTime 解析与底层重建辅助
  • core/live_photo_image.py — 共享图片 ID 读取；其旧 minimal EXIF writer 不是本轮 write_cover 路线
  • core/frame_reader.py — FFprobe 精确 PTS 与 FFmpeg 实际所选帧导出
  • core/frame_tools.py — 定位固定 FFmpeg / FFprobe 工具
  • core/heic_encoder.py — 调用独立 HEIC 编码器，提供真实 HEIC 封面
  • core/imaging.py — Pillow / pi-heif 图片读回与方向 / 色彩处理辅助
  • core/__init__.py — core 包标识（原文件为空）
  • lab_app.py — Tk 界面 / 后台处理入口；依可执行文件名选择实验方法

唯一方法表：07 / 08 / 09、输出格式与 MOV 路线

来源

历史原文件 SHA-256（脱敏前；不是下方公开正文哈希）

当前公开正文 UTF-8 SHA-256：df61f166c8790beccbf25d93fafb5ab4386a35a277ae5f0bdf1038985fcbee7d · 1384 字节（HTML 解码后，含正文换行）。

"""Fixed, separately launched experiments; no LiveViewer integration."""
from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Method:
    code: str
    executable: str
    title: str
    movie_method: str
    image_format: str
    description: str


METHODS = (
    Method('07', '07_保留原视频时间_JPG', '07 · 保留原视频时间 / JPG 对照', 'keep_timing', 'JPEG',
           '新 JPG 保留原机完整 Apple 信息；视频保留原轨道和旧关键帧时间，只换配对标识。\n这是定位原因的对照，不保证 iPhone 使用新的关键帧时间。'),
    Method('08', '08_原机时间轨_JPG', '08 · 沿用原机时间轨 / JPG', 'original_marker', 'JPEG',
           '新 JPG 保留原机完整 Apple 信息，沿用原机时间轨结构，把标记移到所选帧。\n使用新的配对标识，不覆盖原文件；待爱思导入验证。'),
    Method('09', '09_原机时间轨_HEIC', '09 · 沿用原机时间轨 / HEIC', 'original_marker', 'HEIC',
           '与 08 使用相同的视频处理方法，新封面改用 HEIC，保留完整 Apple 信息。\n用于比较 JPG 与 HEIC 容器；不覆盖原文件。'),
)


def get_method(value):
    for method in METHODS:
        if value in (method.code, method.executable):
            return method
    raise ValueError('请使用工具包中原名为 07、08 或 09 的程序。')

"""Complete source Apple metadata and source-native marker experiments."""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from fractions import Fraction
import json
from pathlib import Path
import uuid

from core.live_photo_movie import inspect_live_photo_movie, media_payload_digests
from core.live_photo_image import read_content_identifier
from engine import Session as BaseSession, check_output_directory, sha256, runtime_root, tools
from engine_image import write_cover, copy_movie_with_identifier, set_movie_identifier
from engine_movie_round2 import rewrite_original_marker
from methods import Method

VERSION = '2.0-experiment.2'


class Session(BaseSession):
    def export(self, index: int, method: Method, parent: Path):
        if type(index) is not int or not 0 <= index < self.metadata.frame_count:
            raise ValueError('所选帧编号无效。')
        if method.code not in ('07', '08', '09'):
            raise ValueError('此轮仅支持 07、08、09 方法。')
        self.validate(full=True)
        when = self.metadata.frames[index].presentation_time
        if not isinstance(when, Fraction):
            raise ValueError('所选帧没有精确时间。')
        parent = check_output_directory(parent)
        identifier = str(uuid.uuid4()).upper()
        folder_name = f'{method.code}_{self.image.stem}_{datetime.now():%Y%m%d-%H%M%S}_{identifier[:8]}'
        destination = parent / folder_name
        stage = parent / ('未完成_' + folder_name)
        stage.mkdir()
        image_name = self.image.stem + ('.HEIC' if method.image_format == 'HEIC' else '.JPG')
        output_image, output_movie = stage / image_name, stage / (self.image.stem + '.MOV')
        try:
            pixels = self.reader.export(index)
            image_report = write_cover(self.image, pixels, output_image, identifier,
                                       method.image_format, cancel_event=self.cancel)
            if method.movie_method == 'keep_timing':
                movie_report = copy_movie_with_identifier(self.movie, output_movie, identifier,
                                                         cancel_event=self.cancel)
                expected_time = self.movie_info.photo_time
                timing_updated = False
            else:
                timing_report = rewrite_original_marker(self.movie, output_movie, when,
                                                        cancel_event=self.cancel)
                identifier_report = set_movie_identifier(output_movie, identifier, cancel_event=self.cancel)
                movie_report = {'schema': 'live-photo-method-lab.round2-movie-stages.v1',
                                'timing_rewrite_before_identifier_update': timing_report,
                                'identifier_update': identifier_report,
                                'final_asset_identifier': identifier}
                expected_time = when
                timing_updated = True
            check = inspect_live_photo_movie(output_movie, cancel_event=self.cancel)
            if (not check.supported or check.marker_count != 1 or check.asset_identifier != identifier
                    or check.photo_time != expected_time or read_content_identifier(output_image) != identifier):
                raise ValueError('结果的配对标识或关键帧时间不符合所选方法，未发布。')
            before = [(kind, value) for _, kind, value in media_payload_digests(self.movie, cancel_event=self.cancel)]
            after = [(kind, value) for _, kind, value in media_payload_digests(output_movie, cancel_event=self.cancel)]
            if before != after:
                raise ValueError('原音视频编码数据被改变，未发布。')
            self.validate(full=True)
            if self.cancel.is_set():
                raise InterruptedError('已取消。')
            report = {
                'schema': 'live-photo-method-lab.round2-result.v1', 'lab_version': VERSION,
                'method': method.code, 'title': method.title, 'image_format': method.image_format,
                'source_image': str(self.image), 'source_movie': str(self.movie),
                'source_sha256': {str(path): value for path, value in self.hashes.items()},
                'sources_unchanged': True, 'selected_frame_zero_based': index,
                'selected_time': str(when), 'selected_seconds': float(when),
                'output_marker_time': str(check.photo_time), 'timing_updated': timing_updated,
                'identifier': identifier, 'image': image_report, 'movie': movie_report,
                'encoded_audio_video_payloads_unchanged': True,
                'output_sha256': {output_image.name: sha256(output_image, self.cancel),
                                  output_movie.name: sha256(output_movie, self.cancel)},
                'iphone_import': '尚未测试；本地校验不能替代爱思与 iPhone 导入',
            }
            (stage / '处理记录.json').write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf8')
            summary = (f'{method.title}\n\n{method.description}\n\n'
                       f'所选第 {index + 1} 帧：{float(when):.6f} 秒。视频实际标记：{float(check.photo_time):.6f} 秒。\n'
                       '原文件未修改。请仅用爱思“导入实况照片”导入本文件夹中的一组图片和 MOV。\n'
                       '不要同时选择原组、上一轮失败结果或其他方法的结果。\n'
                       '请反馈：方法编号、是否导入为一个 LIVE、长按播放、新封面和声音是否正常。\n'
                       '原机 MakerNote 的非关联字段保持原值，它们是源拍摄记录，不是本次视频帧的新测量。\n')
            (stage / '本组说明.txt').write_text(summary, encoding='utf-8-sig')
            self.validate()
            if self.cancel.is_set():
                raise InterruptedError('已取消。')
            stage.rename(destination)
            return destination, report
        except BaseException as error:
            for created in (output_image, output_movie):
                if created.is_file():
                    created.unlink()
            (stage / '失败记录.txt').write_text(str(error), encoding='utf-8-sig')
            raise

精确 EXIF / 完整 Apple MakerNote、ExifTool 写入与 MOV ID 修改

当前公开正文 UTF-8 SHA-256：c76e180e455d9b71ab8e1c57dcd4843ef0b527091a43600ba97b9a2f12070650 · 27410 字节（HTML 解码后，含正文换行）。

"""Independent ExifTool metadata writer for new experiment resources only.

Apple MakerNotes must already exist: ExifTool copies the complete block, then
edits ContentIdentifier in a second invocation. Every other typed field is
compared byte-for-byte; legal offset/alignment changes are reported separately.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
import hashlib
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import shutil
import stat
import struct
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time

from PIL import Image
from core import imaging as _imaging
from core.heic_encoder import encode_heic
from core import live_photo_movie as movie

_SIZES = {1: 1, 2: 1, 3: 2, 4: 4, 5: 8, 6: 1, 7: 1, 8: 2, 9: 4,
          10: 8, 11: 4, 12: 8, 13: 4, 16: 8, 17: 8, 18: 8}
_CAPTURE = ('IFD0:Make', 'IFD0:Model', 'ExifIFD:DateTimeOriginal', 'ExifIFD:CreateDate',
            'ExifIFD:OffsetTimeOriginal', 'ExifIFD:OffsetTimeDigitized',
            'ExifIFD:SubSecTimeOriginal', 'ExifIFD:SubSecTimeDigitized', 'GPS:all')
_CAPTURE_IFD0 = {0x10f: 'Make', 0x110: 'Model'}
_CAPTURE_EXIF = {0x9003: 'DateTimeOriginal', 0x9004: 'CreateDate',
                 0x9011: 'OffsetTimeOriginal', 0x9012: 'OffsetTimeDigitized',
                 0x9291: 'SubSecTimeOriginal', 0x9292: 'SubSecTimeDigitized'}
_GPS_NAMES = ('GPSVersionID', 'GPSLatitudeRef', 'GPSLatitude', 'GPSLongitudeRef',
    'GPSLongitude', 'GPSAltitudeRef', 'GPSAltitude', 'GPSTimeStamp', 'GPSSatellites',
    'GPSStatus', 'GPSMeasureMode', 'GPSDOP', 'GPSSpeedRef', 'GPSSpeed', 'GPSTrackRef',
    'GPSTrack', 'GPSImgDirectionRef', 'GPSImgDirection', 'GPSMapDatum',
    'GPSDestLatitudeRef', 'GPSDestLatitude', 'GPSDestLongitudeRef', 'GPSDestLongitude',
    'GPSDestBearingRef', 'GPSDestBearing', 'GPSDestDistanceRef', 'GPSDestDistance',
    'GPSProcessingMethod', 'GPSAreaInformation', 'GPSDateStamp', 'GPSDifferential', 'GPSHPositioningError')


def _cancel(event):
    if event is not None and event.is_set():
        raise InterruptedError('已取消。')


def _tool():
    base = Path(sys._MEIPASS) if getattr(sys, 'frozen', False) else Path(__file__).parent / 'runtime'
    path = base / 'tools/exiftool/exiftool.exe'
    if not path.is_file():
        raise OSError('ExifTool 组件缺失，请完整解压实验工具。')
    return path


def _run(arguments, cancel_event=None):
    _cancel(cancel_event)
    arguments = [str(value) for value in arguments]
    if any(any(character in value for character in ('\r', '\n', '\0')) for value in arguments):
        raise ValueError('ExifTool 参数不能包含换行或 NUL 字符。')
    argument_bytes = ('\n'.join(arguments) + '\n').encode('utf8')
    env = os.environ.copy()
    # The bundled Windows Perl does not provide the POSIX C.UTF-8 locale.
    env.update(LC_ALL='C', LC_CTYPE='C', LANG='C')
    # Windows Perl's command-line decoding loses characters outside the ACP.
    # Keep its bootstrap arguments ASCII and send actual arguments as UTF-8.
    process = subprocess.Popen([str(_tool()), '-charset', 'filename=UTF8', '-@', '-'],
        stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, env=env,
        creationflags=subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW if os.name == 'nt' else 0)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + 120
    first_communication = True
    try:
        while True:
            _cancel(cancel_event)
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                raise TimeoutError('ExifTool 元数据处理超时。')
            try:
                stdout, stderr = process.communicate(
                    input=argument_bytes if first_communication else None, timeout=.1)
                break
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                first_communication = False
        _cancel(cancel_event)
        if process.returncode:
            raise OSError('ExifTool 处理失败：' + stderr.decode('utf8', 'replace')[-2000:])
        if len(stdout) > 4 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise ValueError('元数据超过本实验的读取范围。')
        # No -m / ignore-minor-errors: corrupt MakerNotes must fail closed.
        if stderr.strip():
            raise ValueError('ExifTool 未能无警告完成：' + stderr.decode('utf8', 'replace')[-2000:])
        return stdout
    finally:
        if process.poll() is None:
            process.kill()
            process.communicate()


def _path(path, *, existing=True):
    path = Path(os.path.abspath(path))
    for parent in ((path,) if path.exists() else ()) + tuple(path.parents):
        info = parent.lstat()
        if stat.S_ISLNK(info.st_mode) or getattr(info, 'st_file_attributes', 0) & 0x400:
            raise ValueError('实验资源暂不支持链接或重解析路径。')
    if existing and not path.is_file():
        raise ValueError('源资源不存在。')
    return path


def _identifier(value):
    if not isinstance(value, str) or re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-fA-F]{8}(?:-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}){3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}', value) is None:
        raise ValueError('新配对标识必须是 36 字符 UUID。')
    return value


def _signature(path):
    value = path.stat()
    return value.st_dev, value.st_ino, value.st_size, value.st_mtime_ns, value.st_ctime_ns


def _sha(path, event=None):
    state = _signature(path)
    value = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open('rb') as stream:
        while block := stream.read(1024 * 1024):
            _cancel(event)
            value.update(block)
    _cancel(event)
    if _signature(path) != state:
        raise OSError('源资源在读取期间发生变化。')
    return value.hexdigest()


def _same(path, state, digest, event):
    if _signature(path) != state or _sha(path, event) != digest or _signature(path) != state:
        raise OSError('源资源已变化，实验已停止。')


def _note(data):
    if not 20 <= len(data) <= 1024 * 1024 or data[:10] != b'Apple iOS\0' or data[12:14] not in (b'MM', b'II'):
        raise ValueError('源图片没有受支持的完整 Apple MakerNotes。')
    endian = '>' if data[12:14] == b'MM' else '<'
    count = struct.unpack_from(endian + 'H', data, 14)[0]
    end = 16 + 12 * count
    if not 1 <= count <= 4096 or end + 4 > len(data) or struct.unpack_from(endian + 'I', data, end)[0]:
        raise ValueError('Apple MakerNotes 目录长度或后续 IFD 不受支持。')
    fields, locations = {}, {}
    for offset in range(16, end, 12):
        tag, kind, amount, pointer = struct.unpack_from(endian + 'HHII', data, offset)
        if tag in fields or kind not in _SIZES or amount > 1024 * 1024:
            raise ValueError('Apple MakerNotes 含重复或不支持的字段。')
        size = amount * _SIZES[kind]
        start = offset + 8 if size <= 4 else pointer
        if (size > 4 and start < end + 4) or start + size > len(data):
            raise ValueError('Apple MakerNotes 字段引用越界。')
        fields[tag] = (kind, amount, data[start:start + size])
        locations[tag] = (start, size)
    if 17 not in fields or fields[17][0] != 2 or not fields[17][2].endswith(b'\0'):
        raise ValueError('源 Apple ContentIdentifier 缺失或格式不受支持。')
    return data[:14], fields, locations


def _read_exif(path, event):
    _cancel(event)
    with Image.open(path) as image:
        if image.format not in ('JPEG', 'HEIF'):
            raise ValueError('源图片仅支持 JPEG 或 HEIC。')
        raw = image.info.get('exif', b'')
        if image.format == 'HEIF':
            from pi_heif import open_heif
            raw = open_heif(path).info.get('exif') or b''
    if not isinstance(raw, bytes) or len(raw) > 1024 * 1024:
        raise ValueError('源图片 EXIF 缺失或超出读取范围。')
    if not raw:
        raise ValueError('源图片 EXIF 缺失。')
    _cancel(event)
    return raw


def _read_note(path, event):
    exif = Image.Exif()
    exif.load(_read_exif(path, event))
    data = exif.get_ifd(0x8769).get(0x927c, b'')
    if not isinstance(data, bytes):
        raise ValueError('Apple MakerNotes 原始字节无效。')
    _cancel(event)
    return data, _note(data)


def _capture_raw(path, event):
    """Read only bounded classic TIFF capture fields, without numeric conversion."""
    raw = _read_exif(path, event)
    data = raw[6:] if raw.startswith(b'Exif\0\0') else raw
    if len(data) < 8 or data[:2] not in (b'II', b'MM'):
        raise ValueError('不支持此 EXIF TIFF 字节序或头。')
    endian = '<' if data[:2] == b'II' else '>'
    if struct.unpack_from(endian + 'H', data, 2)[0] != 42:
        raise ValueError('仅支持标准 TIFF EXIF，不支持 BigTIFF。')
    seen, directory_ranges, value_ranges = set(), [], []
    payload_total = 0

    def directory(start, group):
        nonlocal payload_total
        if start in seen or not 8 <= start <= len(data) - 2:
            raise ValueError(f'{group} EXIF 目录引用重复、循环或越界。')
        seen.add(start)
        count = struct.unpack_from(endian + 'H', data, start)[0]
        end = start + 2 + count * 12
        if count > 4096 or end + 4 > len(data):
            raise ValueError(f'{group} EXIF 目录长度无效。')
        if any(start < stop and end + 4 > begin for begin, stop in directory_ranges):
            raise ValueError(f'{group} EXIF 目录范围重叠。')
        directory_ranges.append((start, end + 4))
        fields = {}
        for offset in range(start + 2, end, 12):
            tag, kind, amount, pointer = struct.unpack_from(endian + 'HHII', data, offset)
            if tag in fields or kind not in _SIZES or amount > 1024 * 1024:
                raise ValueError(f'{group}:0x{tag:04x} 字段重复、类型或数量不受支持。')
            size = amount * _SIZES[kind]
            payload_total += size
            if payload_total > 4 * 1024 * 1024:
                raise ValueError('EXIF 字段累计读取长度超过本实验范围。')
            location = offset + 8 if size <= 4 else pointer
            if location < 8 or location + size > len(data):
                raise ValueError(f'{group}:0x{tag:04x} 字段引用越界。')
            if size > 4:
                value_ranges.append((location, location + size, group, tag))
            fields[tag] = (kind, amount, data[location:location + size])
        next_ifd = struct.unpack_from(endian + 'I', data, end)[0]
        if group != 'IFD0' and next_ifd:
            raise ValueError(f'{group} 后续 EXIF 目录不受支持。')
        return fields

    root = directory(struct.unpack_from(endian + 'I', data, 4)[0], 'IFD0')

    def child(tag, group):
        if tag not in root:
            return {}
        kind, count, payload = root[tag]
        if kind != 4 or count != 1:
            raise ValueError(f'{group} EXIF 目录指针类型无效。')
        return directory(struct.unpack(endian + 'I', payload)[0], group)

    exif, gps = child(0x8769, 'ExifIFD'), child(0x8825, 'GPS')
    for start, end, group, tag in value_ranges:
        if any(start < stop and end > begin for begin, stop in directory_ranges):
            raise ValueError(f'{group}:0x{tag:04x} 字段覆盖 EXIF 目录。')
    for tag, (kind, count, payload) in gps.items():
        if kind in (13, 18):
            raise ValueError(f'GPS:0x{tag:04x} 指针类型不支持精确复制。')
        if kind in (5, 10):
            code = 'I' if kind == 5 else 'i'
            if any(struct.unpack_from(endian + code, payload, n + 4)[0] == 0
                   for n in range(0, len(payload), 8)):
                raise ValueError(f'GPS:0x{tag:04x} 有理数分母为零。')
    selected = {'IFD0': {tag: root[tag] for tag in _CAPTURE_IFD0 if tag in root},
                'ExifIFD': {tag: exif[tag] for tag in _CAPTURE_EXIF if tag in exif}, 'GPS': gps}
    for group in ('IFD0', 'ExifIFD'):
        for tag, (kind, count, payload) in selected[group].items():
            if kind != 2 or not count or not payload.endswith(b'\0'):
                raise ValueError(f'{group}:0x{tag:04x} 拍摄文字字段类型无效。')
    return {'endian': endian, **selected}


def _capture_block(capture, note):
    """Build a minimal EXIF block, preserving selected typed values exactly."""
    endian = capture['endian']
    groups = {group: dict(capture[group]) for group in ('IFD0', 'ExifIFD', 'GPS')}
    groups['ExifIFD'][0x927c] = (7, len(note), note)
    groups['IFD0'][0x8769] = (4, 1, b'\0' * 4)
    if groups['GPS']:
        groups['IFD0'][0x8825] = (4, 1, b'\0' * 4)
    order = ['IFD0', 'ExifIFD'] + (['GPS'] if groups['GPS'] else [])
    offsets, position = {}, 8
    for group in order:
        offsets[group] = position
        position += 2 + len(groups[group]) * 12 + 4
    groups['IFD0'][0x8769] = (4, 1, struct.pack(endian + 'I', offsets['ExifIFD']))
    if groups['GPS']:
        groups['IFD0'][0x8825] = (4, 1, struct.pack(endian + 'I', offsets['GPS']))
    total_size = position
    for group in order:
        for kind, count, payload in groups[group].values():
            if kind not in _SIZES or len(payload) != count * _SIZES[kind]:
                raise ValueError('EXIF 字段长度不一致。')
            if len(payload) > 4:
                total_size += total_size % 2 + len(payload)
            if total_size > 65527:
                raise ValueError('精确 EXIF 块超过本实验可写入 JPEG APP1 的范围。')
    content = bytearray((b'II' if endian == '<' else b'MM') + struct.pack(endian + 'HI', 42, 8))
    content += b'\0' * (position - len(content))
    for group in order:
        offset = offsets[group]
        struct.pack_into(endian + 'H', content, offset, len(groups[group]))
        for index, (tag, (kind, count, payload)) in enumerate(sorted(groups[group].items())):
            if len(payload) != count * _SIZES[kind]:
                raise ValueError('EXIF 字段长度不一致。')
            if len(payload) <= 4:
                value = payload.ljust(4, b'\0')
            else:
                if len(content) % 2:
                    content += b'\0'
                value = struct.pack(endian + 'I', len(content))
                content += payload
            entry = offset + 2 + index * 12
            content[entry:entry + 12] = struct.pack(endian + 'HHI', tag, kind, count) + value
    if len(content) > 65527:
        raise ValueError('精确 EXIF 块超过本实验可写入 JPEG APP1 的范围。')
    return bytes(content)


def _capture_name(group, tag):
    names = _CAPTURE_IFD0 if group == 'IFD0' else _CAPTURE_EXIF if group == 'ExifIFD' else dict(enumerate(_GPS_NAMES))
    return f'{group}:{names.get(tag, f"0x{tag:04x}")}'


def _verify_capture_raw(before, after):
    if before['endian'] != after['endian']:
        raise ValueError('EXIF 原始字节序未保留。')
    changes = []
    for group in ('IFD0', 'ExifIFD', 'GPS'):
        old, new = before[group], after[group]
        for tag in sorted(old.keys() | new.keys()):
            if old.get(tag) != new.get(tag):
                changes.append(_capture_name(group, tag))
    if changes:
        raise ValueError('拍摄元信息未精确保留：' + '、'.join(changes))


def _capture(path, event):
    import json
    result = json.loads(_run(['-j', '-a', '-G1', '-s', '-n', *('-' + key for key in _CAPTURE), path], event))[0]
    return {key: value for key, value in result.items() if key != 'SourceFile'}


def _verify_note(before, after, identifier):
    original_raw, (original_head, original_fields, original_locations) = before
    output_raw, (output_head, output_fields, output_locations) = after
    if original_head != output_head or original_fields.keys() != output_fields.keys():
        raise ValueError('ExifTool 改变了 Apple MakerNotes 前缀或字段集合。')
    if any(original_fields[key] != output_fields[key] for key in original_fields if key != 17):
        raise ValueError('ExifTool 改变了非配对标识的 MakerNote 字段，实验已停止。')
    wanted = identifier.encode('ascii') + b'\0'
    if output_fields[17] != (2, len(wanted), wanted):
        raise ValueError('新 Apple ContentIdentifier 未写入或读回不一致。')
    original_mask, output_mask = bytearray(original_raw), bytearray(output_raw)
    for content, locations in ((original_mask, original_locations), (output_mask, output_locations)):
        start, size = locations[17]
        content[start:start + size] = bytes(size)
    return {'typed_all_nonid_values_equal': True,
            'physical_equal_except_identifier': original_mask == output_mask,
            'source_makernote_size': len(original_raw), 'output_makernote_size': len(output_raw),
            'source_makernote_sha256': hashlib.sha256(original_raw).hexdigest(),
            'output_makernote_sha256': hashlib.sha256(output_raw).hexdigest(),
            'preserved_nonid_fields': [{'tag': tag, 'type': item[0], 'count': item[1],
                'value_sha256': hashlib.sha256(item[2]).hexdigest()}
                for tag, item in original_fields.items() if tag != 17]}


def _copy_exclusive(source, destination, event):
    created = False
    try:
        with destination.open('xb') as target:
            created = True
            with source.open('rb') as original:
                while block := original.read(1024 * 1024):
                    _cancel(event)
                    target.write(block)
            target.flush()
        _cancel(event)
    except BaseException:
        if created:
            destination.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        raise


def write_cover(source_image, pixels, destination, identifier, image_format, cancel_event=None):
    identifier = _identifier(identifier)
    source, destination = _path(source_image), _path(destination, existing=False)
    if destination.exists() or source == destination:
        raise FileExistsError('只允许新建实验封面，不能覆盖现有资源。')
    if image_format not in ('JPEG', 'HEIC') or pixels.width < 1 or pixels.height < 1:
        raise ValueError('封面格式或尺寸无效。')
    source_state, source_hash = _signature(source), _sha(source, cancel_event)
    original_note, original_capture = _read_note(source, cancel_event), _capture(source, cancel_event)
    original_raw_capture = _capture_raw(source, cancel_event)
    created = False
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='._lpimage-', dir=destination.parent) as directory:
        work = Path(directory)
        extension = '.jpg' if image_format == 'JPEG' else '.heic'
        bare, copied, final = (work / (name + extension) for name in ('pixels', 'copied', 'final'))
        try:
            rgb = pixels.convert('RGB')
            if image_format == 'JPEG':
                rgb.save(bare, 'JPEG', quality=100, subsampling=0)
            else:
                encode_heic(rgb, bare, cancel_event=cancel_event)
            # Per-tag GPS copies pass through floating point/PrintConv and can
            # also add mandatory tags missing in the source. Inject only the
            # selected raw fields and complete MakerNote as a bounded block.
            metadata = work / 'capture.exif'
            metadata.write_bytes(_capture_block(original_raw_capture, original_note[0]))
            _run(['-EXIF<=' + str(metadata), '-o', copied, bare], cancel_event)
            _verify_capture_raw(original_raw_capture, _capture_raw(copied, cancel_event))
            now = datetime.now().astimezone()
            zone = now.strftime('%z')
            zone = zone[:3] + ':' + zone[3:]
            # Copy and ID editing require two invocations: a combined command
            # can silently leave the old ID despite exit code zero.
            _run(['-Apple:ContentIdentifier=' + identifier, '-IFD0:Orientation#=1',
                  f'-ExifIFD:ExifImageWidth={rgb.width}', f'-ExifIFD:ExifImageHeight={rgb.height}',
                  '-IFD0:ModifyDate=' + now.strftime('%Y:%m:%d %H:%M:%S'),
                  '-ExifIFD:OffsetTime=' + zone, '-o', final, copied], cancel_event)
            verification = _verify_note(original_note, _read_note(final, cancel_event), identifier)
            _verify_capture_raw(original_raw_capture, _capture_raw(final, cancel_event))
            final_capture = _capture(final, cancel_event)
            if final_capture != original_capture:
                names = sorted(key for key in original_capture.keys() | final_capture.keys()
                               if original_capture.get(key) != final_capture.get(key))
                raise ValueError('拍摄元信息独立读回不一致：' + '、'.join(names))
            with Image.open(bare) as encoded, Image.open(final) as decoded:
                encoded.load(); decoded.load()
                if (decoded.format != ('JPEG' if image_format == 'JPEG' else 'HEIF')
                        or decoded.size != rgb.size or decoded.convert('RGB').tobytes() != encoded.convert('RGB').tobytes()):
                    raise ValueError('ExifTool 改变了新封面的像素、尺寸或格式。')
            import json
            properties = json.loads(_run(['-j', '-n', '-Orientation', '-ExifImageWidth', '-ExifImageHeight',
                                          '-Apple:ContentIdentifier', final], cancel_event))[0]
            if (properties.get('Orientation'), properties.get('ExifImageWidth'), properties.get('ExifImageHeight')) != (1, rgb.width, rgb.height):
                raise ValueError('新封面的方向或尺寸元数据不正确。')
            if properties.get('ContentIdentifier') != identifier:
                raise ValueError('ExifTool 独立读回的新配对标识不正确。')
            _same(source, source_state, source_hash, cancel_event)
            result_hash = _sha(final, cancel_event)
            _copy_exclusive(final, destination, cancel_event)
            created = True
            _same(source, source_state, source_hash, cancel_event)
            _cancel(cancel_event)
            return {'schema': 'live-photo-method-lab.image-exiftool.v1', 'asset_identifier': identifier,
                    'image_format': image_format, 'size': list(rgb.size), 'source_sha256': source_hash,
                    'output_sha256': result_hash, 'capture_tags_preserved': True,
                    'capture_typed_raw_fields_preserved': True,
                    'capture_field_names': [_capture_name(group, tag)
                        for group in ('IFD0', 'ExifIFD', 'GPS') for tag in sorted(original_raw_capture[group])],
                    'pixels_after_metadata_write_identical': True, **verification}
        except BaseException:
            if created:
                destination.unlink(missing_ok=True)
            raise


def _sample_digests(loaded, event):
    result = []
    with loaded.path.open('rb') as stream:
        for track in loaded.tracks:
            samples = []
            for offset, size in track.samples:
                _cancel(event)
                stream.seek(offset)
                data = stream.read(size)
                if len(data) != size:
                    raise ValueError('MOV 样本读取截断。')
                samples.append((size, hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()))
            result.append((track.identifier, track.handler, track.scale, track.duration,
                           track.times, track.visible_times, samples))
    return result


def _track_structure_without_offsets(track):
    mdia = movie._one(movie._children(track.box), b'mdia')
    minf = movie._one(movie._children(mdia), b'minf')
    stbl = movie._atom(b'stbl', b''.join(box.data for box in movie._children(track.stbl)
                                       if box.kind not in (b'stco', b'co64')))
    return movie._replace(track.box, {b'mdia': movie._replace(mdia, {b'minf': movie._replace(minf, {b'stbl': stbl})})})


def _movie_edit(source, output, identifier, event):
    from engine_movie import _global_non_identifier_fields
    before = movie._load(source, event)
    state, source_hash = _signature(source), _sha(source, event)
    old_info = movie._info(before)
    sample_state = _sample_digests(before, event)
    _run(['-Keys:ContentIdentifier=' + identifier, '-o', output, source], event)
    after = movie._load(output, event)
    new_info = movie._info(after)
    if new_info.asset_identifier != identifier:
        raise ValueError('MOV 配对标识读回不一致。')
    if (before.scale, before.duration, old_info.photo_time, old_info.marker_count) != (
            after.scale, after.duration, new_info.photo_time, new_info.marker_count):
        raise ValueError('ExifTool 改变了 MOV 时间或封面标记。')
    if sample_state != _sample_digests(after, event):
        raise ValueError('ExifTool 改变或丢失了原 MOV 轨道样本及时间。')
    if [_track_structure_without_offsets(track) for track in before.tracks] != [
            _track_structure_without_offsets(track) for track in after.tracks]:
        raise ValueError('ExifTool 改变了原 MOV 轨道属性。')
    if _global_non_identifier_fields(before) != _global_non_identifier_fields(after):
        raise ValueError('ExifTool 改变了非配对的全局 MOV 元数据。')
    _same(source, state, source_hash, event)
    return {'schema': 'live-photo-method-lab.movie-exiftool-id.v1', 'asset_identifier': identifier,
            'source_sha256': source_hash, 'output_sha256': _sha(output, event),
            'marker_time': str(new_info.photo_time) if new_info.photo_time is not None else None,
            'marker_count': new_info.marker_count, 'track_count': len(after.tracks),
            'all_track_samples_and_times_preserved': True, 'track_properties_except_chunk_offsets_preserved': True,
            'non_identifier_global_metadata_preserved': True}


def copy_movie_with_identifier(source, destination, identifier, cancel_event=None):
    identifier = _identifier(identifier)
    source, destination = _path(source), _path(destination, existing=False)
    if destination.exists() or source == destination:
        raise FileExistsError('只允许新建 MOV 副本。')
    state, source_hash = _signature(source), _sha(source, cancel_event)
    created = False
    try:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='._lpmovie-', dir=destination.parent) as directory:
            final = Path(directory) / 'final.mov'
            report = _movie_edit(source, final, identifier, cancel_event)
            _same(source, state, source_hash, cancel_event)
            _copy_exclusive(final, destination, cancel_event)
            created = True
            _same(source, state, source_hash, cancel_event)
            _cancel(cancel_event)
            return report
    except BaseException:
        if created:
            destination.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        raise


def set_movie_identifier(new_existing_staged_movie, identifier, cancel_event=None):
    """Only call on the caller's newly generated private stage, never an input."""
    identifier = _identifier(identifier)
    staged = _path(new_existing_staged_movie)
    state, source_hash = _signature(staged), _sha(staged, cancel_event)
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='._lpmovie-', dir=staged.parent) as directory:
        final = Path(directory) / 'final.mov'
        report = _movie_edit(staged, final, identifier, cancel_event)
        _same(staged, state, source_hash, cancel_event)
        _cancel(cancel_event)
        os.replace(final, staged)
        return report

"""Experiment: move the marker using the original track's native shell.

No media encoder, new handler template, or identifier writer is used here.
The original ID is retained for a separate, independently checked ID edit.
This is an import experiment, not a statement of Apple/i4 compatibility.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from fractions import Fraction
import os
from pathlib import Path
import struct

from core import live_photo_movie as m
from engine_movie import _fraction, _hash, _verify_preserved


def _field(data, offset, width, value):
    m._check(0 <= value < 1 << (8 * width) and offset + width <= len(data),
             "原轨道时间字段不能精确容纳新值。")
    changed = bytearray(data)
    changed[offset:offset + width] = value.to_bytes(width, 'big')
    return bytes(changed)


def _marker_sample(track):
    deltas = [delta for count, delta in m._table(m._one(m._children(track.stbl), b'stts'), 2)
              for _ in range(count)]
    found = []
    for index, payload in enumerate(track.metadata_samples):
        atoms = [box for box in m._boxes(payload) if box.kind in track.marker_ids]
        if atoms:
            m._check(len(atoms) == 1 and atoms[0].payload == b'\xff',
                     "原标记必须是唯一 int8 -1 样本。")
            found.append((index, atoms[0].data, deltas[index]))
    m._check(len(found) == 1, "原轨道缺少唯一有效标记。")
    return found[0]


def _edit(track, delay, duration):
    old = m._one(m._children(track.box), b'edts', optional=True)
    version = 0
    if old:
        children = m._children(old)
        m._check(len(children) == 1 and children[0].kind == b'elst',
                 "原编辑列表含未知扩展，不能安全调整。")
        version = children[0].payload[0]
    m._check(version in (0, 1), "编辑列表版本不支持。")
    edits = ([(delay, -1)] if delay else []) + [(duration, 0)]
    width = 64 if version else 32
    m._check(all(0 < length < 1 << width for length, _ in edits), "编辑列表时长超出原版本范围。")
    content = m._pack(len(edits)) + b''.join(
        struct.pack('>Qqhh' if version else '>Iihh', length, media_start, 1, 0)
        for length, media_start in edits)
    return m._atom(b'edts', m._full(b'elst', content, version=version))


def _clone_marker(track, identifier, loaded, when, offset, marker, delta):
    """Retain shell and original marker declaration; replace only time/index data."""
    movie_delay = when * loaded.scale
    m._check(movie_delay.denominator == 1,
             "所选精确 PTS 不能由原 movie 时钟表示，拒绝近似。")
    delay = int(movie_delay)
    duration = Fraction(delta, track.scale)
    # The sample duration/media scale stay exact. The edit envelope uses movie
    # ticks and may extend to the next tick, as track durations require.
    edit_ticks = (delta * loaded.scale + track.scale - 1) // track.scale
    m._check(when + duration <= Fraction(loaded.duration, loaded.scale)
             and delay + edit_ticks <= loaded.duration, "原标记持续时间在新位置超出影片结尾。")
    tc = m._children(track.box)
    tkhd = m._one(tc, b'tkhd')
    m._check(tkhd.payload[0] in (0, 1), "原 tkhd 版本不支持。")
    id_at = 20 if tkhd.payload[0] else 12
    duration_at, duration_width = (28, 8) if tkhd.payload[0] else (20, 4)
    new_tkhd = _field(tkhd.payload, id_at, 4, identifier)
    new_tkhd = _field(new_tkhd, duration_at, duration_width, delay + edit_ticks)
    mdia = m._one(tc, b'mdia')
    mdhd = m._one(m._children(mdia), b'mdhd')
    mdhd_at, mdhd_width = (24, 8) if mdhd.payload[0] else (16, 4)
    new_mdhd = _field(mdhd.payload, mdhd_at, mdhd_width, delta)
    # Preserve all sample-entry extensions, but only the existing marker key.
    entry = track.sample_entries[0]
    key_bytes = b''.join(key.data for key in track.keys if key.kind in track.marker_ids)
    m._check(key_bytes, "原标记声明不存在。")
    new_entry = m._atom(b'mebx', entry.payload[:8] + b''.join(
        m._atom(b'keys', key_bytes) if box.kind == b'keys' else box.data
        for box in m._metadata_extensions(entry)))
    original_tables = m._children(track.stbl)
    allowed = {b'stsd', b'stts', b'ctts', b'stsc', b'stsz', b'stco', b'co64'}
    m._check(all(box.kind in allowed for box in original_tables),
             "原标记轨含依赖样本编号的未知扩展，不能安全克隆。")
    co_kind = next(box.kind for box in original_tables if box.kind in (b'stco', b'co64'))
    if offset > m.U32:
        co_kind = b'co64'
    offsets = m._full(co_kind, m._pack(1) + offset.to_bytes(8 if co_kind == b'co64' else 4, 'big'))
    replacements = {
        b'stsd': m._full(b'stsd', m._pack(1) + new_entry),
        b'stts': m._full(b'stts', m._pack(1) + struct.pack('>II', 1, delta)),
        b'ctts': b'',
        b'stsc': m._full(b'stsc', m._pack(1) + struct.pack('>III', 1, 1, 1)),
        b'stsz': m._full(b'stsz', m._pack(0) + m._pack(1) + m._pack(len(marker))),
        b'stco': offsets, b'co64': offsets,
    }
    stbl = m._replace(track.stbl, replacements)
    minf = m._one(m._children(mdia), b'minf')
    new_mdia = m._replace(mdia, {
        b'mdhd': m._atom(b'mdhd', new_mdhd),
        b'minf': m._replace(minf, {b'stbl': stbl}),
    })
    edit = _edit(track, delay, edit_ticks)
    replacements = {b'tkhd': m._atom(b'tkhd', new_tkhd), b'edts': edit, b'mdia': new_mdia}
    raw = m._replace(track.box, replacements)
    if not any(box.kind == b'edts' for box in tc):
        # Add the edit directly after tkhd, leaving remaining shell ordering.
        raw = m._atom(b'trak', b''.join(replacements.get(box.kind, box.data)
                                      + (edit if box.kind == b'tkhd' else b'') for box in tc))
    return raw, duration, Fraction(edit_ticks, loaded.scale)


def _copy_with_movie(loaded, destination, rewritten, appended, tail_moov, cancel_event):
    created = False
    try:
        with loaded.path.open('rb') as incoming, destination.open('xb') as outgoing:
            created = True
            for box in loaded.top:
                m._cancel(cancel_event)
                if box.kind == b'moov' and tail_moov:
                    continue
                incoming.seek(box.start)
                remaining, first = box.size, True
                while remaining:
                    m._cancel(cancel_event)
                    data = incoming.read(min(remaining, 1024 * 1024))
                    m._check(data, "复制原 MOV 时读取截断。")
                    if first:
                        if box.kind == b'moov':
                            data = data[:4] + b'free' + data[8:]
                        elif data[:4] == b'\0' * 4:
                            data = m._pack(box.size) + data[4:]
                        first = False
                    outgoing.write(data)
                    remaining -= len(data)
            outgoing.write(m._atom(b'mdat', appended))
            outgoing.write(rewritten)
            outgoing.flush()
            os.fsync(outgoing.fileno())
        m._check(loaded.signature == m._signature(loaded.path), "保存期间原 MOV 已更改。")
    except BaseException:
        if created:
            destination.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        raise


def rewrite_original_marker(source, destination, photo_time: Fraction, cancel_event=None):
    """Create new MOV at exact PTS, preserving ID and original marker shell.

    Pure-marker tracks retain their ID. Mixed metadata keeps its old ID and
    all non-marker timing/data; a clone holding the original marker key gets
    a fresh track ID. Only an existing, uniquely supported marker is accepted.
    """
    source, destination = Path(source), Path(destination)
    m._check(os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(source)) != os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(destination))
             and not destination.exists() and not destination.is_symlink(), "输出必须是不存在的新 MOV。")
    m._check(isinstance(photo_time, Fraction), "标记时间必须为精确 Fraction。")
    loaded = m._load(source, cancel_event)
    m._check(loaded.asset_identifier, "原 MOV 缺少配对标识。")
    tracks = [track for track in loaded.tracks if track.marker_ids]
    m._check(len(tracks) == 1, "需要原 MOV 的唯一有效 still-image-time 轨道。")
    original = tracks[0]
    visible = [time for track in loaded.tracks if track.handler == b'vide' for time in track.visible_times]
    m._check(0 <= photo_time < Fraction(loaded.duration, loaded.scale)
             and visible.count(photo_time) == 1, "所选时间不是唯一有效视频呈现帧。")
    marker_index, marker, delta = _marker_sample(original)
    mixed = any(key.kind not in original.marker_ids and key.kind != b'\0' * 4 for key in original.keys)
    if not mixed:
        m._check(all(all(box.kind in original.marker_ids or box.kind == b'\0' * 4
                         for box in m._boxes(payload)) for payload in original.metadata_samples),
                 "纯标记轨包含未声明样本。")
    identifier = max(track.identifier for track in loaded.tracks) + 1 if mixed else original.identifier
    m._check(identifier < m.U32, "轨道 ID 已用尽。")
    source_hash = _hash(source, cancel_event)
    m._check(loaded.signature == m._signature(source), "处理前原 MOV 已变动。")
    # A trailing index can be replaced directly in the new file: no dead moov
    # body remains. Other layouts retain prefix offsets by replacing old moov
    # with free before appending a single active moov.
    tail_moov = loaded.top[-1].kind == b'moov'
    append_at = loaded.moov.start if tail_moov else loaded.signature[0]
    payload = bytearray()
    original_update = None
    if mixed:
        allowed = {b'stsd', b'stts', b'ctts', b'stsc', b'stsz', b'stco', b'co64'}
        m._check(all(box.kind in allowed for box in m._children(original.stbl)),
                 "原混合metadata采样扩展尚不支持。")
        original_update, stripped = m._strip_marker(original, append_at + 8)
        payload.extend(stripped)
    clone, duration, edit_duration = _clone_marker(
        original, identifier, loaded, photo_time, append_at + 8 + len(payload), marker, delta)
    payload.extend(marker)
    children = []
    for box in loaded.children:
        if box is original.box:
            children.append(original_update if mixed else clone)
        elif box.kind == b'mvhd' and mixed:
            data = box.payload
            children.append(m._atom(b'mvhd', _field(data, len(data)-4, 4,
                            max(m._u32(data, len(data)-4), identifier+1))))
        else:
            children.append(box.data)
    if mixed:
        children.append(clone)
    rewritten = m._atom(b'moov', b''.join(children))
    created = False
    try:
        _copy_with_movie(loaded, destination, rewritten, bytes(payload), tail_moov, cancel_event)
        created = True
        after = m._load(destination, cancel_event)
        info = m._info(after)
        m._check(info.asset_identifier == loaded.asset_identifier and info.photo_time == photo_time
                 and info.marker_count == 1, "新文件配对标识或精确标记时间校验失败。")
        verification = _verify_preserved(loaded, after, 'qtff', cancel_event)
        new = next(track for track in after.tracks if track.identifier == identifier)
        m._check(new.scale == original.scale and len(new.samples) == 1 and new.metadata_samples == [marker],
                 "原标记样本或时间基发生改变。")
        m._check(m._table(m._one(m._children(new.stbl), b'stts'), 2) == [(1, delta)],
                 "原标记持续时间发生改变。")
        for kind in (b'hdlr',):
            old_box = m._one(m._children(m._one(m._children(original.box), b'mdia')), kind)
            new_box = m._one(m._children(m._one(m._children(new.box), b'mdia')), kind)
            m._check(old_box.data == new_box.data, "原生 handler 未保留。")
        for kind in (b'tref',):
            old_box = m._one(m._children(original.box), kind, optional=True)
            new_box = m._one(m._children(new.box), kind, optional=True)
            m._check((old_box.data if old_box else None) == (new_box.data if new_box else None),
                     "原生轨道关联未保留。")
        m._check(m._one(m._children(original.box), b'tkhd').payload[:4]
                 == m._one(m._children(new.box), b'tkhd').payload[:4], "原 tkhd flags 未保留。")
        m._check([key.data for key in original.keys if key.kind in original.marker_ids]
                 == [key.data for key in new.keys], "原 marker key ID 或完整声明未保留。")
        output_hash = _hash(destination, cancel_event)
        m._check(loaded.signature == m._signature(source) and after.signature == m._signature(destination),
                 "最终校验期间文件已更改。")
        m._cancel(cancel_event)
        return {
            'schema': 'live-photo-method-lab.original-marker.v1',
            'method': 'original_marker_shell',
            'source_sha256': source_hash, 'output_sha256': output_hash,
            'asset_identifier': info.asset_identifier, 'identifier_changed': False,
            'source_time': _fraction(original.marker_times[0]), 'output_time': _fraction(photo_time),
            'source_marker_sample_index': marker_index,
            'marker_duration': _fraction(duration), 'edit_envelope_duration': _fraction(edit_duration),
            'marker_media_scale': original.scale, 'marker_duration_preserved': True,
            'source_marker_track': original.identifier, 'output_marker_track': identifier,
            'mixed_track_split': mixed, 'marker_count': 1, 'track_count': info.track_count,
            'native_handler_flags_tref_preserved': True,
            'marker_key_id_and_declaration_preserved': True,
            'trailing_moov_replaced_without_dead_index': tail_moov,
            'verification': verification, 'import_compatibility_verified': False,
        }
    except BaseException:
        if created:
            destination.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        raise

共用输入配对、精确帧读取、源指纹、输出目录和取消；旧 export 被子类覆盖

"""Read selected local input; publish a new, individually verified experiment pair."""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from fractions import Fraction
import hashlib
import json
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import stat
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import time
import uuid

from PIL import Image
from core.frame_reader import FrameReader
from core.live_photo_image import build_cover_exif, read_content_identifier, validate_cover_metadata
from core.live_photo_movie import inspect_live_photo_movie, media_payload_digests
from core.heic_encoder import encode_heic
from engine_movie import rewrite_movie
from methods import Method

VERSION = '1.0-experiment.1'


def runtime_root():
    if getattr(sys, 'frozen', False):
        return Path(sys._MEIPASS)
    return Path(__file__).parent / 'runtime'


def tools():
    base = runtime_root() / 'tools' / 'ffmpeg'
    result = (base / 'ffmpeg.exe', base / 'ffprobe.exe')
    if not all(path.is_file() for path in result):
        raise OSError('读取组件缺失，请将整个工具包解压后运行，保留 _internal 文件夹。')
    return result


def signature(path):
    value = path.stat()
    return value.st_dev, value.st_ino, value.st_size, value.st_mtime_ns, value.st_ctime_ns


def sha256(path, cancel=None):
    result = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open('rb') as stream:
        while chunk := stream.read(1024 * 1024):
            if cancel is not None and cancel.is_set():
                raise InterruptedError('已取消。')
            result.update(chunk)
    return result.hexdigest()


def check_path(path):
    path = Path(os.path.abspath(path))
    for part in (path, *path.parents):
        attributes = part.lstat()
        if stat.S_ISLNK(attributes.st_mode) or getattr(attributes, 'st_file_attributes', 0) & 0x400:
            raise ValueError('实验工具请使用本地普通文件夹，暂不支持链接或重解析路径。')
    if not path.is_file():
        raise ValueError('请选择本地图片或 MOV 文件。')
    return path


def check_output_directory(path):
    path = Path(os.path.abspath(path))
    for part in (path, *path.parents):
        value = part.lstat()
        if stat.S_ISLNK(value.st_mode) or getattr(value, 'st_file_attributes', 0) & 0x400:
            raise ValueError('输出位置请使用本地普通文件夹，暂不支持链接路径。')
    if not path.is_dir():
        raise ValueError('输出文件夹不存在。')
    return path


def run_ffmpeg(arguments, cancel):
    process = subprocess.Popen([str(item) for item in arguments], stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                               stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
                               creationflags=subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW if sys.platform == 'win32' else 0)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + 120
    try:
        while True:
            if cancel.is_set():
                raise InterruptedError('已取消。')
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                raise TimeoutError('MOV 重封装超时。')
            try:
                _, errors = process.communicate(timeout=.1)
                break
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                pass
        if process.returncode:
            raise OSError('MOV 重封装失败：' + errors.decode('utf8', 'replace')[-1800:])
    finally:
        if process.poll() is None:
            process.kill()
            process.communicate()


def find_pair(selected):
    selected = check_path(selected)
    suffix = selected.suffix.casefold()
    if suffix not in ('.heic', '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.mov'):
        raise ValueError('请选择 HEIC、JPG、JPEG 或 MOV。')
    candidates = [p for p in selected.parent.iterdir()
                  if p.stem.casefold() == selected.stem.casefold() and p.is_file()]
    images = [p for p in candidates if p.suffix.casefold() in ('.heic', '.jpg', '.jpeg')]
    movies = [p for p in candidates if p.suffix.casefold() == '.mov']
    if suffix != '.mov':
        images = [selected]
    if len(images) != 1 or len(movies) != 1:
        raise ValueError('需要同一文件夹内同名的一张 HEIC/JPG 和一个 MOV。若有多张同名图片，请直接选所需图片。')
    return check_path(images[0]), check_path(movies[0])


class Session:
    def __init__(self, selected, cancel=None):
        self.cancel = cancel or threading.Event()
        self.image, self.movie = find_pair(selected)
        self.signatures = {p: signature(p) for p in (self.image, self.movie)}
        self.hashes = {p: sha256(p, self.cancel) for p in self.signatures}
        self.validate()
        ffmpeg, ffprobe = tools()
        self.reader = FrameReader(self.movie, ffmpeg=ffmpeg, ffprobe=ffprobe, cancel_event=self.cancel)
        try:
            self.metadata = self.reader.open()
            if self.metadata.timestamps_estimated:
                raise ValueError('该视频无法取得精确帧时间，实验已停止。')
            self.movie_info = inspect_live_photo_movie(self.movie, cancel_event=self.cancel)
            if not self.movie_info.supported:
                raise ValueError(self.movie_info.unsupported_reason or '当前实验不支持此 MOV 结构。')
            self.image_identifier = read_content_identifier(self.image)
            if not self.image_identifier or self.image_identifier != self.movie_info.asset_identifier:
                raise ValueError('源图片与 MOV 的配对标识缺失或不一致，请选择原始有效的一组 Live Photo。')
            self.validate()
        except BaseException:
            self.reader.close()
            raise

    def validate(self, full=False):
        for path, original in self.signatures.items():
            if signature(path) != original or full and sha256(path, self.cancel) != self.hashes[path]:
                raise OSError('源文件已变化，已停止处理。请重新打开这组文件。')
        # Hashing the movie can take longer than hashing the image. Recheck both
        # identities together after the last hash, including at publication.
        if any(signature(path) != original for path, original in self.signatures.items()):
            raise OSError('源文件已变化，已停止处理。请重新打开这组文件。')

    def preview(self, index):
        self.validate()
        return self.reader.preview(index, 1000)

    def close(self):
        self.cancel.set()
        self.reader.close()

    def export(self, index: int, method: Method, parent: Path):
        if type(index) is not int or not 0 <= index < self.metadata.frame_count:
            raise ValueError('所选帧编号无效。')
        self.validate(full=True)
        frame = self.metadata.frames[index]
        when = frame.presentation_time
        if not isinstance(when, Fraction):
            raise ValueError('所选帧没有精确时间。')
        parent = check_output_directory(parent)
        identifier = str(uuid.uuid4()).upper()
        folder_name = f'{method.code}_{self.image.stem}_{datetime.now():%Y%m%d-%H%M%S}_{identifier[:8]}'
        destination = parent / folder_name
        stage = parent / ('未完成_' + folder_name)
        stage.mkdir()  # Exclusive creation; inputs and existing outputs are never overwritten.
        image_name = self.image.stem + ('.HEIC' if method.image_format == 'HEIC' else '.JPG')
        output_image, output_movie = stage / image_name, stage / (self.image.stem + '.MOV')
        remuxed = stage / '_处理中.mov'
        try:
            pixels = self.reader.export(index)
            exif = build_cover_exif(self.image, identifier, pixels.size)
            if method.image_format == 'HEIC':
                encode_heic(pixels, output_image, exif=exif, cancel_event=self.cancel)
            else:
                with output_image.open('xb') as target:
                    pixels.save(target, 'JPEG', quality=100 if method.code == '06' else 95, subsampling=0, exif=exif)
            validate_cover_metadata(output_image, identifier)
            with Image.open(output_image) as decoded:
                decoded.load()
                if decoded.size != pixels.size or decoded.format != ('HEIF' if method.image_format == 'HEIC' else 'JPEG'):
                    raise ValueError('新封面解码或尺寸校验失败。')
            if method.movie_method == 'remux_editlist':
                ffmpeg, ffprobe = tools()
                scale = math.lcm(self.metadata.time_base.denominator, when.denominator)
                if not 1 <= scale <= 0x7fffffff:
                    raise ValueError('原视频时间基超出这版 demo 的重封装范围。')
                run_ffmpeg([ffmpeg, '-nostdin', '-hide_banner', '-loglevel', 'error', '-n',
                            '-copyts', '-i', self.movie, '-map', '0:v:0', '-map', '0:a?',
                            '-map_metadata', '-1', '-map_chapters', '-1', '-c', 'copy',
                            '-avoid_negative_ts', 'disabled', '-movie_timescale', str(scale),
                            '-video_track_timescale', str(scale), '-f', 'mov', remuxed], self.cancel)
                with FrameReader(remuxed, ffmpeg=ffmpeg, ffprobe=ffprobe, cancel_event=self.cancel) as remux_reader:
                    remux_meta = remux_reader.metadata
                    if remux_meta.frame_count != self.metadata.frame_count or remux_meta.timestamps_estimated:
                        raise ValueError('重封装后的帧数或时间不符合输入，已停止。')
                    output_time = remux_meta.frames[index].presentation_time
                    if not isinstance(output_time, Fraction):
                        raise ValueError('重封装后缺少精确关键帧时间。')
                    check_pixels = remux_reader.export(index)
                    if check_pixels.size != pixels.size or check_pixels.tobytes() != pixels.tobytes():
                        raise ValueError('重封装改变了所选帧的画面或方向，已停止。')
                before_payload = [(handler, digest) for _, handler, digest in media_payload_digests(self.movie, cancel_event=self.cancel)]
                after_payload = [(handler, digest) for _, handler, digest in media_payload_digests(remuxed, cancel_event=self.cancel)]
                if before_payload != after_payload:
                    raise ValueError('重封装后的音视频编码数据与原件不一致，已停止。')
                movie_report = rewrite_movie(remuxed, output_movie, identifier, output_time,
                                             'editlist', cancel_event=self.cancel)
                movie_report['remux'] = {
                    'video_audio_stream_copy': True, 'encoded_payload_hashes_equal': True,
                    'selected_decoded_pixels_identical': True,
                    'source_selected_time': str(when), 'output_selected_time': str(output_time),
                    'source_frame_count': self.metadata.frame_count,
                    'old_auxiliary_and_metadata_tracks_carried': False,
                    'description': 'Rebuild only primary video/audio plus a new timed marker, as a standalone compatibility experiment.',
                }
                remuxed.unlink()
            else:
                movie_report = rewrite_movie(self.movie, output_movie, identifier, when,
                                             method.movie_method, cancel_event=self.cancel)
            self.validate(full=True)
            if self.cancel.is_set():
                raise InterruptedError('已取消。')
            report = {
                'schema': 'live-photo-method-lab.result.v1', 'lab_version': VERSION,
                'method': method.code, 'title': method.title, 'image_format': method.image_format,
                'source_image': str(self.image), 'source_movie': str(self.movie),
                'source_sha256': {str(p): value for p, value in self.hashes.items()},
                'sources_unchanged': True, 'selected_frame_zero_based': index,
                'selected_time': str(when), 'selected_seconds': float(when),
                'identifier': identifier, 'movie': movie_report,
                'output_sha256': {output_image.name: sha256(output_image, self.cancel), output_movie.name: sha256(output_movie, self.cancel)},
                'iphone_import': '尚未测试，需经爱思单独导入确认',
            }
            (stage / '处理记录.json').write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf8')
            summary = (f'{method.title}\n\n{method.description}\n\n所选帧：第 {index + 1} 帧，{float(when):.6f} 秒。\n'
                       '原文件未修改；视频画面和声音未重新编码。\n'
                       '请仅将此文件夹内的图片和 MOV 一起用爱思“导入实况照片”导入。\n'
                       '不要同时选中原组或其他方法的结果，以免反馈混淆。\n'
                       '请记录：是否导入为一个 LIVE、长按能否播放、关键照片是否为所选帧、声音是否正常。\n'
                       '本地结构校验通过不代表 iPhone 导入已通过。\n')
            (stage / '本组说明.txt').write_text(summary, encoding='utf-8-sig')
            self.validate()
            if self.cancel.is_set():
                raise InterruptedError('已取消。')
            stage.rename(destination)
            return destination, report
        except BaseException as error:
            # A failed experiment cannot look like a completed pair. Delete only
            # the two files this invocation exclusively created, never inputs.
            for created in (output_image, output_movie, remuxed):
                if created.is_file():
                    created.unlink()
            (stage / '失败记录.txt').write_text(str(error), encoding='utf-8-sig')
            raise

"""Lossless MOV strategies for the standalone, uncertified import experiment.

This lab module never overwrites a source. The identifier-only control keeps
the old marker time intentionally. The two timing strategies preserve every
original non-marker sample and its presentation time, including auxv tracks.
No method here claims i4 or iPhone import compatibility.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from fractions import Fraction
import hashlib
import os
from pathlib import Path

from core import live_photo_movie as movie

METHODS = ("identifier_only", "qtff", "editlist")
LivePhotoMovieError = movie.LivePhotoMovieError


def _fraction(value):
    return None if value is None else {"numerator": value.numerator, "denominator": value.denominator}


def _hash_region(path, start, size, cancel_event):
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open("rb") as stream:
        stream.seek(start)
        while size:
            movie._cancel(cancel_event)
            block = stream.read(min(size, 1024 * 1024))
            movie._check(block, "MOV 校验读取截断。")
            digest.update(block)
            size -= len(block)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def _hash(path, cancel_event):
    signature = movie._signature(path)
    result = _hash_region(path, 0, signature[0], cancel_event)
    movie._check(signature == movie._signature(path), "MOV 哈希期间文件已变动。")
    return result


def _track_outside_sample_table(track):
    mdia = movie._one(movie._children(track.box), b"mdia")
    minf = movie._one(movie._children(mdia), b"minf")
    return movie._replace(track.box, {b"mdia": movie._replace(mdia, {
        b"minf": movie._replace(minf, {b"stbl": b""})})})


def _global_non_identifier_fields(loaded):
    """Retain actual key definitions and data, without relying on ID indexes."""
    result = []
    for location, boxes in [("moov", loaded.children)] + [
            (f"udta-{i}", movie._children(b))
            for i, b in enumerate(loaded.children) if b.kind == b"udta"]:
        for j, box in enumerate(b for b in boxes if b.kind == b"meta"):
            layout = movie._meta_layout(box)
            if layout is None:
                result.append((location, j, "opaque", box.data))
                continue
            _, _, keys, values, ids, _ = layout
            kept = tuple((i + 1, key.data) for i, key in enumerate(keys) if i + 1 not in ids)
            data = tuple(value.data for value in values if int.from_bytes(value.kind, "big") not in ids)
            if kept or data:
                result.append((location, j, kept, data))
    return result


def _verify_preserved(before, after, method, cancel_event):
    movie._check((before.scale, before.duration) == (after.scale, after.duration), "原 MOV 时钟发生改变。")
    movie._check(_global_non_identifier_fields(before) == _global_non_identifier_fields(after),
                 "非关联标识的全局元数据发生改变。")
    # Entire old mdat payloads stay at the same offsets. This also protects
    # opaque samples in auxiliary tracks, not just conventional audio/video.
    count = 0
    for box in before.top:
        if box.kind != b"mdat":
            continue
        start, size = box.start + box.header, box.size - box.header
        movie._check(_hash_region(before.path, start, size, cancel_event)
                     == _hash_region(after.path, start, size, cancel_event),
                     "原 mdat 样本字节发生改变。")
        count += 1
    after_tracks = {track.identifier: track for track in after.tracks}
    preserved = []
    auxiliary = []
    for old in before.tracks:
        movie._cancel(cancel_event)
        new = after_tracks.get(old.identifier)
        movie._check(new is not None, "原轨道丢失。")
        if method == "identifier_only" or old.handler != b"meta":
            movie._check(old.box.data == new.box.data, "对照轨道或非metadata轨道索引发生改变。")
        elif not old.marker_ids:
            movie._check(movie._canonical_metadata_track(old) == new.box.data,
                         "无标记metadata轨道除旧描述规范化外发生改变。")
        elif any(key.kind not in old.marker_ids and key.kind != b"\0" * 4 for key in old.keys):
            movie._check(not new.marker_ids and old.times == new.times
                         and old.visible_times == new.visible_times,
                         "混合metadata轨道其他样本时间发生改变。")
            movie._check(_track_outside_sample_table(old) == _track_outside_sample_table(new),
                         "混合metadata轨道非采样表属性发生改变。")
            ignored = {b"stsd", b"stsc", b"stsz", b"stco", b"co64"}
            movie._check([b.data for b in movie._children(old.stbl) if b.kind not in ignored]
                         == [b.data for b in movie._children(new.stbl) if b.kind not in ignored],
                         "混合metadata的原时间表或其他采样属性发生改变。")
            movie._check([k.data for k in old.keys if k.kind not in old.marker_ids]
                         == [k.data for k in new.keys], "混合metadata非标记声明发生改变。")
            expected = [b"".join(b.data for b in movie._boxes(p) if b.kind not in old.marker_ids)
                        or movie._atom(b"\0" * 4) for p in old.metadata_samples]
            movie._check(expected == new.metadata_samples, "混合metadata非标记样本发生改变。")
        else:
            # A pure marker track is intentionally replaced at the same ID.
            old_ref = movie._one(movie._children(old.box), b"tref", optional=True)
            new_ref = movie._one(movie._children(new.box), b"tref", optional=True)
            movie._check((old_ref.data if old_ref else None) == (new_ref.data if new_ref else None),
                         "标记轨道关联发生改变。")
            continue
        preserved.append(old.identifier)
        if old.handler not in (b"vide", b"soun", b"meta"):
            auxiliary.append(old.identifier)
    movie._check(before.signature == movie._signature(before.path)
                 and after.signature == movie._signature(after.path), "验证期间 MOV 文件已变动。")
    return {
        "all_original_mdat_payloads_preserved": True,
        "original_mdat_count": count,
        "non_marker_sample_bytes_and_timing_preserved": True,
        "non_metadata_tracks_byte_identical": True,
        "non_identifier_global_metadata_preserved": True,
        "preserved_track_ids": preserved,
        "auxiliary_track_ids": auxiliary,
    }


def _identifier_only(loaded, destination, identifier, cancel_event):
    rewritten = movie._atom(b"moov", b"".join(movie._rewrite_global_metadata(loaded.children, identifier)))
    created = False
    try:
        with loaded.path.open("rb") as source, destination.open("xb") as output:
            created = True
            for box in loaded.top:
                movie._cancel(cancel_event)
                source.seek(box.start)
                remaining, first = box.size, True
                while remaining:
                    movie._cancel(cancel_event)
                    data = source.read(min(remaining, 1024 * 1024))
                    movie._check(data, "MOV 对照文件复制截断。")
                    if first:
                        if box.kind == b"moov":
                            data = data[:4] + b"free" + data[8:]
                        elif data[:4] == b"\0" * 4:
                            data = movie._pack(box.size) + data[4:]
                        first = False
                    output.write(data)
                    remaining -= len(data)
            output.write(rewritten)
            output.flush()
            os.fsync(output.fileno())
        movie._check(loaded.signature == movie._signature(loaded.path), "保存对照文件期间原 MOV 已变动。")
    except BaseException:
        if created:
            destination.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        raise


def rewrite_movie(source, destination, identifier, photo_time: Fraction, method: str, cancel_event=None):
    """Create a new MOV; return JSON-compatible verification, never overwrite.

    ``identifier_only`` deliberately retains the old marker time. ``editlist``
    rejects frame times not exactly representable in the original movie clock.
    Unknown/unsupported containers fail closed; no transcode fallback exists.
    """
    source, destination = Path(source), Path(destination)
    movie._check(method in METHODS, "未知 MOV 实验方法。")
    movie._check(os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(source)) != os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(destination))
                 and not destination.exists() and not destination.is_symlink(), "MOV 输出必须为不存在的新文件。")
    movie._check(isinstance(identifier, str) and identifier and "\0" not in identifier
                 and len(identifier.encode("utf-8")) <= 4096, "Live Photo 配对标识无效。")
    movie._check(isinstance(photo_time, Fraction), "帧时间必须是精确 Fraction。")
    before = movie._load(source, cancel_event)
    old_info = movie._info(before)
    visible = [time for track in before.tracks if track.handler == b"vide" for time in track.visible_times]
    movie._check(0 <= photo_time < Fraction(before.duration, before.scale)
                 and visible.count(photo_time) == 1, "所选时间不是唯一、有效的视频呈现帧。")
    if method == "identifier_only":
        movie._check(old_info.marker_count == 1, "仅更新关联标识的对照必须已有唯一有效关键帧标记。")
    source_hash = _hash(source, cancel_event)
    movie._check(before.signature == movie._signature(source), "开始实验前 MOV 已变动。")
    created = False
    try:
        if method == "identifier_only":
            _identifier_only(before, destination, identifier, cancel_event)
        else:
            movie.rewrite_live_photo_movie(source, destination, identifier, photo_time,
                                            cancel_event=cancel_event, timing_method=method)
        created = True
        after = movie._load(destination, cancel_event)
        output_info = movie._info(after)
        expected_time = old_info.photo_time if method == "identifier_only" else photo_time
        movie._check(output_info.asset_identifier == identifier and output_info.marker_count == 1
                     and output_info.photo_time == expected_time, "MOV 输出标识或关键帧时间不符合实验方法。")
        verification = _verify_preserved(before, after, method, cancel_event)
        output_hash = _hash(destination, cancel_event)
        movie._check(before.signature == movie._signature(source)
                     and after.signature == movie._signature(destination), "实验完成前 MOV 文件已变动。")
        movie._cancel(cancel_event)
        return {
            "schema": "live-photo-method-lab.movie.v1",
            "method": method,
            "timing_updated": method != "identifier_only",
            "source_time": _fraction(old_info.photo_time),
            "selected_time": _fraction(photo_time),
            "output_time": _fraction(output_info.photo_time),
            "asset_identifier": identifier,
            "marker_count": output_info.marker_count,
            "track_count": output_info.track_count,
            "source_sha256": source_hash,
            "output_sha256": output_hash,
            "verification": verification,
            "import_compatibility_verified": False,
        }
    except BaseException:
        if created:
            destination.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        raise
