"""Conservative, lossless QuickTime Live Photo metadata rewriting.

No encoder is involved. Existing audio/video chunks keep their offsets and
bytes. Only the active movie box and timed metadata sample tables are changed.
photo_time is an exact *movie presentation* time, after the video edit list.

Format references (implementation is original, no third-party code copied):
https://developer.apple.com/documentation/quicktime-file-format/overview_of_timed_metadata
https://developer.apple.com/documentation/quicktime-file-format/timed_metadata_sample_descriptions
https://developer.apple.com/documentation/quicktime-file-format/constant-size_timed_metadata_sample_data
https://developer.apple.com/documentation/quicktime-file-format/metadata_atoms_and_types

Fragmented/encrypted/external media, multiple video tracks and complex edits
are rejected. This file does not claim Apple Photos import certification.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from fractions import Fraction
import hashlib
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import struct


CONTENT_KEY = b"com.apple.quicktime.content.identifier"
STILL_KEY = b"com.apple.quicktime.still-image-time"
MAX_MOOV = 64 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_SAMPLES = 200_000
MAX_METADATA = 32 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_BOXES = 200_000
U32 = 0xffffffff


class LivePhotoMovieError(ValueError):
    """Unsupported or ambiguous media; no source file has been changed."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LivePhotoMovieInfo:
    asset_identifier: str | None = None
    photo_time: Fraction | None = None
    duration: Fraction | None = None
    supported: bool = False
    unsupported_reason: str | None = None
    marker_count: int = 0
    track_count: int = 0
    media_payload_digests: tuple[tuple[int, str, str], ...] = ()


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class _Box:
    kind: bytes
    start: int
    size: int
    header: int
    data: bytes = b""

    @property
    def payload(self):
        return self.data[self.header:]


@dataclass
class _Track:
    box: _Box
    identifier: int
    handler: bytes
    scale: int
    duration: int
    stbl: _Box
    sample_entries: list[_Box]
    samples: list[tuple[int, int]]
    times: list[Fraction]
    visible_times: list[Fraction]
    marker_ids: set[bytes]
    keys: list[_Box]
    marker_times: list[Fraction]
    metadata_samples: list[bytes]


@dataclass
class _Movie:
    path: Path
    signature: tuple
    top: list[_Box]
    moov: _Box
    children: list[_Box]
    scale: int
    duration: int
    tracks: list[_Track]
    asset_identifier: str | None


def _check(value, message):
    if not value:
        raise LivePhotoMovieError(message)


def _cancel(cancel_event):
    if cancel_event is not None and cancel_event.is_set():
        raise InterruptedError("已取消 Live Photo 视频保存。")


def _u32(data, offset=0):
    _check(offset + 4 <= len(data), "MOV 数据字段截断。")
    return struct.unpack_from(">I", data, offset)[0]


def _pack(value):
    _check(0 <= value <= U32, "MOV 整数字段超过支持范围。")
    return struct.pack(">I", value)


def _atom(kind, payload=b""):
    _check(len(kind) == 4 and len(payload) + 8 <= U32, "MOV atom 大小超限。")
    return _pack(len(payload) + 8) + kind + payload


def _full(kind, payload=b"", version=0, flags=0):
    return _atom(kind, bytes([version]) + flags.to_bytes(3, "big") + payload)


def _boxes(data, start=0):
    result = []
    pos = start
    while pos < len(data):
        _check(len(result) < MAX_BOXES and pos + 8 <= len(data), "MOV atom 边界无效。")
        size, kind = struct.unpack_from(">I4s", data, pos)
        header = 8
        if size == 1:
            _check(pos + 16 <= len(data), "MOV 64位 atom 截断。")
            size = struct.unpack_from(">Q", data, pos + 8)[0]
            header = 16
        _check(size >= header and pos + size <= len(data), "MOV atom 长度无效或未限定。")
        result.append(_Box(kind, pos, size, header, data[pos:pos + size]))
        pos += size
    return result


def _one(boxes, kind, optional=False):
    matches = [b for b in boxes if b.kind == kind]
    _check(len(matches) == 1 or (optional and not matches), f"MOV {kind!r} 缺失或重复。")
    return matches[0] if matches else None


def _children(box):
    return _boxes(box.payload)


def _replace(box, replacements):
    return _atom(box.kind, b"".join(replacements.get(b.kind, b.data) for b in _children(box)))


def _signature(path):
    s = path.stat()
    return s.st_size, s.st_mtime_ns, s.st_dev, s.st_ino


def _top(stream, length):
    result = []
    pos = 0
    while pos < length:
        _check(len(result) < MAX_BOXES and pos + 8 <= length, "MOV 顶层 atom 截断。")
        stream.seek(pos)
        size, kind = struct.unpack(">I4s", stream.read(8))
        header = 8
        if size == 1:
            _check(pos + 16 <= length, "MOV 64位 atom 截断。")
            size = struct.unpack(">Q", stream.read(8))[0]
            header = 16
        elif size == 0:
            size = length - pos
            _check(size <= U32 and kind == b"mdat", "不支持未限定的巨大 MOV atom。")
        _check(size >= header and pos + size <= length, "MOV 顶层 atom 长度无效。")
        result.append(_Box(kind, pos, size, header))
        pos += size
    return result


def _clock(box):
    p = box.payload
    _check(len(p) >= 24 and p[0] in (0, 1) and p[1:4] == b"\0" * 3, "MOV 时钟版本不支持。")
    offset = 12 if p[0] == 0 else 20
    scale = _u32(p, offset)
    if p[0] == 0:
        duration = _u32(p, offset + 4)
    else:
        _check(len(p) >= offset + 12, "MOV 64位时钟截断。")
        duration = struct.unpack_from(">Q", p, offset + 4)[0]
    _check(scale > 0 and duration > 0, "MOV 时间基或时长无效。")
    return scale, duration


def _table(box, columns, signed_last=False):
    p = box.payload
    _check(len(p) >= 8 and p[0] in ((0, 1) if signed_last else (0,))
           and p[1:4] == b"\0" * 3, "MOV 表头不支持。")
    count = _u32(p, 4)
    _check(count <= MAX_SAMPLES and len(p) == 8 + count * columns * 4, "MOV 表长度超限或无效。")
    fmt = ">" + "I" * (columns - 1) + ("i" if signed_last and p[0] == 1 else "I")
    return [struct.unpack_from(fmt, p, 8 + i * columns * 4) for i in range(count)]


def _sample_ranges(stbl, entries, mdats):
    boxes = _children(stbl)
    _check(not any(b.kind in {b"saio", b"senc", b"saiz", b"stz2"} for b in boxes), "不支持加密或紧凑采样表。")
    sz = _one(boxes, b"stsz").payload
    _check(len(sz) >= 12 and sz[:4] == b"\0" * 4, "MOV 样本大小表不支持。")
    fixed, count = _u32(sz, 4), _u32(sz, 8)
    _check(0 < count <= MAX_SAMPLES and len(sz) == 12 + (0 if fixed else 4 * count), "MOV 样本数超限或无效。")
    sizes = [fixed] * count if fixed else [_u32(sz, 12 + 4 * i) for i in range(count)]
    co = [b for b in boxes if b.kind in {b"stco", b"co64"}]
    _check(len(co) == 1, "MOV chunk 偏移表缺失或重复。")
    p = co[0].payload
    width = 8 if co[0].kind == b"co64" else 4
    chunks = _u32(p, 4)
    _check(p[:4] == b"\0" * 4 and 0 < chunks <= MAX_SAMPLES and len(p) == 8 + width * chunks, "MOV chunk 偏移表无效。")
    offsets = [int.from_bytes(p[8 + width * i:8 + width * (i + 1)], "big") for i in range(chunks)]
    sc = _table(_one(boxes, b"stsc"), 3)
    _check(sc and sc[0][0] == 1 and all(a[0] < b[0] for a, b in zip(sc, sc[1:])), "MOV chunk 映射无效。")
    _check(all(0 < first <= chunks and 0 < n <= count and 1 <= desc <= len(entries) for first, n, desc in sc), "MOV chunk 描述索引无效。")
    result = []
    run = 0
    for i, offset in enumerate(offsets, 1):
        while run + 1 < len(sc) and sc[run + 1][0] <= i:
            run += 1
        n = sc[run][1]
        _check(len(result) + n <= count, "MOV chunk 样本数溢出。")
        for size in sizes[len(result):len(result) + n]:
            _check(any(lo <= offset and offset + size <= hi for lo, hi in mdats), "MOV 样本不在本文件 mdat 内。")
            result.append((offset, size))
            offset += size
    _check(len(result) == count, "MOV 样本计数不一致。")
    return result


def _presentation_times(track, stbl, scale, count, movie_scale):
    rows = _table(_one(_children(stbl), b"stts"), 2)
    _check(sum(n for n, _ in rows) == count and all(n and d for n, d in rows), "MOV DTS 表无效。")
    ticks = []
    elapsed = 0
    for n, delta in rows:
        for _ in range(n):
            ticks.append(elapsed)
            elapsed += delta
    ctts = _one(_children(stbl), b"ctts", optional=True)
    if ctts:
        composition = _table(ctts, 2, signed_last=True)
        _check(sum(n for n, _ in composition) == count, "MOV PTS 表计数不一致。")
        offsets = [offset for n, offset in composition for _ in range(n)]
        ticks = [tick + offset for tick, offset in zip(ticks, offsets)]
    times = [Fraction(tick, scale) for tick in ticks]
    tkhd = _one(_children(track), b"tkhd").payload
    duration_offset, duration_width = (28, 8) if tkhd[0] == 1 else (20, 4)
    _check(len(tkhd) >= duration_offset + duration_width, "MOV track 时长截断。")
    track_duration = int.from_bytes(tkhd[duration_offset:duration_offset + duration_width], "big")
    visible_start, visible_end = Fraction(0), Fraction(track_duration, movie_scale)
    edts = _one(_children(track), b"edts", optional=True)
    if edts:
        elst = _one(_children(edts), b"elst").payload
        _check(len(elst) >= 8 and elst[0] in (0, 1) and elst[1:4] == b"\0" * 3, "MOV edit list 版本不支持。")
        version, n = elst[0], _u32(elst, 4)
        width = 20 if version else 12
        _check(n in (1, 2) and len(elst) == 8 + width * n, "不支持多段 MOV edit list。")
        edits = [struct.unpack_from(">Qqhh" if version else ">Iihh", elst, 8 + i * width) for i in range(n)]
        _check(all(rate == 1 and frac == 0 for _, _, rate, frac in edits), "不支持变速 MOV edit list。")
        delay = Fraction(0)
        if n == 2:
            _check(edits[0][1] == -1, "仅支持首段为空白的 MOV edit list。")
            delay = Fraction(edits[0][0], movie_scale)
        duration, media_time, _, _ = edits[-1]
        _check(media_time >= 0 and duration > 0, "MOV 有效 edit 区间无效。")
        times = [t - Fraction(media_time, scale) + delay for t in times]
        visible_start, visible_end = delay, delay + Fraction(duration, movie_scale)
    return times, [t for t in times if visible_start <= t < visible_end]


def _metadata_extensions(entry):
    _check(entry.kind == b"mebx" and len(entry.payload) >= 16, "不支持此 timed metadata 样本格式。")
    _check(entry.payload[:6] == b"\0" * 6 and entry.payload[6:8] == b"\0\1",
           "不支持此 mebx 样本头。")
    # QTFF's standard sample description is 16 bytes INCLUDING size/type:
    # reserved[6] + data_reference_index[2], then the keys extension directly.
    # The initial Windows test build emitted exactly eight extra zero bytes.
    # Read only that precise legacy shape, never scan for a plausible key box;
    # all rewritten metadata descriptions below use the canonical 8-byte prefix.
    start = 8
    if entry.payload[8:16] == b"\0" * 8:
        _check(len(entry.payload) >= 24 and entry.payload[20:24] == b"keys",
               "旧版 mebx 填充后缺少有效 keys 容器。")
        start = 16
    extensions = _boxes(entry.payload, start)
    _one(extensions, b"keys")
    return extensions


def _metadata_layout(entry):
    extensions = _metadata_extensions(entry)
    keys = _children(_one(extensions, b"keys"))
    _check(len({key.kind for key in keys}) == len(keys), "mebx key ID 重复。")
    marker_ids = set()
    for key in keys:
        if key.kind == b"\0" * 4:
            continue
        _check(key.kind != b"\xff" * 4, "mebx 使用保留 key ID。")
        definition = _one(_children(key), b"keyd").payload
        if definition == b"mdta" + STILL_KEY:
            dtype = _one(_children(key), b"dtyp").payload
            _check(dtype == struct.pack(">II", 0, 65), "still-image-time 类型不是 int8。")
            marker_ids.add(key.kind)
    _check(len(marker_ids) <= 1, "同一轨道出现多个 still-image-time key。")
    return keys, marker_ids


def _meta_layout(box):
    # QuickTime has no FullBox header; ISO meta has four version/flag bytes.
    p = box.payload
    prefix = b"\0" * 4 if p[:4] == b"\0" * 4 else b""
    children = _boxes(p, len(prefix))
    hdlr = _one(children, b"hdlr", optional=True)
    if not hdlr or len(hdlr.payload) < 12 or hdlr.payload[8:12] != b"mdta":
        _check(CONTENT_KEY not in p and STILL_KEY not in p, "未知 metadata 容器包含 Live Photo key。")
        return None
    keys_box = _one(children, b"keys")
    keys_payload = keys_box.payload
    _check(keys_payload[:4] == b"\0" * 4 and len(keys_payload) >= 8, "mdta keys 表不支持。")
    keys = _boxes(keys_payload, 8)
    _check(len(keys) == _u32(keys_payload, 4), "mdta keys 计数不一致。")
    _check(not any(k.kind == b"mdta" and k.payload == STILL_KEY for k in keys), "发现非时序 still-image-time 标签，拒绝伪标记。")
    ilst = _one(children, b"ilst")
    values = _children(ilst)
    _check(all(1 <= int.from_bytes(b.kind, 'big') <= len(keys) for b in values), "mdta ilst 索引无效。")
    ids = {i + 1 for i, k in enumerate(keys) if k.kind == b"mdta" and k.payload == CONTENT_KEY}
    identifiers = []
    for item in values:
        if int.from_bytes(item.kind, "big") in ids:
            data = _one(_children(item), b"data").payload
            _check(len(data) > 8 and _u32(data) == 1, "ContentIdentifier 编码不是 UTF-8。")
            try:
                value = data[8:].decode("utf-8")
            except UnicodeError as error:
                raise LivePhotoMovieError("ContentIdentifier UTF-8 无效。") from error
            _check(value and "\0" not in value, "ContentIdentifier 为空或含NULL。")
            identifiers.append(value)
    return prefix, children, keys, values, ids, identifiers


def _reject_unhandled_track_pairing(box, depth=0):
    # QTFF also permits metadata at track/media scope. Changing those boxes
    # would violate the untouched audio/video-track contract, and leaving an
    # old pairing ID there would produce an ambiguous asset. Inspect only
    # real container children, never codec bytes or mdat string coincidences.
    _check(depth <= 8, "MOV metadata 容器嵌套过深。")
    for child in _children(box):
        if child.kind == b"meta":
            layout = _meta_layout(child)
            if layout:
                _check(not layout[4], "不支持轨道或media层ContentIdentifier；不能安全保留配对标识。")
        elif child.kind in (b"trak", b"mdia", b"udta"):
            _reject_unhandled_track_pairing(child, depth + 1)


def _load(path, cancel_event=None):
    path = Path(path)
    _cancel(cancel_event)
    signature = _signature(path)
    with path.open("rb") as stream:
        top = _top(stream, signature[0])
        _check(not any(b.kind in {b"moof", b"mfra", b"sidx"} for b in top), "不支持碎片化 MOV。")
        moov_pos = _one(top, b"moov")
        _check(moov_pos.size <= MAX_MOOV, "MOV 索引超出安全大小限制。")
        stream.seek(moov_pos.start)
        moov = _Box(b"moov", moov_pos.start, moov_pos.size, moov_pos.header, stream.read(moov_pos.size))
        children = _children(moov)
        _check(not any(b.kind in {b"cmov", b"mvex"} for b in children), "不支持压缩或碎片化 MOV 索引。")
        scale, duration = _clock(_one(children, b"mvhd"))
        mdats = [(b.start + b.header, b.start + b.size) for b in top if b.kind == b"mdat"]
        _check(mdats, "MOV 没有 mdat。")
        identifiers = []
        for parent in [children] + [_children(b) for b in children if b.kind == b"udta"]:
            for meta in (b for b in parent if b.kind == b"meta"):
                layout = _meta_layout(meta)
                if layout:
                    identifiers.extend(layout[-1])
        _check(len(set(identifiers)) <= 1, "MOV 中存在冲突的 ContentIdentifier。")
        tracks = []
        for track in (b for b in children if b.kind == b"trak"):
            _cancel(cancel_event)
            _reject_unhandled_track_pairing(track)
            tc = _children(track)
            tkhd = _one(tc, b"tkhd").payload
            _check(len(tkhd) >= 24 and tkhd[0] in (0, 1), "MOV track header 不支持。")
            identifier = _u32(tkhd, 12 if tkhd[0] == 0 else 20)
            _check(identifier > 0, "MOV track ID 无效。")
            mdia = _children(_one(tc, b"mdia"))
            hdlr = _one(mdia, b"hdlr").payload
            _check(len(hdlr) >= 12, "MOV handler 截断。")
            handler = hdlr[8:12]
            ts, td = _clock(_one(mdia, b"mdhd"))
            minf = _children(_one(mdia, b"minf"))
            dref = _one(_children(_one(minf, b"dinf")), b"dref").payload
            refs = _boxes(dref, 8)
            # QTFF permits a self-contained Macintosh alias as well as URL.
            # Require v0/self-reference-only with no external alias/URL data.
            _check(dref[:8] == b"\0\0\0\0\0\0\0\1" and len(refs) == 1 and refs[0].kind in (b"url ", b"alis")
                   and refs[0].payload == b"\0\0\0\1", "不支持外部 MOV 媒体引用。")
            stbl = _one(minf, b"stbl")
            stsd = _one(_children(stbl), b"stsd").payload
            entries = _boxes(stsd, 8)
            _check(stsd[:4] == b"\0" * 4 and len(entries) == _u32(stsd, 4) and entries, "MOV stsd 无效。")
            _check(all(e.kind not in {b"encv", b"enca"} and len(e.payload) >= 8 and e.payload[6:8] == b"\0\1" for e in entries),
                   "不支持加密或外部样本描述。")
            samples = _sample_ranges(stbl, entries, mdats)
            times, visible_times = _presentation_times(track, stbl, ts, len(samples), scale)
            keys, marker_ids, marker_times, metadata_samples = [], set(), [], []
            if handler == b"meta":
                _check(len(entries) == 1 and entries[0].kind == b"mebx", "不支持多描述或非mebx metadata轨道。")
                keys, marker_ids = _metadata_layout(entries[0])
                _check(not any(_one(_children(key), b"keyd").payload == b"mdta" + CONTENT_KEY
                               for key in keys if key.kind != b"\0" * 4),
                       "不支持时序ContentIdentifier；不能安全保留配对标识。")
                _check(sum(n for _, n in samples) <= MAX_METADATA, "MOV timed metadata 超出安全大小限制。")
                for (offset, size), when in zip(samples, times):
                    _cancel(cancel_event)
                    stream.seek(offset)
                    payload = stream.read(size)
                    atoms = _boxes(payload)
                    _check(not any(b.kind == b"\xff" * 4 for b in atoms), "不支持扩展 timed metadata sample。")
                    markers = [b for b in atoms if b.kind in marker_ids]
                    _check(len(markers) <= 1 and all(len(b.payload) == 1 for b in markers), "still-image-time 样本无效。")
                    _check(not markers or when in visible_times, "still-image-time 样本被edit list排除。")
                    marker_times.extend([when] * len(markers))
                    metadata_samples.append(payload)
            else:
                _check(STILL_KEY not in stsd and CONTENT_KEY not in stsd, "其他轨道包含未知 Live Photo metadata。")
            tracks.append(_Track(track, identifier, handler, ts, td, stbl, entries, samples, times, visible_times,
                                 marker_ids, keys, marker_times, metadata_samples))
    _check(tracks and len({t.identifier for t in tracks}) == len(tracks), "MOV track ID 缺失或重复。")
    _check(sum(t.handler == b"vide" for t in tracks) == 1, "仅支持单视频轨道 MOV。")
    _check(sum(len(t.marker_times) for t in tracks) <= 1, "MOV 包含多个 still-image-time 标记。")
    for t in tracks:
        _check(not t.marker_ids or len(t.marker_times) == 1, "still-image-time key 没有唯一有效样本。")
    _check(signature == _signature(path), "读取期间 MOV 源文件已更改。")
    return _Movie(path, signature, top, moov, children, scale, duration, tracks, identifiers[0] if identifiers else None)


def _info(movie, digests=()):
    times = [time for t in movie.tracks for time in t.marker_times]
    return LivePhotoMovieInfo(movie.asset_identifier, times[0] if times else None,
                             Fraction(movie.duration, movie.scale), True, None,
                             len(times), len(movie.tracks), digests)


def inspect_live_photo_movie(path, *, cancel_event=None):
    try:
        return _info(_load(path, cancel_event))
    except LivePhotoMovieError as error:
        return LivePhotoMovieInfo(unsupported_reason=str(error))


def video_presentation_times(path, *, cancel_event=None):
    movie = _load(path, cancel_event)
    return tuple(sorted(t for track in movie.tracks if track.handler == b"vide" for t in track.visible_times
                        if 0 <= t < Fraction(movie.duration, movie.scale)))


def media_payload_digests(path, *, cancel_event=None):
    """Hashes encoded samples in decode order, independently for audio/video."""
    movie = _load(path, cancel_event)
    result = []
    with Path(path).open("rb") as stream:
        for track in movie.tracks:
            if track.handler not in (b"vide", b"soun"):
                continue
            digest = hashlib.sha256()
            for offset, size in track.samples:
                _cancel(cancel_event)
                stream.seek(offset)
                remaining = size
                while remaining:
                    _cancel(cancel_event)
                    data = stream.read(min(1024 * 1024, remaining))
                    _check(data, "MOV 样本读取截断。")
                    digest.update(data)
                    remaining -= len(data)
            result.append((track.identifier, track.handler.decode("ascii"), digest.hexdigest()))
    _check(movie.signature == _signature(Path(path)), "校验期间 MOV 文件已更改。")
    return tuple(result)


def verify_movie_payload_preserved(source, destination, *, cancel_event=None):
    before = _load(source, cancel_event)
    after = _load(destination, cancel_event)
    _check((before.scale, before.duration) == (after.scale, after.duration), "MOV movie 时钟被更改。")
    for original in before.tracks:
        if original.handler not in (b"vide", b"soun"):
            continue
        matches = [t for t in after.tracks if t.identifier == original.identifier]
        _check(len(matches) == 1 and matches[0].box.data == original.box.data,
               "原音视频轨道索引、旋转或其他属性被更改。")
    a = media_payload_digests(source, cancel_event=cancel_event)
    b = media_payload_digests(destination, cancel_event=cancel_event)
    _check(a == b, "MOV 原音视频编码样本被更改。")
    return b


def _rewrite_meta(box, identifier):
    layout = _meta_layout(box)
    if layout is None:
        return box.data, False
    prefix, children, keys, values, ids, _ = layout
    _check(len(ids) <= 1, "同一 metadata 容器包含重复 ContentIdentifier key。")
    if not ids:
        keys.append(_Box(b"mdta", 0, 0, 8, _atom(b"mdta", CONTENT_KEY)))
        index = len(keys)
    else:
        index = next(iter(ids))
    value = _atom(_pack(index), _atom(b"data", _pack(1) + _pack(0) + identifier.encode("utf-8")))
    new_values = [b.data for b in values if int.from_bytes(b.kind, "big") != index] + [value]
    replacements = {b"keys": _full(b"keys", _pack(len(keys)) + b"".join(k.data for k in keys)),
                    b"ilst": _atom(b"ilst", b"".join(new_values))}
    return _atom(b"meta", prefix + b"".join(replacements.get(b.kind, b.data) for b in children)), True


def _new_meta(identifier):
    hdlr = _full(b"hdlr", b"\0" * 4 + b"mdta" + b"\0" * 12 + b"\0")
    keys = _full(b"keys", _pack(1) + _atom(b"mdta", CONTENT_KEY))
    value = _atom(_pack(1), _atom(b"data", _pack(1) + _pack(0) + identifier.encode("utf-8")))
    return _atom(b"meta", hdlr + keys + _atom(b"ilst", value))


def _rewrite_global_metadata(children, identifier):
    result = []
    updated = False
    for box in children:
        if box.kind == b"meta":
            raw, changed = _rewrite_meta(box, identifier)
            result.append(raw)
            updated |= changed
        elif box.kind == b"udta":
            output = []
            for item in _children(box):
                if item.kind == b"meta":
                    raw, changed = _rewrite_meta(item, identifier)
                    output.append(raw)
                    updated |= changed
                else:
                    output.append(item.data)
            result.append(_atom(b"udta", b"".join(output)))
        else:
            result.append(box.data)
    if not updated:
        result.append(_new_meta(identifier))
    return result


def _sample_tables(payloads, offset):
    return {
        b"stsc": _full(b"stsc", _pack(1) + struct.pack(">III", 1, len(payloads), 1)),
        b"stsz": _full(b"stsz", _pack(0) + _pack(len(payloads)) + b"".join(_pack(len(p)) for p in payloads)),
        b"stco": _full(b"co64", _pack(1) + struct.pack(">Q", offset)),
        b"co64": _full(b"co64", _pack(1) + struct.pack(">Q", offset)),
    }


def _canonical_metadata_track(track):
    """Normalize only the exact old test-build mebx padding; preserve samples."""
    entry = track.sample_entries[0]
    if entry.payload[8:16] != b"\0" * 8:
        return track.box.data
    entry = _atom(b"mebx", entry.payload[:8] + b"".join(b.data for b in _metadata_extensions(entry)))
    stbl = _replace(track.stbl, {b"stsd": _full(b"stsd", _pack(1) + entry)})
    mdia = _one(_children(track.box), b"mdia")
    minf = _one(_children(mdia), b"minf")
    return _replace(track.box, {b"mdia": _replace(mdia, {b"minf": _replace(minf, {b"stbl": stbl})})})


def _strip_marker(track, offset):
    entries = _metadata_extensions(track.sample_entries[0])
    keys = b"".join(key.data for key in track.keys if key.kind not in track.marker_ids)
    entry = _atom(b"mebx", track.sample_entries[0].payload[:8] + b"".join(
        _atom(b"keys", keys) if b.kind == b"keys" else b.data for b in entries))
    payloads = [b"".join(b.data for b in _boxes(p) if b.kind not in track.marker_ids)
                or _atom(b"\0" * 4) for p in track.metadata_samples]
    replacements = _sample_tables(payloads, offset)
    replacements[b"stsd"] = _full(b"stsd", _pack(1) + entry)
    stbl = _replace(track.stbl, replacements)
    mdia = _one(_children(track.box), b"mdia")
    minf = _one(_children(mdia), b"minf")
    return _replace(track.box, {b"mdia": _replace(mdia, {b"minf": _replace(minf, {b"stbl": stbl})})}), b"".join(payloads)


def _new_track(identifier, movie, when, offset, tref=b"", *, timing_method="qtff"):
    _check(timing_method in ("qtff", "editlist"), "未知实验时间标记方法。")
    scale = math.lcm(movie.scale, when.denominator)
    _check(scale <= U32, "所选帧时间无法由支持的32位metadata时间基精确表示。")
    delay = int(when * scale)
    _check(delay < U32, "所选帧超过metadata单样本时长范围。")
    payloads = ([_atom(b"\0" * 4)] if delay else []) + [_atom(_pack(1), b"\xff")]
    ticks = ([delay] if delay else []) + [1]
    total = delay + 1
    movie_duration = (total * movie.scale + scale - 1) // scale
    edts = b""
    if timing_method == "editlist":
        # elst segment durations use the unchanged MOVIE timescale. Do not
        # round an unrepresentable PTS or rescale untouched video/aux tracks.
        movie_delay = when * movie.scale
        _check(movie_delay.denominator == 1,
               "此精确帧时间不能由原 MOV 时钟表示；editlist 方法拒绝近似，请选 qtff。")
        delay = int(movie_delay)
        scale, total = movie.scale, 1
        payloads, ticks = [_atom(_pack(1), b"\xff")], [1]
        movie_duration = delay + 1
        edits = ([(delay, -1)] if delay else []) + [(1, 0)]
        edts = _atom(b"edts", _full(b"elst", _pack(len(edits)) + b"".join(
            struct.pack(">Iihh", duration, start, 1, 0) for duration, start in edits)))
    _check(movie_duration <= movie.duration, "封面帧标记超出视频播放时长。")
    matrix = struct.pack(">9I", 0x10000, 0, 0, 0, 0x10000, 0, 0, 0, 0x40000000)
    tkhd = _full(b"tkhd", struct.pack(">IIIII", 0, 0, identifier, 0, movie_duration)
                 + b"\0" * 16 + matrix + b"\0" * 8, flags=3)
    mdhd = _full(b"mdhd", struct.pack(">IIIIHH", 0, 0, scale, total, 0, 0))
    # QTFF media handler: mhlr component type and a counted component name.
    # This is not the ISO/global metadata handler used by _new_meta().
    name = b"Live Photo Metadata"
    hdlr = _full(b"hdlr", b"mhlr" + b"meta" + b"\0" * 12 + bytes([len(name)]) + name)
    declaration = _atom(b"keyd", b"mdta" + STILL_KEY) + _atom(b"dtyp", struct.pack(">II", 0, 65))
    entry = _atom(b"mebx", b"\0" * 6 + b"\0\1" + _atom(b"keys", _atom(_pack(1), declaration)))
    tables = _sample_tables(payloads, offset)
    stbl = _atom(b"stbl", _full(b"stsd", _pack(1) + entry)
                 + _full(b"stts", _pack(len(ticks)) + b"".join(struct.pack(">II", 1, tick) for tick in ticks))
                 + tables[b"stsc"] + tables[b"stsz"] + tables[b"co64"])
    # QTFF generic media header, graphicsmode=copy, white opcolor, no balance.
    gmhd = _atom(b"gmhd", _full(b"gmin", struct.pack(">HHHHHH", 0, 0xffff, 0xffff, 0xffff, 0, 0)))
    dinf = _atom(b"dinf", _full(b"dref", _pack(1) + _full(b"url ", flags=1)))
    mdia = _atom(b"mdia", mdhd + hdlr + _atom(b"minf", gmhd + dinf + stbl))
    return _atom(b"trak", tkhd + edts + tref + mdia), b"".join(payloads)


def rewrite_live_photo_movie(source, destination, asset_identifier, photo_time: Fraction, *, cancel_event=None,
                             timing_method="qtff"):
    """Write a new stage file only. Caller owns the later image+movie commit."""
    source, destination = Path(source), Path(destination)
    _check(source.resolve() != destination.resolve() and not destination.exists(), "MOV 输出必须为不存在的新文件。")
    _check(isinstance(asset_identifier, str) and asset_identifier and "\0" not in asset_identifier
           and len(asset_identifier.encode("utf-8")) <= 4096, "Live Photo 配对标识无效。")
    _check(isinstance(photo_time, Fraction), "封面时间必须是精确 Fraction，不能使用浮点估算。")
    movie = _load(source, cancel_event)
    duration = Fraction(movie.duration, movie.scale)
    _check(0 <= photo_time < duration, "封面帧时间超出视频时长。")
    video_times = [time for t in movie.tracks if t.handler == b"vide" for time in t.visible_times]
    _check(photo_time in video_times, "封面时间不对应视频实际呈现帧。")
    _check(video_times.count(photo_time) == 1, "多个视频帧具有相同呈现时间，无法唯一确定封面。")
    marker_tracks = [t for t in movie.tracks if t.marker_ids]
    _check(len(marker_tracks) <= 1, "多条 still-image-time 轨道存在歧义。")
    replace_track = None
    if marker_tracks and all(k.kind in marker_tracks[0].marker_ids or k.kind == b"\0" * 4 for k in marker_tracks[0].keys):
        replace_track = marker_tracks[0]
        _check(all(all(b.kind in replace_track.marker_ids or b.kind == b"\0" * 4 for b in _boxes(p))
                   for p in replace_track.metadata_samples), "原标记轨道含未知样本，不能安全替换。")
    next_id = replace_track.identifier if replace_track else max(t.identifier for t in movie.tracks) + 1
    _check(next_id < U32, "MOV track ID 用尽。")
    appended = bytearray()
    # Old test-build files may already have a marker removed from another
    # metadata track. Canonicalize its description too, without relocating or
    # editing any of that track's sample values/times or other declarations.
    track_updates = {t.identifier: _canonical_metadata_track(t) for t in movie.tracks if t.handler == b"meta"}
    base_offset = movie.signature[0] + 8
    for track in marker_tracks:
        if track is not replace_track:
            allowed = {b"stsd", b"stts", b"ctts", b"stsc", b"stsz", b"stco", b"co64", b"stss", b"sdtp"}
            _check(all(b.kind in allowed for b in _children(track.stbl)), "混合metadata轨道有尚未支持的采样扩展。")
            raw, payload = _strip_marker(track, base_offset + len(appended))
            track_updates[track.identifier] = raw
            appended.extend(payload)
    tref = b""
    if marker_tracks:
        reference = _one(_children(marker_tracks[0].box), b"tref", optional=True)
        tref = reference.data if reference else b""
    new_track, payload = _new_track(next_id, movie, photo_time, base_offset + len(appended), tref,
                                    timing_method=timing_method)
    appended.extend(payload)
    children = []
    for box in movie.children:
        if box.kind == b"trak":
            track = next(t for t in movie.tracks if t.box is box)
            if track is replace_track:
                children.append(new_track)
            else:
                children.append(track_updates.get(track.identifier, box.data))
        elif box.kind == b"mvhd":
            data = bytearray(box.payload)
            _check(len(data) >= 100, "MOV movie header 截断。")
            data[-4:] = _pack(max(_u32(data, len(data) - 4), next_id + 1))
            children.append(_atom(b"mvhd", bytes(data)))
        else:
            children.append(box.data)
    if not replace_track:
        children.append(new_track)
    rewritten = _atom(b"moov", b"".join(_rewrite_global_metadata(_boxes(b"".join(children)), asset_identifier)))
    # Reuse the active movie slot on later saves; no accumulating dead moov copies.
    # A shorter previous index left a padding free immediately after moov.
    # Coalesce that padding too, so alternating time=0/nonzero cannot accumulate
    # dead movie indexes. Original media offsets and total prefix size stay fixed.
    slot_end = movie.moov.start + movie.moov.size
    padding_starts = set()
    for top in movie.top:
        if (top.start == slot_end and top.kind == b"free"
                and slot_end - movie.moov.start + top.size <= MAX_MOOV):
            padding_starts.add(top.start)
            slot_end += top.size
    spare = slot_end - movie.moov.start - len(rewritten)
    in_place = spare == 0 or spare >= 8
    moov_patch = rewritten + (_atom(b"free", b"\0" * (spare - 8)) if spare >= 8 else b"") if in_place else None
    created = False
    try:
        _cancel(cancel_event)
        with source.open("rb") as incoming, destination.open("xb") as outgoing:
            created = True
            for top in movie.top:
                _cancel(cancel_event)
                if in_place and top.start in padding_starts:
                    continue
                incoming.seek(top.start)
                if top.kind == b"moov" and in_place:
                    outgoing.write(moov_patch)
                    continue
                remaining = top.size
                first = True
                while remaining:
                    _cancel(cancel_event)
                    block = incoming.read(min(1024 * 1024, remaining))
                    _check(block, "MOV 复制过程中截断。")
                    if first:
                        if top.kind == b"moov":
                            block = block[:4] + b"free" + block[8:]
                        elif block[:4] == b"\0" * 4:
                            block = _pack(top.size) + block[4:]
                        first = False
                    outgoing.write(block)
                    remaining -= len(block)
            outgoing.write(_atom(b"mdat", bytes(appended)))
            if not in_place:
                outgoing.write(rewritten)
            outgoing.flush()
            os.fsync(outgoing.fileno())
        _check(_signature(source) == movie.signature, "保存期间 MOV 源文件已更改。")
        check = _load(destination, cancel_event)
        result = _info(check)
        _check(result.asset_identifier == asset_identifier and result.photo_time == photo_time and result.marker_count == 1,
               "写入后的 Live Photo 标识或封面时间校验失败。")
        digests = verify_movie_payload_preserved(source, destination, cancel_event=cancel_event)
        _cancel(cancel_event)
        return _info(check, digests)
    except BaseException:
        if created:
            destination.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        raise

"""Fresh cover EXIF with a bounded Apple MakerNote ContentIdentifier.

MakerNote tag 0x0011 is Apple's ContentIdentifier, not EXIF ImageUniqueID.
The Apple IFD starts 14 bytes into its note; offsets are note-relative:
https://github.com/exiftool/exiftool/blob/master/lib/Image/ExifTool/Apple.pm
https://github.com/exiftool/exiftool/blob/master/lib/Image/ExifTool/MakerNotes.pm

Only capture provenance is copied. Old thumbnails, orientation, dimensions,
exposure/rendering fields and opaque MakerNotes describe the old pixels and
must not survive a replacement video frame. Missing time zones stay missing.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
import math
from pathlib import Path
import re
import struct

from PIL import Image

from . import imaging as _imaging  # Register the existing HEIC reader.

EXIF_IFD = 0x8769
GPS_IFD = 0x8825
MAKER_NOTE = 0x927C
CONTENT_IDENTIFIER = 0x0011
MAX_IDENTIFIER_BYTES = 512
MAX_EXIF_BYTES = 60 * 1024  # Fits a single JPEG APP1 segment, including header.
_APPLE_PREFIX = b'Apple iOS\x00\x00\x01'
_SAFE_TEXT = (0x010E, 0x010F, 0x0110, 0x013B, 0x8298)  # description/make/model/artist/copyright


def _identifier_bytes(value: str) -> bytes:
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value or not value.strip():
        raise ValueError('Live Photo 关联标识不能为空。')
    if any(ord(character) < 32 or ord(character) == 127 for character in value):
        raise ValueError('Live Photo 关联标识含无效控制字符。')
    try:
        encoded = value.encode('utf-8')
    except UnicodeError as error:
        raise ValueError('Live Photo 关联标识不是有效 UTF-8。') from error
    if len(encoded) > MAX_IDENTIFIER_BYTES:
        raise ValueError('Live Photo 关联标识过长。')
    return encoded


def _maker_note(identifier: str) -> bytes:
    value = _identifier_bytes(identifier) + b'\x00'
    # 14-byte header, IFD count, one 12-byte ASCII entry, next-IFD offset.
    offset = 14 + 2 + 12 + 4
    stored = value.ljust(4, b'\x00') if len(value) <= 4 else struct.pack('>I', offset)
    return (_APPLE_PREFIX + b'MM' + struct.pack('>H', 1)
            + struct.pack('>HHI', CONTENT_IDENTIFIER, 2, len(value)) + stored
            + struct.pack('>I', 0) + (value if len(value) > 4 else b''))


def _read_maker_identifier(note) -> str | None:
    if not isinstance(note, bytes) or not note.startswith(b'Apple iOS\x00'):
        return None
    if len(note) > MAX_EXIF_BYTES or len(note) < 20 or note[12:14] not in (b'MM', b'II'):
        raise ValueError('Apple MakerNote 结构无效。')
    endian = '>' if note[12:14] == b'MM' else '<'
    count = struct.unpack_from(endian + 'H', note, 14)[0]
    end = 16 + count * 12
    if count > 1024 or end + 4 > len(note):
        raise ValueError('Apple MakerNote 目录越界。')
    values = []
    for position in range(16, end, 12):
        tag, kind, length, pointer = struct.unpack_from(endian + 'HHII', note, position)
        if tag != CONTENT_IDENTIFIER:
            continue
        if kind != 2 or not 1 < length <= MAX_IDENTIFIER_BYTES + 1:
            raise ValueError('Apple 关联标识类型或长度无效。')
        start = position + 8 if length <= 4 else pointer
        if (length > 4 and start < end + 4) or start + length > len(note):
            raise ValueError('Apple 关联标识数据越界。')
        raw = note[start:start + length]
        if not raw.endswith(b'\x00'):
            raise ValueError('Apple 关联标识未终止。')
        try:
            value = raw[:-1].decode('utf-8')
        except UnicodeError as error:
            raise ValueError('Apple 关联标识编码无效。') from error
        _identifier_bytes(value)
        values.append(value)
    if len(values) > 1:
        raise ValueError('Apple MakerNote 包含重复关联标识。')
    return values[0] if values else None


def _metadata(path: Path):
    path = Path(path)
    if path.suffix.casefold() not in ('.jpg', '.jpeg', '.heic'):
        raise ValueError('Live Photo 封面仅支持 JPEG 或 HEIC。')
    with Image.open(path) as image:
        expected = 'HEIF' if path.suffix.casefold() == '.heic' else 'JPEG'
        if image.format != expected:
            raise ValueError('封面扩展名与实际格式不匹配。')
        raw = image.info.get('exif', b'')
        if expected == 'HEIF':
            # The Pillow HEIF opener normalizes EXIF Orientation in memory.
            # Validate the stored bytes, not that normalized copy.
            from pi_heif import open_heif
            raw = open_heif(path).info.get('exif') or b''
        if isinstance(raw, bytes) and len(raw) > 1024 * 1024:
            raise ValueError('源图片 EXIF 超出安全读取范围。')
        exif = Image.Exif()
        if raw:
            exif.load(raw)
        # Resolve nested offsets before closing the image's stream.
        details = dict(exif.get_ifd(EXIF_IFD)) if EXIF_IFD in exif else {}
        gps = dict(exif.get_ifd(GPS_IFD)) if GPS_IFD in exif else {}
        return dict(exif), details, gps, image.size


def _text(value, limit=1024):
    if isinstance(value, str) and len(value) <= limit and '\x00' not in value:
        return value
    return None


def _date(value, format):
    value = _text(value, 32)
    if value is None:
        return None
    try:
        parsed = datetime.strptime(value, format)
    except ValueError:
        return None
    return value if parsed.strftime(format) == value else None


def _rational(value, minimum=0.0, maximum=math.inf):
    if isinstance(value, (str, bytes, bool)):
        return None
    try:
        number = float(value)
        if math.isfinite(number) and minimum <= number <= maximum:
            return value
    except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError, ZeroDivisionError):
        pass
    return None


def _triple(value, maxima):
    if not isinstance(value, (tuple, list)) or len(value) != 3:
        return None
    if all(_rational(number, 0, maximum) is not None for number, maximum in zip(value, maxima)):
        return tuple(value)
    return None


def _capture_gps(gps):
    result = {}
    for ref_tag, value_tag, refs, bound in ((1, 2, ('N', 'S'), 90), (3, 4, ('E', 'W'), 180)):
        value = _triple(gps.get(value_tag), (bound, 59.999999999, 59.999999999))
        if value is not None and gps.get(ref_tag) in refs:
            degrees, minutes, seconds = map(float, value)
            if degrees + minutes / 60 + seconds / 3600 <= bound:
                result[ref_tag], result[value_tag] = gps[ref_tag], value
    altitude = _rational(gps.get(6))
    ref = gps.get(5)
    if isinstance(ref, bytes) and len(ref) == 1:
        ref = ref[0]
    if altitude is not None and ref in (0, 1):
        result[5], result[6] = ref, altitude
    timestamp = _triple(gps.get(7), (23, 59, 59.999999999))
    if timestamp is not None:
        result[7] = timestamp
    date = _date(gps.get(29), '%Y:%m:%d')
    if date is not None:
        result[29] = date
    datum = _text(gps.get(18), 128)
    if datum:
        result[18] = datum
    error = _rational(gps.get(31))
    if error is not None:
        result[31] = error
    version = gps.get(0)
    if result and isinstance(version, (bytes, tuple, list)) and len(version) == 4:
        if all(type(value) is int and 0 <= value <= 255 for value in version):
            result[0] = bytes(version)
    return result


def build_cover_exif(source: Path, asset_identifier: str, size: tuple[int, int]) -> bytes:
    """Return new EXIF bytes; never modify the source or invent a capture zone."""
    _identifier_bytes(asset_identifier)
    if (not isinstance(size, (tuple, list)) or len(size) != 2
            or any(type(value) is not int or not 1 <= value <= 100_000 for value in size)
            or size[0] * size[1] > 100_000_000):
        raise ValueError('封面图片尺寸无效。')
    primary, original, gps, _ = _metadata(source)
    output = Image.Exif()
    for tag in _SAFE_TEXT:
        value = _text(primary.get(tag))
        if value is not None:
            output[tag] = value
    modified = datetime.now().astimezone()
    output[0x0132] = modified.strftime('%Y:%m:%d %H:%M:%S')
    output[0x0100], output[0x0101] = size
    output[0x0112] = 1
    output[0x0131] = 'LiveViewer'
    zone = modified.strftime('%z')
    details = {0xA002: size[0], 0xA003: size[1], MAKER_NOTE: _maker_note(asset_identifier),
               0x9010: zone[:3] + ':' + zone[3:], 0x9290: f'{modified.microsecond:06d}'}
    for tag in (0x9003, 0x9004):
        date = _date(original.get(tag), '%Y:%m:%d %H:%M:%S')
        if date is not None:
            details[tag] = date
    for tag in (0x9011, 0x9012):
        value = original.get(tag)
        if isinstance(value, str) and re.fullmatch(r'[+-](?:0\d|1[0-4]):[0-5]\d', value):
            if value[1:3] != '14' or value[-2:] == '00':
                details[tag] = value
    for tag in (0x9291, 0x9292):
        value = original.get(tag)
        if isinstance(value, str) and re.fullmatch(r'\d{1,9}', value):
            details[tag] = value
    version = original.get(0x9000)
    if isinstance(version, bytes) and re.fullmatch(rb'\d{4}', version):
        details[0x9000] = version
    output[EXIF_IFD] = details
    location = _capture_gps(gps)
    if location:
        output[GPS_IFD] = location
    encoded = output.tobytes()
    if len(encoded) > MAX_EXIF_BYTES:
        raise ValueError('封面 EXIF 超过 JPEG/HEIC 兼容长度。')
    return encoded


def read_content_identifier(path: Path) -> str | None:
    """Read only Apple tag 17; absent or unusable notes have no usable ID."""
    _, details, _, _ = _metadata(path)
    try:
        return _read_maker_identifier(details.get(MAKER_NOTE))
    except ValueError:
        return None


def validate_cover_metadata(path: Path, identifier: str) -> None:
    """Check persisted EXIF and actual dimensions before the caller commits."""
    _identifier_bytes(identifier)
    primary, details, _, size = _metadata(path)
    if _read_maker_identifier(details.get(MAKER_NOTE)) != identifier:
        raise ValueError('保存后的 Apple Live Photo 关联标识不匹配。')
    if primary.get(0x0112) != 1:
        raise ValueError('保存后的封面方向不正确。')
    if (primary.get(0x0100), primary.get(0x0101)) != size:
        raise ValueError('保存后的封面尺寸与 EXIF 不匹配。')
    if (details.get(0xA002), details.get(0xA003)) != size:
        raise ValueError('保存后的 EXIF 像素尺寸不匹配。')

FFprobe 精确 PTS 与 FFmpeg 实际所选帧导出

"""Cancellable exact decoded-frame access, including variable-rate videos.

ffprobe's decoded-frame list and FFmpeg's select(n) both use decoder output
order. Seeking by an estimated frame rate or by milliseconds is deliberately
avoided: neither can identify adjacent frames reliably in variable-rate video.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from bisect import bisect_left
from dataclasses import dataclass
from fractions import Fraction
import io
import json
import math
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import time

from PIL import Image

from .frame_tools import resolve_frame_tools


MAX_FRAMES = 20_000
MAX_DURATION_SECONDS = 600
MAX_PIXELS = 64_000_000
MAX_PROBE_BYTES = 8 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_IMAGE_BYTES = 256 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_ERROR_BYTES = 1024 * 1024
PROCESS_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 45


class FrameCancelled(InterruptedError):
    """A cancelled request is recoverable; the next request can reuse a reader."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FrameInfo:
    index: int
    pts_seconds: float
    duration_seconds: float
    # Decoder presentation timestamp, in the movie timeline after edit lists.
    # Unlike the UI position, this is never shifted by the first decoded PTS.
    presentation_time: Fraction | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class VideoMetadata:
    width: int
    height: int
    rotation: float
    frames: tuple[FrameInfo, ...]
    duration_seconds: float
    codec: str
    start_time_seconds: float
    timestamps_estimated: bool = False
    time_base: Fraction | None = None
    still_image_time: Fraction | None = None
    asset_identifier: str | None = None

    @property
    def frame_count(self):
        return len(self.frames)


def _number(value, fallback=None):
    try:
        number = float(value)
        return number if math.isfinite(number) else fallback
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return fallback


def _fraction(value, fallback=0.0):
    try:
        numerator, denominator = str(value).split('/', 1)
        return float(numerator) / float(denominator)
    except (ValueError, ZeroDivisionError):
        return fallback


def _exact_fraction(value):
    try:
        return Fraction(str(value))
    except (ValueError, ZeroDivisionError):
        return None


class FrameReader:
    """One worker owns read operations; cancellation may come from the GUI.

    No media bytes are changed. Metadata is indexed once, previews are decoded
    on demand, and only one child process can run per reader. There is no frame
    image cache, keeping memory independent of the video's total frame count.
    """

    def __init__(self, path: Path, *, ffmpeg=None, ffprobe=None, cancel_event=None):
        self.path = Path(path).resolve()
        self._tool_arguments = (ffmpeg, ffprobe)
        self._tools = None
        self._cancel_event = cancel_event
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._generation = 0
        self._process = None
        self._closed = False
        self._metadata = None
        self._signature = None

    @property
    def metadata(self) -> VideoMetadata:
        return self.open()

    @property
    def frame_count(self) -> int:
        return len(self.metadata.frames)

    def _check_cancelled(self, generation=None):
        with self._lock:
            cancelled = self._closed or (generation is not None and generation != self._generation)
        if cancelled or (self._cancel_event is not None and self._cancel_event.is_set()):
            raise FrameCancelled("已取消视频读取。")

    def _file_signature(self):
        status = self.path.stat()
        return status.st_size, status.st_mtime_ns

    def _verify_source(self):
        self._check_cancelled()
        if self._signature is not None and self._file_signature() != self._signature:
            raise OSError("视频文件已变化，请重新打开封面图编辑器。")

    def _run(self, arguments, output_limit):
        self._check_cancelled()
        with self._lock:
            generation = self._generation
            if self._process is not None:
                raise RuntimeError("同一个视频读取器不能同时执行多个请求。")
        flags = subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW if sys.platform == 'win32' else 0
        process = subprocess.Popen(
            [str(argument) for argument in arguments], stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, creationflags=flags,
        )
        with self._lock:
            self._process = process
        deadline = time.monotonic() + PROCESS_TIMEOUT_SECONDS
        try:
            while True:
                self._check_cancelled(generation)
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                    raise TimeoutError("视频读取超时，请检查文件或移动硬盘连接。")
                try:
                    output, errors = process.communicate(timeout=.1)
                    break
                except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as pending:
                    if (pending.output is not None and len(pending.output) > output_limit
                            or pending.stderr is not None and len(pending.stderr) > MAX_ERROR_BYTES):
                        raise ValueError("视频数据超过逐帧读取的资源上限。")
            self._check_cancelled(generation)
            if len(output) > output_limit or len(errors) > MAX_ERROR_BYTES:
                raise ValueError("视频数据超过逐帧读取的资源上限。")
            if process.returncode:
                detail = errors.decode('utf-8', 'replace').strip()[-1200:]
                raise OSError("无法读取视频帧。" + ("\n" + detail if detail else ""))
            return output
        finally:
            if process.poll() is None:
                process.kill()
                process.communicate()
            with self._lock:
                if self._process is process:
                    self._process = None

    def open(self) -> VideoMetadata:
        self._check_cancelled()
        if self._metadata is not None:
            self._verify_source()
            return self._metadata
        if not self.path.is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError("视频文件暂时不可访问，请检查硬盘连接。")
        signature = self._file_signature()
        self._tools = resolve_frame_tools(*self._tool_arguments)
        command = [self._tools.ffprobe, '-v', 'error', '-threads', '1',
                   '-protocol_whitelist', 'file,pipe', '-select_streams', 'v:0',
                   '-show_frames', '-show_streams', '-show_format', '-show_entries',
                   'frame=best_effort_timestamp,pts,best_effort_timestamp_time,pkt_duration_time,duration_time,width,height:'
                   'stream=width,height,codec_name,avg_frame_rate,duration,time_base,start_pts,start_time:stream_tags=rotate:'
                   'stream_side_data=rotation,crop_left,crop_right,crop_top,crop_bottom:format=duration',
                   '-of', 'json', str(self.path)]
        document = json.loads(self._run(command, MAX_PROBE_BYTES))
        streams, frames = document.get('streams', []), document.get('frames', [])
        if not streams or not frames:
            raise ValueError("视频中没有可读取的画面。")
        if len(frames) > MAX_FRAMES:
            raise ValueError(f"视频超过 {MAX_FRAMES:,} 帧，暂不支持在封面图编辑器中读取。")
        stream = streams[0]
        # A MOV clean-aperture crop can leave the stream's coded size larger
        # than each decoded frame. The editor must report the pixels exported
        # by FFmpeg, before applying the stream's display rotation.
        width = int(frames[0].get('width') or stream.get('width', 0))
        height = int(frames[0].get('height') or stream.get('height', 0))
        if width < 1 or height < 1 or width * height > MAX_PIXELS:
            raise ValueError("视频画面尺寸超过读取上限。")
        rotation = _number(stream.get('tags', {}).get('rotate'), 0.0)
        for data in stream.get('side_data_list', []):
            if 'rotation' in data:
                rotation = _number(data['rotation'], rotation)
            # ffprobe exposes MOV clean-aperture cropping as stream side data;
            # FFmpeg's CLI applies it automatically before display rotation.
            # Codec-level cropping is already reflected in frame.width/height.
            crops = [_number(data.get(name), 0) for name in ('crop_left', 'crop_right', 'crop_top', 'crop_bottom')]
            if any(value < 0 for value in crops):
                raise ValueError("视频裁切尺寸无效。")
            width -= int(crops[0]) + int(crops[1])
            height -= int(crops[2]) + int(crops[3])
        if width < 1 or height < 1 or width * height > MAX_PIXELS:
            raise ValueError("视频裁切尺寸无效。")
        if round(rotation) % 180 == 90:
            width, height = height, width
        fps = _fraction(stream.get('avg_frame_rate'), 0.0)
        default_duration = 1.0 / fps if fps > 0 else 1.0 / 30
        timestamps, durations, precise_times, estimated = [], [], [], False
        time_base = _exact_fraction(stream.get('time_base'))
        if time_base is not None and time_base <= 0:
            time_base = None
        for frame in frames:
            raw_pts = _exact_fraction(frame.get('best_effort_timestamp'))
            if raw_pts is None:
                raw_pts = _exact_fraction(frame.get('pts'))
            exact = raw_pts * time_base if raw_pts is not None and raw_pts.denominator == 1 and time_base is not None else None
            timestamp = float(exact) if exact is not None else _number(frame.get('best_effort_timestamp_time'))
            duration = _number(frame.get('duration_time'), _number(frame.get('pkt_duration_time'), default_duration))
            duration = duration if duration > 0 else default_duration
            if timestamp is None:
                timestamp = timestamps[-1] + durations[-1] if timestamps else 0.0
                estimated = True
            if exact is None:
                estimated = True
            if timestamps and timestamp < timestamps[-1] - .000001:
                raise ValueError("视频帧时间顺序异常，无法可靠逐帧选择。")
            timestamps.append(timestamp)
            durations.append(duration)
            precise_times.append(exact)
        start = timestamps[0]
        records = []
        for index, timestamp in enumerate(timestamps):
            interval = timestamps[index + 1] - timestamp if index + 1 < len(timestamps) else durations[index]
            records.append(FrameInfo(index, max(0.0, timestamp - start), max(0.0, interval), precise_times[index]))
        duration = records[-1].pts_seconds + records[-1].duration_seconds
        if duration > MAX_DURATION_SECONDS:
            raise ValueError("视频超过 10 分钟，暂不支持在封面图编辑器中读取。")
        still_image_time = None
        asset_identifier = None
        if self.path.suffix.casefold() in ('.mov', '.mp4'):
            from .live_photo_movie import inspect_live_photo_movie
            try:
                movie = inspect_live_photo_movie(self.path, cancel_event=self._cancel_event)
                if movie.supported:
                    still_image_time = movie.photo_time
                    asset_identifier = movie.asset_identifier
            except InterruptedError:
                raise
            except (ValueError, OSError):
                # Generic preview remains available for unsupported metadata.
                # Saving performs its own strict pair validation and may refuse it.
                pass
        self._check_cancelled()
        if self._file_signature() != signature:
            raise OSError("视频文件已变化，请重新打开封面图编辑器。")
        self._signature = signature
        self._metadata = VideoMetadata(width, height, rotation, tuple(records), duration,
                                       str(stream.get('codec_name', '')), start, estimated, time_base,
                                       still_image_time, asset_identifier)
        return self._metadata

    def nearest_index(self, milliseconds: float) -> int:
        frames = self.metadata.frames
        seconds = max(0.0, float(milliseconds) / 1000)
        positions = [frame.pts_seconds for frame in frames]
        index = bisect_left(positions, seconds)
        if index == len(positions):
            return len(positions) - 1
        if index and seconds - positions[index - 1] <= positions[index] - seconds:
            return index - 1
        return index

    def _read(self, index: int, edge=None) -> Image.Image:
        metadata = self.open()
        if not isinstance(index, int) or not 0 <= index < metadata.frame_count:
            raise IndexError("视频帧编号超出范围。")
        self._verify_source()
        filters = [f'select=eq(n\\,{index})']
        if edge is not None:
            edge = max(64, min(4096, int(edge)))
            filters.append(f'scale=w=min({edge}\\,iw):h=min({edge}\\,ih):force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease')
        command = [self._tools.ffmpeg, '-nostdin', '-hide_banner', '-loglevel', 'error',
                   '-threads', '1', '-filter_threads', '1', '-protocol_whitelist', 'file,pipe',
                   '-i', str(self.path), '-map', '0:v:0', '-an', '-sn', '-dn', '-vf', ','.join(filters),
                   '-frames:v', '1', '-fps_mode', 'passthrough', '-c:v', 'png', '-f', 'image2pipe', 'pipe:1']
        content = self._run(command, MAX_IMAGE_BYTES)
        if not content:
            raise ValueError("未能读取所选视频帧。")
        with Image.open(io.BytesIO(content)) as decoded:
            if decoded.width * decoded.height > MAX_PIXELS:
                raise ValueError("视频画面尺寸超过读取上限。")
            image = decoded.convert('RGB')
            image.load()
        self._verify_source()
        return image

    def preview(self, index: int, edge=1200) -> Image.Image:
        return self._read(index, edge)

    def export(self, index: int) -> Image.Image:
        return self._read(index)

    def fork(self, *, cancel_event=None) -> "FrameReader":
        """Reuse this verified immutable index with independent decode state.

        The editor's preview cache may need another worker, but re-running
        ffprobe there would decode the video twice before its first preview.
        No process, cancellation generation or closed flag is shared.
        """
        self.open()
        self._verify_source()
        with self._lock:
            if self._closed:
                raise FrameCancelled("已取消视频读取。")
            metadata, signature, tools = self._metadata, self._signature, self._tools
        result = FrameReader(self.path, ffmpeg=self._tool_arguments[0], ffprobe=self._tool_arguments[1],
                             cancel_event=cancel_event)
        result._metadata = metadata
        result._signature = signature
        result._tools = tools
        return result

    def cancel_current(self):
        with self._lock:
            self._generation += 1
            process = self._process
        if process is not None:
            try:
                process.terminate()
            except OSError:
                pass

    def close(self):
        with self._lock:
            self._closed = True
        self.cancel_current()

    def __enter__(self):
        self.open()
        return self

    def __exit__(self, *_args):
        self.close()

"""Locate the private frame tools without loading their DLLs into Qt."""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import sys


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FrameTools:
    ffmpeg: Path
    ffprobe: Path


def resolve_frame_tools(ffmpeg=None, ffprobe=None) -> FrameTools:
    if ffmpeg is not None or ffprobe is not None:
        if ffmpeg is None or ffprobe is None:
            raise ValueError("ffmpeg 和 ffprobe 必须同时指定。")
        result = FrameTools(Path(ffmpeg), Path(ffprobe))
    elif getattr(sys, 'frozen', False):
        # The isolated CLI folder carries its matching FFmpeg DLLs. Qt keeps
        # its own media DLLs in PySide6; never merge the two library trees.
        base = Path(getattr(sys, '_MEIPASS', Path(sys.executable).parent / '_internal')) / 'tools' / 'ffmpeg'
        result = FrameTools(base / 'ffmpeg.exe', base / 'ffprobe.exe')
    elif sys.platform == 'win32':
        base = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'tools' / 'ffmpeg'
        result = FrameTools(base / 'ffmpeg.exe', base / 'ffprobe.exe')
    else:
        # Development only. The Windows portable application never depends on
        # the user's PATH, a Python installation, or a separately installed app.
        encoder = os.environ.get('LIVEVIEWER_FFMPEG') or shutil.which('ffmpeg')
        probe = os.environ.get('LIVEVIEWER_FFPROBE') or shutil.which('ffprobe')
        if not encoder or not probe:
            raise OSError("开发环境缺少 ffmpeg／ffprobe；Windows 绿色版自带读取组件。")
        result = FrameTools(Path(encoder), Path(probe))
    if not result.ffmpeg.is_file() or not result.ffprobe.is_file():
        raise OSError("视频逐帧读取组件缺失，请重新完整解压 LiveViewer。")
    return result

"""True HEVC HEIC export through an isolated, bundled encoder subprocess."""
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import time

from PIL import Image


def _executable() -> Path:
    name = 'LiveViewer.HeicEncoder' + ('.exe' if sys.platform == 'win32' else '')
    if getattr(sys, 'frozen', False):
        root = Path(getattr(sys, '_MEIPASS', Path(sys.executable).parent / '_internal')) / 'tools/heic'
    else:
        root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'runtime/tools/heic'
    path = root / name
    if not path.is_file():
        raise OSError('HEIC 保存组件缺失，请重新完整解压 LiveViewer。')
    return path


def encode_heic(image: Image.Image, destination: Path, *,
                cancel_event: threading.Event | None = None,
                exif: bytes | None = None) -> None:
    """Write genuine HEIC bytes; caller owns staging and final validation/commit."""
    if cancel_event is not None and cancel_event.is_set():
        raise InterruptedError('已取消 HEIC 保存。')
    if image.width < 1 or image.height < 1 or image.width * image.height > 100_000_000:
        raise ValueError('图片尺寸超出 HEIC 保存范围。')
    if exif is not None:
        if (not isinstance(exif, bytes) or not 14 <= len(exif) <= 60 * 1024
                or exif[:6] != b'Exif\x00\x00'
                or exif[6:10] not in (b'II*\x00', b'MM\x00*')):
            raise ValueError('HEIC EXIF 数据格式或长度无效。')
    rgb = image.convert('RGB')
    executable = _executable()
    pixels = rgb.tobytes()
    arguments = [str(executable), str(rgb.width), str(rgb.height), '95']
    if exif is not None:
        arguments.append(str(len(exif)))
        pixels += exif
    # stdout goes directly to the private stage, avoiding another encoded-file
    # copy in memory and preventing encoder diagnostics from becoming pixels.
    process = None
    created = False
    try:
        with Path(destination).open('xb') as target:
            created = True
            flags = subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW if sys.platform == 'win32' else 0
            process = subprocess.Popen(arguments,
                                       stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=target, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
                                       creationflags=flags)
            started = time.monotonic()
            first = True
            while True:
                if cancel_event is not None and cancel_event.is_set():
                    raise InterruptedError('已取消 HEIC 保存。')
                if time.monotonic() - started > 180:
                    raise TimeoutError('HEIC 编码超时，原图片未更改。')
                try:
                    _, errors = process.communicate(input=pixels if first else None, timeout=0.1)
                    break
                except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                    first = False
            if process.returncode:
                message = errors.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')[-2000:]
                raise OSError('HEIC 编码失败，原图片未更改。' + message)
            target.flush()
            if target.tell() < 32:
                raise OSError('HEIC 编码结果为空，原图片未更改。')
    except BaseException:
        if process is not None and process.poll() is None:
            process.kill()
            process.communicate()
        if created:
            Path(destination).unlink(missing_ok=True)
        raise

"""Image decoding independent of the UI, including portable HEIC support."""
from io import BytesIO
from pathlib import Path

from PIL import Image, ImageCms, ImageOps

try:
    from pi_heif import register_heif_opener
    register_heif_opener()
except ImportError:
    pass


def load_image(path: Path, max_edge: int | None = None) -> Image.Image:
    with Image.open(path) as source:
        source.seek(0)
        original_size = source.size
        profile = source.info.get("icc_profile")
        if max_edge and source.format == "JPEG":
            source.draft("RGB", (max_edge, max_edge))
        result = ImageOps.exif_transpose(source)
        if max_edge:
            result.thumbnail((max_edge, max_edge), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)
        if profile:
            try:
                result = ImageCms.profileToProfile(
                    result, ImageCms.ImageCmsProfile(BytesIO(profile)),
                    ImageCms.createProfile("sRGB"), outputMode="RGBA" if "A" in result.getbands() else "RGB")
            except (ValueError, OSError, TypeError, ImageCms.PyCMSError):
                pass
        if "A" in result.getbands() or result.mode == "P" and "transparency" in result.info:
            rgba = result.convert("RGBA")
            background = Image.new("RGB", result.size, "white")
            background.paste(rgba, mask=rgba.getchannel("A"))
            result = background
        else:
            result = result.convert("RGB")
        result = result.copy()
        result.info["original_size"] = original_size
        return result


def thumbnail_bytes(path: Path, edge: int = 256) -> bytes:
    if edge < 1:
        raise ValueError("Thumbnail edge must be positive")
    result = load_image(path, edge)
    stream = BytesIO()
    result.save(stream, format="JPEG", quality=84, optimize=False)
    return stream.getvalue()
