"""Small Windows portable UI. --process/--check-runtime never creates a window."""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
import importlib.metadata
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import queue
import sys
import threading
import traceback

from engine_round2 import Session, VERSION, runtime_root, tools
from methods import get_method


def state_directory():
    root = Path(sys.executable).parent if getattr(sys, 'frozen', False) else Path(__file__).parent
    return root / 'data'


def read_state():
    try:
        data = json.loads((state_directory() / 'last-session.json').read_text(encoding='utf8'))
        return data if isinstance(data, dict) else {}
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return {}


def save_state(data):
    try:
        directory = state_directory()
        directory.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        temporary = directory / 'last-session.tmp'
        temporary.write_text(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf8')
        temporary.replace(directory / 'last-session.json')
    except OSError:
        pass  # Remembering UI convenience never determines processing success.


def runtime_check():
    import tkinter
    from PIL import Image
    import pi_heif
    ffmpeg, ffprobe = tools()
    exiftool = runtime_root() / 'tools/exiftool/exiftool.exe'
    if not exiftool.is_file():
        raise OSError('ExifTool 元数据组件缺失，请完整解压工具包。')
    # Tcl interpreter only, no Tk window, focus change, or mouse operation.
    tcl = tkinter.Tcl()
    return {'status': 'passed', 'version': VERSION, 'frozen': bool(getattr(sys, 'frozen', False)),
            'python': sys.version, 'tcl': tcl.eval('info patchlevel'),
            'pillow': Image.__version__, 'pi_heif': importlib.metadata.version('pi-heif'),
            'ffmpeg': str(ffmpeg), 'ffprobe': str(ffprobe), 'exiftool': str(exiftool), 'gui_created': False}


def run_gui(method):
    import tkinter as tk
    from tkinter import ttk, filedialog, messagebox
    from PIL import Image, ImageTk

    class App:
        def __init__(self, root):
            self.root = root
            self.method = method
            self.session = None
            self.executor = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1, thread_name_prefix='live-photo-demo')
            self.events = queue.Queue()
            self.future = None
            self.cancel = threading.Event()
            self.job = None
            self.closing = False
            self.pending_preview = None
            self.preview_index = None
            self.index = 0
            self.debounce = None
            self.photo = None
            self.last_output = None
            self.state = read_state()
            self.output_parent = self.state.get('output_parent', '')
            if not isinstance(self.output_parent, str) or not Path(self.output_parent).is_dir():
                self.output_parent = ''
            root.title(method.title + ' · 保存修复1 · ' + VERSION)
            root.geometry('900x760')
            root.minsize(720, 620)
            root.option_add('*Font', ('Microsoft YaHei UI', 10))
            style = ttk.Style(root)
            if 'vista' in style.theme_names():
                style.theme_use('vista')
            outer = ttk.Frame(root, padding=18)
            outer.pack(fill='both', expand=True)
            ttk.Label(outer, text=method.title, font=('Microsoft YaHei UI', 17, 'bold')).pack(anchor='w')
            ttk.Label(outer, text=method.description, wraplength=830, foreground='#475569').pack(anchor='w', pady=(6, 14))
            bar = ttk.Frame(outer)
            bar.pack(fill='x')
            self.open_button = ttk.Button(bar, text='选择照片或 MOV…', command=self.select_file)
            self.open_button.pack(side='left')
            self.last_button = ttk.Button(bar, text='打开上一组', command=self.open_last)
            self.last_button.pack(side='left', padx=8)
            if not self.state.get('input'):
                self.last_button.state(['disabled'])
            self.source_label = ttk.Label(outer, text='选择同一文件夹内、同名的 HEIC/JPG + MOV。', wraplength=830)
            self.source_label.pack(anchor='w', pady=(10, 8))
            self.canvas = tk.Canvas(outer, background='#101827', highlightthickness=0, height=370)
            self.canvas.pack(fill='both', expand=True)
            self.canvas.create_text(350, 160, text='选择文件后，在这里预览关键帧', fill='#cbd5e1', tags='placeholder')
            self.canvas.bind('<Configure>', self.resize_preview)
            self.slider = ttk.Scale(outer, from_=0, to=1, command=self.slide)
            self.slider.pack(fill='x', pady=(12, 4))
            self.slider.state(['disabled'])
            row = ttk.Frame(outer)
            row.pack(fill='x')
            self.previous = ttk.Button(row, text='上一帧  ←', command=lambda: self.step(-1))
            self.previous.pack(side='left')
            self.next = ttk.Button(row, text='下一帧  →', command=lambda: self.step(1))
            self.next.pack(side='left', padx=8)
            self.frame_label = ttk.Label(row, text='尚未打开文件')
            self.frame_label.pack(side='right')
            output_row = ttk.Frame(outer)
            output_row.pack(fill='x', pady=(14, 4))
            self.output_button = ttk.Button(output_row, text='选择输出位置…', command=self.choose_output)
            self.output_button.pack(side='left')
            self.output_label = ttk.Label(output_row, text=self.output_parent or '首次保存时选择；每次自动建立独立文件夹。', wraplength=620)
            self.output_label.pack(side='left', padx=10)
            actions = ttk.Frame(outer)
            actions.pack(fill='x', pady=(8, 4))
            self.save_button = ttk.Button(actions, text='保存这张关键帧的实验副本', command=self.export)
            self.save_button.pack(side='left')
            self.folder_button = ttk.Button(actions, text='打开结果文件夹', command=self.open_output)
            self.folder_button.pack(side='left', padx=8)
            self.folder_button.state(['disabled'])
            self.progress = ttk.Progressbar(actions, mode='indeterminate', length=150)
            self.progress.pack(side='right')
            self.status = ttk.Label(outer, text='不覆盖原文件。结果需通过爱思单独导入验证。', wraplength=830)
            self.status.pack(anchor='w', pady=(8, 0))
            self.set_controls(False)
            root.bind('<Left>', lambda event: self.step(-1))
            root.bind('<Right>', lambda event: self.step(1))
            root.protocol('WM_DELETE_WINDOW', self.close)
            root.after(75, self.poll)

        def set_controls(self, enabled):
            state = ['!disabled'] if enabled else ['disabled']
            for control in (self.previous, self.next, self.slider):
                control.state(state)
            self.save_button.state(['!disabled'] if enabled and self.preview_index == self.index else ['disabled'])

        def submit(self, kind, action):
            self.job = kind
            self.progress.start(12)
            self.future = self.executor.submit(action)
            self.future.add_done_callback(lambda future: self.events.put((kind, future)))

        def select_file(self):
            if self.job or self.closing:
                return
            selected = filedialog.askopenfilename(title='选择同名照片和 MOV 中的一个文件',
                filetypes=[('Live Photo 文件', '*.heic *.HEIC *.jpg *.JPG *.jpeg *.JPEG *.mov *.MOV'), ('所有文件', '*.*')])
            if selected:
                self.open_file(selected)

        def open_last(self):
            if not self.job and self.state.get('input'):
                self.open_file(self.state['input'])

        def open_file(self, selected):
            if self.session:
                self.session.close()
            self.cancel = threading.Event()
            self.session = None
            self.preview_index = None
            self.preview_image = None
            self.photo = None
            self.canvas.delete('all')
            self.canvas.create_text(self.canvas.winfo_width() // 2, self.canvas.winfo_height() // 2,
                                    text='正在读取新文件…', fill='#cbd5e1', tags='placeholder')
            self.pending_preview = None
            self.set_controls(False)
            self.open_button.state(['disabled'])
            self.last_button.state(['disabled'])
            self.status.config(text='正在读取帧时间与配对信息…')
            self.submit('open', lambda: Session(selected, self.cancel))

        def slide(self, value):
            if self.session is None or self.job in ('open', 'save') or self.closing:
                return
            self.index = max(0, min(self.session.metadata.frame_count - 1, round(float(value))))
            frame = self.session.metadata.frames[self.index]
            self.frame_label.config(text=f'第 {self.index + 1} / {self.session.metadata.frame_count} 帧 · {float(frame.presentation_time):.6f} 秒')
            self.save_button.state(['disabled'])
            self.pending_preview = self.index
            if self.debounce:
                self.root.after_cancel(self.debounce)
            self.debounce = self.root.after(140, self.request_preview)

        def step(self, amount):
            if self.session is not None and self.job not in ('open', 'save') and not self.closing:
                self.slider.set(max(0, min(self.session.metadata.frame_count - 1, self.index + amount)))

        def request_preview(self):
            self.debounce = None
            if self.job or self.pending_preview is None or self.closing:
                return
            selected = self.pending_preview
            self.pending_preview = None
            self.submit('preview', lambda: (selected, self.session.preview(selected)))

        def resize_preview(self, event=None):
            if getattr(self, 'preview_image', None) is not None:
                image = self.preview_image.copy()
                image.thumbnail((max(1, self.canvas.winfo_width() - 12), max(1, self.canvas.winfo_height() - 12)), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)
                self.photo = ImageTk.PhotoImage(image)
                self.canvas.delete('all')
                self.canvas.create_image(self.canvas.winfo_width() // 2, self.canvas.winfo_height() // 2, image=self.photo)
            else:
                self.canvas.coords('placeholder', self.canvas.winfo_width() // 2, self.canvas.winfo_height() // 2)

        def choose_output(self):
            if self.job in ('save', 'open'):
                return False
            chosen = filedialog.askdirectory(title='选择保存结果的位置（自动建立新的子文件夹）', initialdir=self.output_parent or None)
            if chosen:
                self.output_parent = chosen
                self.output_label.config(text=chosen)
            return bool(chosen)

        def export(self):
            if self.job or self.session is None or self.preview_index != self.index:
                return
            if not self.output_parent and not self.choose_output():
                return
            selected = self.index
            self.set_controls(False)
            self.open_button.state(['disabled'])
            self.last_button.state(['disabled'])
            self.output_button.state(['disabled'])
            self.status.config(text='正在保存新封面、保留原机信息并校验配对；原文件保持不变…')
            self.submit('save', lambda: self.session.export(selected, method, Path(self.output_parent)))

        def open_output(self):
            if self.last_output and self.last_output.is_dir():
                os.startfile(self.last_output)

        def poll(self):
            try:
                kind, future = self.events.get_nowait()
            except queue.Empty:
                kind = None
            if kind:
                self.job = None
                self.progress.stop()
                try:
                    value = future.result()
                    if self.closing:
                        if kind == 'open':
                            value.close()
                    elif kind == 'open':
                        self.session = value
                        self.source_label.config(text=str(value.image))
                        self.slider.configure(to=max(1, value.metadata.frame_count - 1))
                        selected = 0
                        if self.state.get('input') == str(value.image) and type(self.state.get('frame')) is int:
                            selected = max(0, min(value.metadata.frame_count - 1, self.state['frame']))
                        self.set_controls(True)
                        self.slider.set(selected)
                        self.status.config(text='拖动时间轴或用左右键逐帧选择。保存后只导入新结果文件夹内的一组。')
                    elif kind == 'preview':
                        selected, picture = value
                        if selected == self.index:
                            self.preview_index = selected
                            self.preview_image = picture
                            self.resize_preview()
                            self.set_controls(True)
                    elif kind == 'save':
                        self.last_output, report = value
                        self.folder_button.state(['!disabled'])
                        self.state = {'input': str(self.session.image), 'frame': self.index, 'output_parent': self.output_parent}
                        save_state(self.state)
                        self.status.config(text='已保存并完成本地校验。请打开结果文件夹，用爱思单独导入这一组，确认 LIVE、长按播放和所选关键照片。')
                        self.set_controls(True)
                except BaseException as error:
                    if not self.closing:
                        self.status.config(text='处理失败：' + str(error))
                        messagebox.showerror('实验未完成', str(error), parent=self.root)
                        self.set_controls(self.session is not None)
                if not self.closing:
                    self.open_button.state(['!disabled'])
                    self.output_button.state(['!disabled'])
                    self.last_button.state(['!disabled'] if self.state.get('input') else ['disabled'])
                    if self.pending_preview is not None:
                        self.request_preview()
            if self.closing and not self.job:
                if self.session:
                    self.session.close()
                self.executor.shutdown(wait=False, cancel_futures=True)
                self.root.destroy()
                return
            self.root.after(75, self.poll)

        def close(self):
            if self.closing:
                return
            self.closing = True
            self.cancel.set()
            if self.session:
                self.session.reader.cancel_current()
            self.set_controls(False)
            self.status.config(text='正在停止后台读取并清理本次未完成输出…')

    root = tk.Tk()
    App(root)
    root.mainloop()


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument('--method', default=None)
    parser.add_argument('--check-runtime', action='store_true')
    parser.add_argument('--process', action='store_true')
    parser.add_argument('--input', type=Path)
    parser.add_argument('--frame', type=int)
    parser.add_argument('--output', type=Path)
    parser.add_argument('--report', type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if getattr(sys, 'frozen', False):
        method = get_method(Path(sys.executable).stem)
        if args.method is not None and args.method != method.code:
            parser.error('请用对应编号的程序执行该方法。')
    else:
        method = get_method(args.method or '08')
    if not args.check_runtime and not args.process:
        run_gui(method)
        return 0
    try:
        if args.check_runtime:
            result = runtime_check()
        else:
            if args.input is None or args.frame is None or args.output is None:
                raise ValueError('--process requires --input, --frame, --output.')
            session = Session(args.input)
            try:
                destination, report = session.export(args.frame, method, args.output)
                result = {'status': 'passed', 'output': str(destination), 'processing': report, 'gui_created': False}
            finally:
                session.close()
        code = 0
    except BaseException as error:
        result = {'status': 'failed', 'error': str(error), 'traceback': traceback.format_exc(), 'gui_created': False}
        code = 1
    if args.report:
        args.report.write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf8')
    elif sys.stdout:
        print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
    return code


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())

"""Synthetic-only tests of the independent ExifTool round-two writer."""
import argparse
import copy
from fractions import Fraction
import hashlib
import json
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path
import struct
import sys
import threading
import time
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch
import uuid

from PIL import Image, ExifTags
from PIL.TiffImagePlugin import IFDRational

LAB = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(LAB))
import engine_image as writer
from core.heic_encoder import encode_heic
from core.live_photo_movie import inspect_live_photo_movie, rewrite_live_photo_movie

RUN = LAB / 'reports' / ('image-artifacts-' + time.strftime('%Y%m%d-%H%M%S') + '-' + uuid.uuid4().hex[:8])
OLD = '11111111-2222-3333-4444-555555555555'
NEW = '66666666-7777-8888-9999-AAAAAAAAAAAA'
OPAQUE = b'opaque-synthetic-payload\0\x81\xff'
LONG8 = 8595185700
DATE, ZONE = '2025:02:03 04:05:06', '+08:00'
EVIDENCE = {'schema': 'live-photo-method-lab.exiftool-writer-tests.v1', 'private_media_used': False,
            'gui_started': False, 'iphone_import_verified': False, 'artifacts': str(RUN), 'images': [], 'movies': []}


def digest(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(Path(path).read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def make_note(identifier, *, unaligned=False):
    if len(identifier) != 36 or not identifier.isascii():
        raise ValueError('Synthetic fixture identifier must be 36 ASCII characters')
    values = [(1, 9, 1, struct.pack('>i', 14)),
              (8, 10, 3, struct.pack('>6i', 1, 3, -2, 5, 7, 11)),
              (17, 2, 37, identifier.encode('ascii') + b'\0'),
              (23, 16, 1, struct.pack('>Q', LONG8)), (65433, 7, len(OPAQUE), OPAQUE)]
    data = bytearray()
    entries = []
    base = 14 + 2 + 12 * len(values) + 4
    for tag, kind, amount, value in values:
        if len(value) <= 4:
            location = value.ljust(4, b'\0')
        else:
            if not unaligned and (base + len(data)) % 2:
                data += b'\0'
            location = struct.pack('>I', base + len(data))
            data += value
        entries.append(struct.pack('>HHI', tag, kind, amount) + location)
    return b'Apple iOS\0\0\1MM' + struct.pack('>H', len(values)) + b''.join(entries) + b'\0' * 4 + data


def synthetic_note(unaligned=False):
    return make_note(OLD, unaligned=unaligned)


def exif(note):
    value = Image.Exif()
    value[0x10f], value[0x110] = 'Apple', 'Synthetic test source'
    value[0x112] = 1
    value[0x8769] = {0x927c: note, 0x9003: DATE, 0x9011: ZONE, 0xA002: 96, 0xA003: 64}
    value[0x8825] = {0: b'\2\3\0\0', 1: 'N', 2: (25.0, 0.0, 0.0), 3: 'E', 4: (121.0, 0.0, 0.0)}
    return value


def complex_exif(*, with_gps_version=False, endian='>'):
    value = exif(synthetic_note())
    value.endian = endian
    value[0x131] = 'MUST NOT COPY source software'
    value[0x100], value[0x101] = 96, 64
    value[0x8769].update({0x9004: '2025:02:03 04:05:07', 0x9011: '+05:45',
        0x9012: '-03:30', 0x9291: '000012300', 0x9292: '987654321', 0x829a: IFDRational(1, 137)})
    value[0x8825] = {1: 'S', 2: (IFDRational(25), IFDRational(17), IFDRational(199999997, 10000000)),
        3: 'W', 4: (IFDRational(121), IFDRational(44), IFDRational(298765432, 10000000)),
        5: 1, 6: IFDRational(123456789, 1000000),
        7: (IFDRational(13), IFDRational(7), IFDRational(123456789, 10000000)),
        8: '07', 9: 'A', 10: '3', 11: IFDRational(7, 13),
        12: 'K', 13: IFDRational(77, 19), 14: 'T', 15: IFDRational(11, 37),
        16: 'M', 17: IFDRational(279234567, 1000000), 18: 'WGS-84',
        23: 'T', 24: IFDRational(292765431, 1000000),
        27: b'ASCII\0\0\0synthetic processing', 29: '2025:02:02', 30: 1, 31: IFDRational(31, 17)}
    if with_gps_version:
        value[0x8825][0] = b'\2\3\0\0'
    return value


def raw_exif(path):
    with Image.open(path) as image:
        raw = image.info.get('exif', b'')
        if image.format == 'HEIF':
            from pi_heif import open_heif
            raw = open_heif(path).info['exif']
    result = Image.Exif()
    result.load(raw)
    return result


def exact_values(value):
    if isinstance(value, IFDRational):
        return ('rational', value.numerator, value.denominator)
    if isinstance(value, (tuple, list)):
        return tuple(exact_values(item) for item in value)
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return {key: exact_values(item) for key, item in value.items()}
    return value


def tiff_entry(data, directory, tag):
    endian = '<' if data[:2] == b'II' else '>'
    count = struct.unpack_from(endian + 'H', data, directory)[0]
    for offset in range(directory + 2, directory + 2 + count * 12, 12):
        if struct.unpack_from(endian + 'H', data, offset)[0] == tag:
            return offset
    raise AssertionError(f'missing synthetic tag {tag:x}')


class EngineImageTests(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        RUN.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
        cls.originals = {}
        cls.image = Image.new('RGB', (96, 64), '#aa3388')
        cls.pixels = Image.new('RGB', (80, 120), '#226688')
        cls.sources = {}
        for kind in ('JPEG', 'HEIC'):
            path = RUN / ('original' + ('.JPG' if kind == 'JPEG' else '.HEIC'))
            if kind == 'JPEG':
                cls.image.save(path, 'JPEG', quality=100, exif=exif(synthetic_note()).tobytes())
            else:
                encode_heic(cls.image, path, exif=exif(synthetic_note()).tobytes())
            cls.sources[kind] = path
            cls.originals[str(path)] = digest(path)
        cls.plain_movie = Path(r'C:\Example\LiveViewer\tests\fixtures\Live_HEVC.MOV')
        cls.movie = RUN / 'original.MOV'
        rewrite_live_photo_movie(cls.plain_movie, cls.movie, OLD, Fraction(0))
        cls.originals[str(cls.movie)] = digest(cls.movie)

    @classmethod
    def tearDownClass(cls):
        if {path: digest(path) for path in cls.originals} != cls.originals:
            raise AssertionError('Synthetic input unexpectedly changed')
        EVIDENCE['source_sha256'] = cls.originals
        EVIDENCE['sources_unchanged'] = True

    def output(self, suffix):
        return RUN / (self.id().split('.')[-1] + '-' + uuid.uuid4().hex[:8] + suffix)

    def test_jpeg_and_heic_sources_to_both_formats_keep_every_nonid_makernote_field(self):
        for source_kind, source in self.sources.items():
            for output_kind in ('JPEG', 'HEIC'):
                with self.subTest(source=source_kind, output=output_kind):
                    output = self.output('.JPG' if output_kind == 'JPEG' else '.HEIC')
                    result = writer.write_cover(source, self.pixels, output, NEW, output_kind)
                    self.assertTrue(result['typed_all_nonid_values_equal'])
                    self.assertTrue(result['capture_tags_preserved'])
                    self.assertTrue(result['pixels_after_metadata_write_identical'])
                    self.assertEqual(result['asset_identifier'], NEW)
                    raw, parsed = writer._read_note(output, None)
                    self.assertEqual(parsed[1][23], (16, 1, struct.pack('>Q', LONG8)))
                    self.assertEqual(parsed[1][65433], (7, len(OPAQUE), OPAQUE))
                    self.assertEqual(parsed[1][17], (2, 37, NEW.encode() + b'\0'))
                    self.assertEqual(result['output_sha256'], digest(output))
                    with Image.open(output) as decoded:
                        self.assertEqual(decoded.size, self.pixels.size)
                        self.assertEqual(decoded.format, 'JPEG' if output_kind == 'JPEG' else 'HEIF')
                    properties = json.loads(writer._run(['-j', '-n', '-Make', '-Model', '-DateTimeOriginal',
                        '-OffsetTimeOriginal', '-GPSLatitude', '-GPSLongitude', '-Orientation', '-ModifyDate', output]))[0]
                    self.assertEqual(properties['DateTimeOriginal'], DATE)
                    self.assertEqual(properties['OffsetTimeOriginal'], ZONE)
                    self.assertEqual(properties['GPSLatitude'], 25)
                    self.assertEqual(properties['GPSLongitude'], 121)
                    self.assertEqual(properties['Orientation'], 1)
                    self.assertIn('ModifyDate', properties)
                    EVIDENCE['images'].append({'source_format': source_kind, 'output_format': output_kind,
                                               'path': str(output), 'verification': result, 'properties': properties})

    def test_alignment_rewrite_reports_physical_difference_but_preserves_typed_values(self):
        source, target = self.output('.JPG'), self.output('.JPG')
        self.image.save(source, 'JPEG', exif=exif(synthetic_note(unaligned=True)))
        before = digest(source)
        result = writer.write_cover(source, self.pixels, target, NEW, 'JPEG')
        self.assertTrue(result['typed_all_nonid_values_equal'])
        self.assertFalse(result['physical_equal_except_identifier'])
        self.assertEqual(digest(source), before)

    def test_precise_capture_without_gps_version_preserves_all_rationals_and_optional_fields(self):
        for source_kind in ('JPEG', 'HEIC'):
            for output_kind in ('JPEG', 'HEIC'):
                with self.subTest(source=source_kind, output=output_kind):
                    source = RUN / ('precise-source-' + uuid.uuid4().hex[:8] + ('.JPG' if source_kind == 'JPEG' else '.HEIC'))
                    raw = complex_exif().tobytes()
                    if source_kind == 'JPEG':
                        self.image.save(source, 'JPEG', exif=raw)
                    else:
                        encode_heic(self.image, source, exif=raw)
                    before, original = digest(source), raw_exif(source)
                    target = RUN / ('precise-target-' + uuid.uuid4().hex[:8] + ('.JPG' if output_kind == 'JPEG' else '.HEIC'))
                    result = writer.write_cover(source, self.pixels, target, NEW, output_kind)
                    final = raw_exif(target)
                    self.assertEqual(exact_values(final.get_ifd(0x8825)), exact_values(original.get_ifd(0x8825)))
                    self.assertNotIn(0, final.get_ifd(0x8825))
                    for key in (0x9003, 0x9004, 0x9011, 0x9012, 0x9291, 0x9292):
                        self.assertEqual(final.get_ifd(0x8769)[key], original.get_ifd(0x8769)[key])
                    self.assertEqual((final[0x10f], final[0x110]), (original[0x10f], original[0x110]))
                    self.assertEqual(final[0x112], 1)
                    self.assertEqual((final.get_ifd(0x8769)[0xa002], final.get_ifd(0x8769)[0xa003]), self.pixels.size)
                    self.assertNotIn(0x131, final)
                    self.assertNotIn(0x100, final)
                    self.assertNotIn(0x101, final)
                    self.assertNotIn(0x829a, final.get_ifd(0x8769))
                    self.assertFalse(final.get_ifd(ExifTags.IFD.IFD1))
                    self.assertTrue(result['capture_tags_preserved'])
                    self.assertTrue(result['typed_all_nonid_values_equal'])
                    self.assertEqual(digest(source), before)

    def test_little_endian_capture_and_absent_optional_groups(self):
        for present in (False, True):
            with self.subTest(gps_present=present):
                value = complex_exif(with_gps_version=True, endian='<')
                if not present:
                    del value[0x8825]
                    for tag in (0x9004, 0x9012, 0x9291, 0x9292):
                        del value[0x8769][tag]
                source = RUN / ('little-source-' + str(present) + '.jpg')
                target = RUN / ('little-target-' + str(present) + '.jpg')
                self.image.save(source, 'JPEG', exif=value.tobytes())
                before = writer._capture_raw(source, None)
                self.assertEqual(before['endian'], '<')
                result = writer.write_cover(source, self.pixels, target, NEW, 'JPEG')
                self.assertEqual(writer._capture_raw(target, None), before)
                self.assertTrue(result['capture_typed_raw_fields_preserved'])

    def test_raw_capture_parser_rejects_ambiguous_and_damaged_directories(self):
        original = complex_exif().tobytes()[6:]
        root = struct.unpack_from('>I', original, 4)[0]
        gps_pointer = tiff_entry(original, root, 0x8825)
        gps = struct.unpack_from('>I', original, gps_pointer + 8)[0]
        altitude = tiff_entry(original, gps, 6)
        count = struct.unpack_from('>H', original, gps)[0]
        variants = {}
        data = bytearray(original)
        struct.pack_into('>I', data, gps_pointer + 8, root)
        variants['cycle'] = bytes(data)
        data = bytearray(original)
        data[gps + 2 + (count - 1) * 12:gps + 4 + (count - 1) * 12] = data[gps + 2:gps + 4]
        variants['duplicate'] = bytes(data)
        data = bytearray(original)
        struct.pack_into('>I', data, altitude + 8, len(data) + 64)
        variants['outside'] = bytes(data)
        data = bytearray(original)
        location = struct.unpack_from('>I', data, altitude + 8)[0]
        struct.pack_into('>I', data, location + 4, 0)
        variants['zero-denominator'] = bytes(data)
        data = bytearray(original)
        struct.pack_into('>H', data, altitude + 2, 13)
        variants['gps-pointer'] = bytes(data)
        data = bytearray(original)
        struct.pack_into('>H', data, 2, 43)
        variants['bigtiff'] = bytes(data)
        for label, content in variants.items():
            with self.subTest(case=label):
                path = RUN / ('damaged-' + label + '.jpg')
                self.image.save(path, 'JPEG', exif=b'Exif\0\0' + content)
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    writer._capture_raw(path, None)

    def test_capture_changes_report_only_field_names_and_reject_any_loss(self):
        source = RUN / 'exact-change-source.jpg'
        self.image.save(source, 'JPEG', exif=complex_exif().tobytes())
        original = writer._capture_raw(source, None)
        variants = []
        changed = copy.deepcopy(original)
        kind, count, value = changed['GPS'][6]
        changed['GPS'][6] = (kind, count, value[:-1] + bytes([value[-1] ^ 1]))
        variants.append((changed, 'GPSAltitude'))
        missing = copy.deepcopy(original)
        del missing['GPS'][17]
        variants.append((missing, 'GPSImgDirection'))
        added = copy.deepcopy(original)
        added['GPS'][0] = (1, 4, b'\2\3\0\0')
        variants.append((added, 'GPSVersionID'))
        for altered, name in variants:
            with self.subTest(field=name), self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, name) as failure:
                writer._verify_capture_raw(original, altered)
            self.assertNotIn('123456789', str(failure.exception))
            self.assertNotIn('279234567', str(failure.exception))

    def test_raw_capture_rejects_partially_overlapping_directory_tables(self):
        data = b'MM' + struct.pack('>HIH', 42, 8, 1)
        data += struct.pack('>HHII', 0x8769, 4, 1, 22) + b'\0' * 6
        with patch.object(writer, '_read_exif', return_value=data):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, '重叠'):
                writer._capture_raw(RUN / 'not-read.jpg', None)

    def test_raw_capture_and_builder_reject_excessive_shared_payload_budget(self):
        count, amount, gps = 4096, 65536, 26
        payload_start = gps + 2 + count * 12 + 4
        data = b'MM' + struct.pack('>HIH', 42, 8, 1)
        data += struct.pack('>HHII', 0x8825, 4, 1, gps) + b'\0' * 4
        data += struct.pack('>H', count)
        data += b''.join(struct.pack('>HHII', 0x1000 + index, 7, amount, payload_start) for index in range(count))
        data += b'\0' * 4 + b'x' * amount
        with patch.object(writer, '_read_exif', return_value=data):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, '累计'):
                writer._capture_raw(RUN / 'not-read.jpg', None)
        capture = {'endian': '>', 'IFD0': {}, 'ExifIFD': {}, 'GPS': {0xff00: (7, amount, b'x' * amount)}}
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, '范围'):
            writer._capture_block(capture, synthetic_note())

    def test_new_cover_does_not_copy_original_thumbnail_or_exposure(self):
        raw = bytearray(complex_exif().tobytes()[6:])
        root = struct.unpack_from('>I', raw, 4)[0]
        root_count = struct.unpack_from('>H', raw, root)[0]
        ifd1 = len(raw)
        struct.pack_into('>I', raw, root + 2 + root_count * 12, ifd1)
        thumbnail = b'\xff\xd8synthetic-old-thumbnail\xff\xd9'
        thumbnail_offset = ifd1 + 2 + 3 * 12 + 4
        raw += struct.pack('>H', 3)
        raw += struct.pack('>HHI', 0x103, 3, 1) + struct.pack('>H', 6) + b'\0\0'
        raw += struct.pack('>HHII', 0x201, 4, 1, thumbnail_offset)
        raw += struct.pack('>HHII', 0x202, 4, 1, len(thumbnail))
        raw += b'\0' * 4 + thumbnail
        source, output = RUN / 'thumbnail-source.jpg', RUN / 'thumbnail-target.jpg'
        self.image.save(source, 'JPEG', exif=b'Exif\0\0' + raw)
        self.assertTrue(raw_exif(source).get_ifd(ExifTags.IFD.IFD1))
        result = writer.write_cover(source, self.pixels, output, NEW, 'JPEG')
        final = raw_exif(output)
        self.assertFalse(final.get_ifd(ExifTags.IFD.IFD1))
        self.assertNotIn(b'synthetic-old-thumbnail', output.read_bytes())
        self.assertNotIn(0x829a, final.get_ifd(0x8769))
        self.assertTrue(result['capture_typed_raw_fields_preserved'])

    def test_long8_is_external_eight_bytes_not_inline_one_byte(self):
        raw = synthetic_note()
        _, fields, locations = writer._note(raw)
        self.assertEqual(fields[23], (16, 1, struct.pack('>Q', LONG8)))
        self.assertEqual(locations[23][1], 8)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(locations[23][0], 14 + 2 + 4 * 12 + 4)

    def test_changed_nonid_field_is_rejected(self):
        original = synthetic_note()
        altered = bytearray(original)
        start, size = writer._note(original)[2][23]
        altered[start + size - 1] ^= 1
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, '非配对标识'):
            writer._verify_note((original, writer._note(original)), (bytes(altered), writer._note(altered)), NEW)

    def test_invalid_or_missing_makernotes_never_publishes(self):
        for data in (b'', b'Apple iOS\0\0\1MM' + b'\xff' * 12):
            with self.subTest(data=data):
                source, target = self.output('.JPG'), self.output('.JPG')
                self.image.save(source, 'JPEG', exif=exif(data))
                with self.assertRaises((ValueError, OSError)):
                    writer.write_cover(source, self.pixels, target, NEW, 'JPEG')
                self.assertFalse(target.exists())

    def test_existing_destination_and_invalid_uuid_preserve_every_file(self):
        target = self.output('.JPG')
        target.write_bytes(b'preserve-existing')
        with self.assertRaises(FileExistsError):
            writer.write_cover(self.sources['JPEG'], self.pixels, target, NEW, 'JPEG')
        self.assertEqual(target.read_bytes(), b'preserve-existing')
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            writer.write_cover(self.sources['JPEG'], self.pixels, self.output('.JPG'), 'not-uuid', 'JPEG')

    def test_pre_cancel_and_copy_id_failure_leave_no_destination(self):
        target = self.output('.JPG')
        event = threading.Event(); event.set()
        with self.assertRaises(InterruptedError):
            writer.write_cover(self.sources['JPEG'], self.pixels, target, NEW, 'JPEG', event)
        self.assertFalse(target.exists())
        actual = writer._run
        def fail_edit(arguments, cancel_event=None):
            if any(str(arg).startswith('-Apple:ContentIdentifier=') for arg in arguments):
                raise OSError('synthetic second-step failure')
            return actual(arguments, cancel_event)
        with patch.object(writer, '_run', side_effect=fail_edit):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(OSError, 'second-step'):
                writer.write_cover(self.sources['JPEG'], self.pixels, target, NEW, 'JPEG')
        self.assertFalse(target.exists())
        self.assertFalse(list(RUN.glob('._lpimage-*')))

    def test_copy_movie_changes_only_identifier_and_preserves_marker_tracks_and_samples(self):
        output = self.output('.MOV')
        result = writer.copy_movie_with_identifier(self.movie, output, NEW)
        before, after = inspect_live_photo_movie(self.movie), inspect_live_photo_movie(output)
        self.assertEqual(after.asset_identifier, NEW)
        self.assertEqual((before.photo_time, before.marker_count, before.track_count),
                         (after.photo_time, after.marker_count, after.track_count))
        self.assertTrue(result['all_track_samples_and_times_preserved'])
        self.assertEqual(result['output_sha256'], digest(output))
        EVIDENCE['movies'].append({'path': str(output), 'verification': result})

    def test_movie_copy_preserves_five_track_mixed_metadata_and_auxv(self):
        from test_engine_movie_round2 import native_source
        for auxiliary_video in (False, True):
            with self.subTest(auxv=auxiliary_video):
                source, output = self.output('.MOV'), self.output('.MOV')
                native_source(source)
                if auxiliary_video:
                    source.write_bytes(source.read_bytes().replace(b'soun', b'auxv', 1))
                before_bytes = source.read_bytes()
                before = writer.movie._load(source)
                self.assertEqual(len(before.tracks), 5)
                self.assertTrue(any(track.marker_ids and len(track.keys) > 1 for track in before.tracks))
                result = writer.copy_movie_with_identifier(source, output, NEW)
                after = writer.movie._load(output)
                self.assertEqual(source.read_bytes(), before_bytes)
                self.assertEqual(len(after.tracks), 5)
                self.assertEqual([track.handler for track in after.tracks], [track.handler for track in before.tracks])
                self.assertEqual(inspect_live_photo_movie(output).asset_identifier, NEW)
                self.assertTrue(result['all_track_samples_and_times_preserved'])
                self.assertTrue(result['track_properties_except_chunk_offsets_preserved'])
                EVIDENCE['movies'].append({'synthetic_five_tracks': True, 'auxv': auxiliary_video,
                                           'path': str(output), 'verification': result})

    def test_set_identifier_on_new_stage_preserves_stage_on_tool_failure(self):
        stage = self.output('.MOV')
        stage.write_bytes(self.movie.read_bytes())
        before = digest(stage)
        with patch.object(writer, '_run', side_effect=OSError('synthetic tool failure')):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(OSError, 'tool failure'):
                writer.set_movie_identifier(stage, NEW)
        self.assertEqual(digest(stage), before)
        result = writer.set_movie_identifier(stage, NEW)
        self.assertEqual(inspect_live_photo_movie(stage).asset_identifier, NEW)
        self.assertEqual(result['output_sha256'], digest(stage))

    def test_set_identifier_can_create_key_on_new_unpaired_movie_without_timed_changes(self):
        stage = self.output('.MOV')
        stage.write_bytes(self.plain_movie.read_bytes())
        before = inspect_live_photo_movie(stage)
        result = writer.set_movie_identifier(stage, NEW)
        after = inspect_live_photo_movie(stage)
        self.assertEqual(after.asset_identifier, NEW)
        self.assertEqual((after.marker_count, after.photo_time, after.track_count),
                         (before.marker_count, before.photo_time, before.track_count))
        self.assertTrue(result['all_track_samples_and_times_preserved'])

    def test_unicode_space_and_emoji_paths_cover_jpeg_heic_and_movie_id_writes(self):
        folder = RUN / '中文 空格 📷'
        source_folder, output_folder = folder / '来源 🧪', folder / '未完成 输出 🎬'
        source_folder.mkdir(parents=True)
        output_folder.mkdir()
        before = {}
        for source_kind, original in self.sources.items():
            source = source_folder / ('源图片 ' + source_kind + ' 😺' + original.suffix)
            source.write_bytes(original.read_bytes())
            before[source] = digest(source)
            for output_kind in ('JPEG', 'HEIC'):
                output = output_folder / (source_kind + ' 新封面 🎉' + ('.JPG' if output_kind == 'JPEG' else '.HEIC'))
                result = writer.write_cover(source, self.pixels, output, NEW, output_kind)
                self.assertTrue(result['typed_all_nonid_values_equal'])
                self.assertTrue(result['capture_tags_preserved'])
                self.assertEqual(writer._read_note(output, None)[1][1][17], (2, 37, NEW.encode() + b'\0'))
                self.assertEqual(result['output_sha256'], digest(output))
        source_movie = source_folder / '原视频 空格 🧪.MOV'
        source_movie.write_bytes(self.movie.read_bytes())
        before[source_movie] = digest(source_movie)
        output_movie = output_folder / '新副本 🎞.MOV'
        result = writer.copy_movie_with_identifier(source_movie, output_movie, NEW)
        self.assertTrue(result['all_track_samples_and_times_preserved'])
        self.assertEqual(inspect_live_photo_movie(output_movie).asset_identifier, NEW)
        stage = output_folder / '新暂存 🔒.MOV'
        stage.write_bytes(output_movie.read_bytes())
        second_id = 'BBBBBBBB-CCCC-DDDD-EEEE-FFFFFFFFFFFF'
        result = writer.set_movie_identifier(stage, second_id)
        self.assertEqual(inspect_live_photo_movie(stage).asset_identifier, second_id)
        self.assertTrue(result['all_track_samples_and_times_preserved'])
        self.assertEqual({path: digest(path) for path in before}, before)
        EVIDENCE['unicode_paths'] = {'source_directory': str(source_folder),
            'output_directory': str(output_folder), 'image_cases': 4, 'movie_cases': 2, 'sources_unchanged': True}

    def test_exiftool_argfile_rejects_line_and_nul_injection_before_process(self):
        for value in ('a\n-DeleteOriginal', 'a\r-o', 'a\0b'):
            with self.subTest(value=value), patch.object(writer.subprocess, 'Popen') as create:
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    writer._run(['-j', value])
                create.assert_not_called()

    def test_exiftool_argfile_sends_utf8_only_once_across_communicate_timeouts(self):
        class Process:
            returncode = 0
            def __init__(self):
                self.calls = []
            def communicate(self, **kwargs):
                self.calls.append(kwargs)
                if len(self.calls) == 1:
                    raise subprocess.TimeoutExpired('synthetic', .1)
                return b'{}', b''
            def poll(self):
                return 0
        process = Process()
        arguments = ['-j', 'C:/Example/中文 空格/emoji 📷.jpg']
        with patch.object(writer.subprocess, 'Popen', return_value=process) as create:
            self.assertEqual(writer._run(arguments), b'{}')
        self.assertEqual(create.call_args.args[0][1:], ['-charset', 'filename=UTF8', '-@', '-'])
        self.assertEqual(create.call_args.kwargs['stdin'], subprocess.PIPE)
        self.assertEqual(process.calls[0]['input'], ('\n'.join(arguments) + '\n').encode('utf8'))
        self.assertIsNone(process.calls[1]['input'])


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument('--report', type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--test', action='append', default=[])
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.report.exists():
        parser.error('use a fresh report path')
    before = {str(path): digest(path) for path in (LAB/'engine_image.py', Path(__file__))}
    suite = (unittest.defaultTestLoader.loadTestsFromNames(args.test) if args.test
             else unittest.defaultTestLoader.loadTestsFromTestCase(EngineImageTests))
    result = unittest.TextTestRunner(verbosity=2).run(suite)
    after = {path: digest(path) for path in before}
    EVIDENCE.update(tests_run=result.testsRun, failures=len(result.failures), errors=len(result.errors),
        skipped=len(result.skipped), success=result.wasSuccessful(), source_sha256_before=before,
        source_sha256_after=after, code_stable=before == after)
    args.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with args.report.open('x', encoding='utf8') as stream:
        json.dump(EVIDENCE, stream, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    return 0 if result.wasSuccessful() and before == after else 1


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())

"""Synthetic BMFF fixtures, plus the repository's generated H.264/HEVC clips.

All fixture bytes below are generated here, not copied from personal media.
The builder deliberately does not import the writer's atom/table helpers.
"""
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, str(__import__("pathlib").Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
from fractions import Fraction
import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
import struct
import tempfile
import threading
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

from core import live_photo_movie as movie
from engine_movie_round2 import rewrite_original_marker
from core.live_photo_movie import inspect_live_photo_movie, video_presentation_times


def box(kind, data=b"", extended=False):
    if extended:
        return struct.pack(">I4sQ", 1, kind, len(data) + 16) + data
    return struct.pack(">I4s", len(data) + 8, kind) + data


def full(kind, data=b"", version=0, flags=0):
    return box(kind, bytes([version]) + flags.to_bytes(3, "big") + data)


def integer(value):
    return struct.pack(">I", value)


def split_atoms(data, offset=0):
    atoms = []
    while offset < len(data):
        length, kind = struct.unpack_from(">I4s", data, offset)
        head = 8
        if length == 1:
            length = struct.unpack_from(">Q", data, offset + 8)[0]
            head = 16
        if not length:
            length = len(data) - offset
        assert length >= head and offset + length <= len(data)
        atoms.append((kind, data[offset + head:offset + length], data[offset:offset + length], offset))
        offset += length
    assert offset == len(data)
    return atoms


MATRIX = struct.pack(">9I", 0x10000, 0, 0, 0, 0x10000, 0, 0, 0, 0x40000000)
KEY = b"com.apple.quicktime.still-image-time"
ID_KEY = b"com.apple.quicktime.content.identifier"


def sample_entry(handler, metadata_keys=None, *, legacy_mebx=False, reference_index=1):
    header = b"\0" * 6 + struct.pack(">H", reference_index)
    if handler == b"meta":
        keys = b"".join(box(integer(i), box(b"keyd", b"mdta" + name)
                        + box(b"dtyp", struct.pack(">II", 0, dtype))) for i, name, dtype in metadata_keys)
        # QTFF standard sample description header is 16 bytes INCLUDING size
        # and format: only reserved[6]+data-reference-index[2] in the payload.
        return box(b"mebx", header + (b"\0" * 8 if legacy_mebx else b"") + box(b"keys", keys))
    return box(b"avc1" if handler == b"vide" else b"mp4a", header + b"synthetic-codec-description")


def track(track_id, handler, samples, offset, *, scale=10, deltas=None, co64=False,
          ctts=None, edit=None, metadata_keys=None, extended=False, dref_atom=None,
          legacy_mebx=False, reference_index=1, track_duration=1000):
    deltas = deltas or [1] * len(samples)
    duration = sum(deltas)
    tkhd = full(b"tkhd", struct.pack(">IIIII", 101, 102, track_id, 0, track_duration)
                + b"\0" * 16 + MATRIX + struct.pack(">II", 16 << 16, 16 << 16), flags=3)
    mdhd = full(b"mdhd", struct.pack(">IIIIHH", 101, 102, scale, duration, 0, 0))
    hdlr = full(b"hdlr", b"\0" * 4 + handler + b"\0" * 12 + b"Synthetic\0")
    stsd = full(b"stsd", integer(1) + sample_entry(handler, metadata_keys,
                                                legacy_mebx=legacy_mebx, reference_index=reference_index))
    stts = full(b"stts", integer(len(deltas)) + b"".join(struct.pack(">II", 1, n) for n in deltas))
    co = full(b"co64" if co64 else b"stco", integer(1) + struct.pack(">Q" if co64 else ">I", offset))
    stsz = full(b"stsz", integer(0) + integer(len(samples)) + b"".join(integer(len(s)) for s in samples))
    stsc = full(b"stsc", integer(1) + struct.pack(">III", 1, len(samples), 1))
    composition = full(b"ctts", integer(len(ctts)) + b"".join(struct.pack(">Ii", 1, n) for n in ctts), version=1) if ctts else b""
    stbl = box(b"stbl", stsd + stts + composition + stsc + stsz + co)
    dref = box(b"dinf", full(b"dref", integer(1) + full(b"url ", flags=1)) if dref_atom is None else dref_atom)
    mdia = box(b"mdia", mdhd + hdlr + box(b"minf", full(b"nmhd") + dref + stbl))
    edts = b""
    if edit:
        version, edits = edit
        elst = full(b"elst", integer(len(edits)) + b"".join(struct.pack(">Qqhh" if version else ">Iihh", d, t, 1, 0)
                                                              for d, t in edits), version=version)
        edts = box(b"edts", elst)
    return box(b"trak", tkhd + edts + mdia, extended)


def metadata(identifier, *, iso=False):
    return box(b"meta", (b"\0" * 4 if iso else b"")
               + full(b"hdlr", b"\0" * 4 + b"mdta" + b"\0" * 12 + b"\0")
               + full(b"keys", integer(2) + box(b"mdta", ID_KEY) + box(b"mdta", b"com.apple.quicktime.creationdate"))
               + box(b"ilst", box(integer(1), box(b"data", integer(1) + integer(0) + identifier.encode()))
                     + box(integer(2), box(b"data", integer(1) + integer(0) + b"2020-01-02T03:04:05+0800"))))


def make_movie(path, *, co64=False, extended=False, mixed=False, marker_only=False,
               ctts=None, edit=None, movie_scale=1000, movie_duration=1000, dual_identifier=None,
               video_scale=10, deltas=(2, 3, 5), front_moov=False, zero_mdat=False,
               extra_metadata_key=b"com.example.synthetic", dref_atom=None,
               legacy_mebx=False, reference_index=1, other_metadata_tracks=False):
    video = [b"video-sample-0", b"second-video-sample", b"third-video-sample"]
    audio = [b"audio-sample-0", b"audio-sample-1"]
    meta_samples = []
    if mixed:
        meta_samples = [box(integer(2), b"first\0"), box(integer(1), b"\xff") + box(integer(2), b"second\0")]
    elif marker_only:
        meta_samples = [box(integer(1), b"\xff")]
    auxiliary = ([box(integer(7), b"synthetic-a0"), box(integer(7), b"synthetic-a1")],
                 [box(integer(9), b"synthetic-b0"), box(integer(9), b"synthetic-b1")]) if other_metadata_tracks else ()
    ftyp = box(b"ftyp", b"qt  " + integer(0) + b"qt  ")
    mdat_payload = b"".join(video + audio + meta_samples + [sample for samples in auxiliary for sample in samples])
    mvhd = full(b"mvhd", struct.pack(">IIII", 101, 102, movie_scale, movie_duration)
                + struct.pack(">IHH", 0x10000, 0x100, 0) + b"\0" * 8 + MATRIX + b"\0" * 24 + integer(4))

    def movie(offset):
        tracks = track(1, b"vide", video, offset, deltas=list(deltas), scale=video_scale, ctts=ctts,
                       edit=edit, co64=co64, extended=extended, dref_atom=dref_atom, reference_index=reference_index,
                       track_duration=movie_duration)
        tracks += track(2, b"soun", audio, offset + sum(map(len, video)), co64=co64,
                        dref_atom=dref_atom, reference_index=reference_index)
        if meta_samples:
            keys = [(1, KEY, 65)] + ([(2, extra_metadata_key, 1)] if mixed else [])
            tracks += track(3, b"meta", meta_samples, offset + sum(map(len, video + audio)),
                            deltas=[2, 1] if mixed else [1], metadata_keys=keys, co64=co64,
                            dref_atom=dref_atom, legacy_mebx=legacy_mebx, reference_index=reference_index)
        extra_offset = offset + sum(map(len, video + audio + meta_samples))
        for index, samples in enumerate(auxiliary):
            key_id = (7, 9)[index]
            tracks += track(4 + index, b"meta", samples, extra_offset, deltas=[2 + index, 1],
                            metadata_keys=[(key_id, b"com.example.synthetic." + str(index).encode(), 1)],
                            co64=co64, dref_atom=dref_atom, reference_index=reference_index, legacy_mebx=legacy_mebx)
            extra_offset += sum(map(len, samples))
        # QuickTime moov/meta has no FullBox prefix; udta/meta follows the
        # commonly used ISO FullBox form. ExifTool parses these independently.
        tags = metadata("old-identifier") if dual_identifier is not None else b""
        if dual_identifier is not None:
            tags += box(b"udta", metadata(dual_identifier, iso=True))
        return box(b"moov", mvhd + tracks + tags, extended)

    mdat_header = 16 if extended else 8
    size = len(movie(0)) if front_moov else 0
    moov = movie(len(ftyp) + size + mdat_header)
    mdat = box(b"mdat", mdat_payload, extended)
    if zero_mdat:
        assert front_moov and not extended
        mdat = b"\0" * 4 + mdat[4:]
    path.write_bytes(ftyp + (moov + mdat if front_moov else mdat + moov))
    return video, audio


def child(data, kind):
    matches = [p for k, p, *_ in split_atoms(data) if k == kind]
    assert len(matches) == 1
    return matches[0]


def tracks(path):
    return [p for k, p, *_ in split_atoms(child(path.read_bytes(), b"moov")) if k == b"trak"]


def marked_track(path):
    found = []
    for raw in tracks(path):
        mdia = child(raw, b"mdia")
        if child(mdia, b"hdlr")[8:12] != b"meta":
            continue
        stbl = child(child(mdia, b"minf"), b"stbl")
        entry = split_atoms(child(stbl, b"stsd"), 8)[0]
        keys = child(entry[1][8:], b"keys")
        if any(child(p, b"keyd") == b"mdta" + KEY for _, p, *_ in split_atoms(keys)):
            found.append((raw, mdia, stbl, entry))
    assert len(found) == 1
    return found[0]


def replace_child(raw, kind, replacement):
    return b''.join(replacement if k == kind else entire for k,_,entire,_ in split_atoms(raw))


def native_source(path, *, mixed=True, auxiliaries=True, front=False, marker_id=7, clock=1000):
    """Independent synthetic native-style shell; no private payload bytes."""
    make_movie(path,mixed=mixed,marker_only=not mixed,other_metadata_tracks=auxiliaries,
               dual_identifier='old-identifier',front_moov=front,movie_scale=clock,movie_duration=clock,
               dref_atom=full(b'dref',integer(1)+full(b'alis',flags=1)))
    data=path.read_bytes()
    # Growing a front index would shift sample offsets. Native-style fixtures
    # here are tail-moov; front layout has separate tests using fixed sizes.
    assert not front
    top=split_atoms(data)
    raw=marked_track(path)[0]
    tkhd=bytearray(child(raw,b'tkhd'));tkhd[:4]=b'\0\0\0\x0f'
    tkhd[20:24]=integer(clock//5+1)
    mdia=child(raw,b'mdia')
    mdhd=bytearray(child(mdia,b'mdhd'))
    mdhd[12:20]=struct.pack('>II',clock,1)
    mdhd[20:22]=struct.pack('>H',21956)
    hdlr=full(b'hdlr',b'mhlrmetaappl'+integer(1)+integer(0)+b'\x13Core Media Metadata')
    minf=child(mdia,b'minf');stbl=child(minf,b'stbl')
    stsd=child(stbl,b'stsd');kind,entry,*_=split_atoms(stsd,8)[0]
    keys=child(entry[8:],b'keys')
    newkeys=b''.join(box(integer(marker_id),p) if child(p,b'keyd')==b'mdta'+KEY else whole
                     for k,p,whole,_ in split_atoms(keys))
    newentry=box(kind,entry[:8]+box(b'keys',newkeys))
    old_offset=struct.unpack_from('>I',child(stbl,b'stco'),8)[0]
    source_samples=movie._load(path).tracks[2].metadata_samples
    old_sample=source_samples[-1]
    marker_old=box(integer(1),b'\xff')
    marker_new=box(integer(marker_id),b'\xff')
    # Existing sample extent stays same length: just change the fixture key ID.
    sample_offset=old_offset+sum(map(len,source_samples[:-1]))
    data=data[:sample_offset]+old_sample.replace(marker_old,marker_new,1)+data[sample_offset+len(old_sample):]
    table={b'stsd':full(b'stsd',integer(1)+newentry),
           b'stts':full(b'stts',integer(1)+struct.pack('>II',1,1)),
           b'stsc':full(b'stsc',integer(1)+struct.pack('>III',1,1,1)),
           b'stsz':full(b'stsz',struct.pack('>III',0,1,len(old_sample))),
           b'stco':full(b'stco',integer(1)+integer(sample_offset))}
    newstbl=box(b'stbl',b''.join(table.get(k,whole) for k,p,whole,_ in split_atoms(stbl)))
    minf=replace_child(minf,b'stbl',newstbl)
    minf=full(b'hdlr',b'dhlralisappl'+b'\0'*8+b'\x13Core Media Data Hdlr')+minf
    mdia=replace_child(mdia,b'mdhd',box(b'mdhd',bytes(mdhd)))
    mdia=replace_child(mdia,b'hdlr',hdlr)
    mdia=replace_child(mdia,b'minf',box(b'minf',minf))
    edts=box(b'edts',full(b'elst',integer(2)+struct.pack('>IihhIihh',clock//5,-1,1,0,1,0,1,0)))
    newraw=box(b'tkhd',bytes(tkhd))+edts+box(b'tref',box(b'cdsc',integer(1)))+box(b'mdia',mdia)
    # Replace only the final movie box, so all encoded sample offsets stay put.
    moov=child(data,b'moov')
    changed=b''.join(box(b'trak',newraw) if k==b'trak' and p==raw else whole
                     for k,p,whole,_ in split_atoms(moov))
    path.write_bytes(b''.join(box(b'moov',changed) if k==b'moov' else whole
                             for k,p,whole,_ in split_atoms(data)))
    assert movie.inspect_live_photo_movie(path).supported


class Round2MovieTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp=tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.root=Path(self.tmp.name)
        self.source,self.output=self.root/'source.mov',self.root/'output.mov'

    def tearDown(self):
        self.tmp.cleanup()

    def run_rewrite(self,when=Fraction(1,2)):
        before=self.source.read_bytes()
        result=rewrite_original_marker(self.source,self.output,when)
        self.assertEqual(before,self.source.read_bytes())
        self.assertEqual(result['source_sha256'],hashlib.sha256(before).hexdigest())
        self.assertEqual(result['output_sha256'],hashlib.sha256(self.output.read_bytes()).hexdigest())
        self.assertFalse(result['identifier_changed'])
        self.assertFalse(result['import_compatibility_verified'])
        self.assertEqual(movie.inspect_live_photo_movie(self.output).photo_time,when)
        return result

    def assert_native_shell(self,original,output):
        old=movie._load(original).tracks[2]
        new=next(t for t in movie._load(output).tracks if t.marker_ids)
        self.assertEqual(child(child(old.box.payload,b'mdia'),b'hdlr'),child(child(new.box.payload,b'mdia'),b'hdlr'))
        self.assertEqual(child(old.box.payload,b'tref'),child(new.box.payload,b'tref'))
        self.assertEqual(child(old.box.payload,b'tkhd')[:4],child(new.box.payload,b'tkhd')[:4])
        self.assertEqual(child(child(child(old.box.payload,b'mdia'),b'minf'),b'hdlr'),
                         child(child(child(new.box.payload,b'mdia'),b'minf'),b'hdlr'))
        self.assertEqual(child(child(child(old.box.payload,b'mdia'),b'minf'),b'dinf'),
                         child(child(child(new.box.payload,b'mdia'),b'minf'),b'dinf'))
        self.assertEqual([k.data for k in old.keys if k.kind in old.marker_ids],[k.data for k in new.keys])
        self.assertEqual(new.marker_ids,{integer(7)})
        self.assertEqual(new.metadata_samples,[box(integer(7),b'\xff')])
        self.assertEqual(new.scale,old.scale)
        self.assertEqual(child(new.stbl.payload,b'stts'),b'\0'*4+integer(1)+struct.pack('>II',1,1))

    def test_mixed_clone_preserves_native_shell_and_original_other_data(self):
        native_source(self.source)
        result=self.run_rewrite()
        self.assert_native_shell(self.source,self.output)
        old=movie._load(self.source);new=movie._load(self.output)
        self.assertEqual(result['marker_duration'],{'numerator':1,'denominator':1000})
        self.assertTrue(result['marker_duration_preserved'])
        self.assertTrue(result['mixed_track_split'])
        self.assertEqual(result['source_marker_track'],3)
        self.assertEqual(result['output_marker_track'],6)
        self.assertEqual(len(new.tracks),6)
        self.assertEqual(new.tracks[2].times,old.tracks[2].times)
        self.assertEqual(new.tracks[2].metadata_samples,[box(integer(2),b'second\0')])
        self.assertEqual([t.box.data for t in new.tracks[:2]+new.tracks[3:5]],
                         [t.box.data for t in old.tracks[:2]+old.tracks[3:5]])
        self.assertEqual(child(child(self.source.read_bytes(),b'moov'),b'meta'),
                         child(child(self.output.read_bytes(),b'moov'),b'meta'))
        self.assertTrue(result['trailing_moov_replaced_without_dead_index'])
        self.assertEqual([k for k,*_ in split_atoms(self.output.read_bytes())],[b'ftyp',b'mdat',b'mdat',b'moov'])

    def test_pure_marker_reuses_track_id_and_native_shell(self):
        native_source(self.source,mixed=False)
        result=self.run_rewrite()
        self.assert_native_shell(self.source,self.output)
        self.assertFalse(result['mixed_track_split'])
        self.assertEqual(result['source_marker_track'],result['output_marker_track'])
        self.assertEqual(result['track_count'],5)

    def test_zero_time_omits_zero_length_empty_edit(self):
        native_source(self.source)
        self.run_rewrite(Fraction(0))
        elst=child(child(marked_track(self.output)[0],b'edts'),b'elst')
        self.assertEqual(elst,b'\0'*4+integer(1)+struct.pack('>Iihh',1,0,1,0))

    def test_native_600_clock_keeps_one_600th_second_and_original_key_seven(self):
        native_source(self.source,clock=600)
        result=self.run_rewrite()
        self.assert_native_shell(self.source,self.output)
        self.assertEqual(result['marker_duration'],{'numerator':1,'denominator':600})
        self.assertEqual(result['marker_media_scale'],600)
        elst=child(child(marked_track(self.output)[0],b'edts'),b'elst')
        self.assertEqual(elst,b'\0'*4+integer(2)+struct.pack('>IihhIihh',300,-1,1,0,1,0,1,0))

    def test_version_one_native_clocks_and_edit_remain_version_one(self):
        native_source(self.source,clock=600)
        data=self.source.read_bytes();raw=marked_track(self.source)[0]
        tkhd=child(raw,b'tkhd');mdia=child(raw,b'mdia');mdhd=child(mdia,b'mdhd')
        newtkhd=full(b'tkhd',struct.pack('>QQIIQ',11,12,3,0,121)+tkhd[24:],version=1,flags=15)
        newmdhd=full(b'mdhd',struct.pack('>QQIQHH',11,12,600,1,21956,0),version=1)
        newedit=box(b'edts',full(b'elst',integer(2)+struct.pack('>QqhhQqhh',120,-1,1,0,1,0,1,0),version=1))
        updated=replace_child(raw,b'tkhd',newtkhd)
        updated=replace_child(updated,b'edts',newedit)
        updated=replace_child(updated,b'mdia',box(b'mdia',replace_child(mdia,b'mdhd',newmdhd)))
        moov=child(data,b'moov')
        self.source.write_bytes(b''.join(box(b'moov',b''.join(box(k,updated) if k==b'trak' and p==raw else whole
                 for k,p,whole,_ in split_atoms(moov))) if k==b'moov' else whole for k,p,whole,_ in split_atoms(data)))
        self.run_rewrite()
        result,mdia,_,_=marked_track(self.output)
        self.assertEqual(child(result,b'tkhd')[0],1)
        self.assertEqual(child(mdia,b'mdhd')[0],1)
        self.assertEqual(child(child(result,b'edts'),b'elst')[0],1)
        self.assertEqual(struct.unpack_from('>Q',child(result,b'tkhd'),28)[0],301)

    def test_new_track_id_is_reflected_in_movie_next_track_id(self):
        native_source(self.source)
        result=self.run_rewrite()
        next_id=struct.unpack('>I',child(child(self.output.read_bytes(),b'moov'),b'mvhd')[-4:])[0]
        self.assertEqual(next_id,result['output_marker_track']+1)

    def test_last_frame_keeps_original_duration_not_whole_frame(self):
        native_source(self.source)
        result=self.run_rewrite(Fraction(1,2))
        self.assertEqual(result['marker_duration'],{'numerator':1,'denominator':1000})
        self.assertNotEqual(result['marker_duration'],{'numerator':1,'denominator':2})

    def test_repeated_rewrites_reuse_existing_pure_clone(self):
        native_source(self.source)
        self.run_rewrite()
        output2=self.root/'again.mov'
        result=rewrite_original_marker(self.output,output2,Fraction(0))
        self.assertFalse(result['mixed_track_split'])
        self.assertEqual(result['output_marker_track'],6)
        self.assertEqual(result['track_count'],6)
        self.assertEqual(result['marker_duration'],{'numerator':1,'denominator':1000})
        self.assertEqual(movie.inspect_live_photo_movie(output2).marker_count,1)

    def test_front_moov_keeps_original_mdat_offsets(self):
        make_movie(self.source,mixed=True,dual_identifier='old-identifier',front_moov=True)
        result=self.run_rewrite()
        self.assertFalse(result['trailing_moov_replaced_without_dead_index'])
        self.assertTrue(result['verification']['all_original_mdat_payloads_preserved'])

    def test_auxv_track_bytes_are_untouched(self):
        native_source(self.source)
        data=self.source.read_bytes()
        self.source.write_bytes(data.replace(b'soun',b'auxv',1))
        original=tracks(self.source)[1]
        result=self.run_rewrite()
        self.assertEqual(tracks(self.output)[1],original)
        self.assertEqual(result['verification']['auxiliary_track_ids'],[2])

    def test_noninteger_movie_tick_refused_instead_of_rounding(self):
        make_movie(self.source,marker_only=True,dual_identifier='old-identifier',video_scale=30000,deltas=(1001,1001,27998))
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'不能由原 movie'):
            rewrite_original_marker(self.source,self.output,Fraction(1001,30000))
        self.assertFalse(self.output.exists())

    def test_missing_original_marker_refused(self):
        make_movie(self.source,dual_identifier='old-identifier')
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'唯一有效'):
            rewrite_original_marker(self.source,self.output,Fraction(1,2))
        self.assertFalse(self.output.exists())

    def test_missing_identifier_refused(self):
        make_movie(self.source,marker_only=True)
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'缺少配对标识'):
            rewrite_original_marker(self.source,self.output,Fraction(1,2))
        self.assertFalse(self.output.exists())

    def test_non_minus_one_marker_refused(self):
        native_source(self.source)
        old=movie._load(self.source).tracks[2]
        offset=old.samples[0][0]
        with self.source.open('r+b') as stream:
            stream.seek(offset+8);stream.write(b'\0')
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'int8 -1'):
            rewrite_original_marker(self.source,self.output,Fraction(1,2))
        self.assertFalse(self.output.exists())

    def test_unknown_sample_extension_refused(self):
        native_source(self.source)
        data=self.source.read_bytes();original=marked_track(self.source)[0]
        mdia=child(original,b'mdia');minf=child(mdia,b'minf');stbl=child(minf,b'stbl')
        changed=replace_child(original,b'mdia',box(b'mdia',replace_child(mdia,b'minf',
                    box(b'minf',replace_child(minf,b'stbl',box(b'stbl',stbl+box(b'zzzz',b'opaque')))))))
        moov=child(data,b'moov')
        self.source.write_bytes(b''.join(box(b'moov',b''.join(box(k,changed) if k==b'trak' and p==original else whole
                 for k,p,whole,_ in split_atoms(moov))) if k==b'moov' else whole for k,p,whole,_ in split_atoms(data)))
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'扩展'):
            rewrite_original_marker(self.source,self.output,Fraction(1,2))
        self.assertFalse(self.output.exists())

    def test_cancellation_preserves_source_and_no_destination(self):
        native_source(self.source)
        before=self.source.read_bytes();cancel=threading.Event();cancel.set()
        with self.assertRaises(InterruptedError):
            rewrite_original_marker(self.source,self.output,Fraction(1,2),cancel)
        self.assertFalse(self.output.exists());self.assertEqual(self.source.read_bytes(),before)

    def test_output_collision_and_same_source_refused(self):
        native_source(self.source)
        self.output.write_bytes(b'keep')
        for output in (self.output,self.source):
            with self.subTest(output=output),self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                rewrite_original_marker(self.source,output,Fraction(1,2))
        self.assertEqual(self.output.read_bytes(),b'keep')

    def test_postwrite_failure_cleans_only_new_file(self):
        native_source(self.source)
        before=self.source.read_bytes()
        with patch('engine_movie_round2._verify_preserved',side_effect=ValueError('controlled verification failure')):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'controlled verification'):
                rewrite_original_marker(self.source,self.output,Fraction(1,2))
        self.assertFalse(self.output.exists());self.assertEqual(self.source.read_bytes(),before)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main(verbosity=2)

当前公开正文 UTF-8 SHA-256：21e89e67086f5f31114e11907e71b59188137afff1744a3fdd496aec33347953 · 8332 字节（HTML 解码后，含正文换行）。

"""Synthetic HEVC/LPCM/HEIC end-to-end check for all three portable methods."""
import argparse
from fractions import Fraction
import hashlib
import importlib.util
import io
import json
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import uuid

LAB = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(LAB))
from PIL import Image
from core.heic_encoder import encode_heic
from core.frame_reader import FrameReader
from core.live_photo_movie import inspect_live_photo_movie
from core.live_photo_image import read_content_identifier
from engine_round2 import Session, tools
from engine_image import copy_movie_with_identifier, _capture
from methods import get_method


def sha(path):
    with path.open('rb') as stream:
        return hashlib.file_digest(stream, 'sha256').hexdigest()


def probe(path):
    _, ffprobe = tools()
    result = subprocess.run([str(ffprobe), '-v', 'error', '-show_streams', '-show_packets',
                             '-show_data_hash', 'sha256', '-of', 'json', str(path)],
                            capture_output=True, check=True, creationflags=subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW)
    return json.loads(result.stdout)


def packet_signature(data):
    streams = {s['index']: s for s in data['streams']}
    result = []
    for stream in data['streams']:
        if stream['codec_type'] in ('video', 'audio'):
            result.append((stream['codec_type'], stream['codec_name'],
                           [p['data_hash'] for p in data['packets'] if p['stream_index'] == stream['index']]))
    return result


def source_hashes():
    paths = (sorted(LAB.glob('*.py')) + sorted((LAB / 'core').glob('*.py'))
             + [LAB / 'tests/test_engine_image.py', Path(__file__)])
    return {p.relative_to(LAB).as_posix(): sha(p) for p in paths if p.name != 'publish_round2.py'}


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument('--report', required=True, type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    started_hashes = source_hashes()
    result = {'schema': 'live-photo-method-lab.round2-integration.v1', 'private_media_used': False,
              'gui_started': False, 'iphone_import_verified': False, 'source_sha256_before': started_hashes,
              'cases': []}
    directory = LAB / 'reports' / ('integration-artifacts-' + time.strftime('%Y%m%d-%H%M%S') + '-' + uuid.uuid4().hex[:8])
    directory.mkdir()
    try:
        previous = json.loads((LAB.parent / 'live-photo-method-lab/reports/engine-lpcm-final-01.json').read_text(encoding='utf8'))
        old_image = Path(previous['fixture']['source_image'])
        old_movie = Path(previous['fixture']['source_movie'])
        old_hashes = {str(p): sha(p) for p in (old_image, old_movie)}
        image, movie = directory / 'SyntheticFullApple.HEIC', directory / 'SyntheticFullApple.MOV'
        identifier = '11111111-2222-3333-4444-555555555555'
        copy_movie_with_identifier(old_movie, movie, identifier)
        loader = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('image_tests', LAB / 'tests/test_engine_image.py')
        fixtures = importlib.util.module_from_spec(loader)
        loader.loader.exec_module(fixtures)
        note = fixtures.make_note(identifier)
        with Image.open(old_image) as original:
            source_pixels = original.convert('RGB')
        encode_heic(source_pixels, image, exif=fixtures.complex_exif().tobytes())
        capture = _capture(image, None)
        assert 'GPS:GPSVersionID' not in capture
        assert read_content_identifier(image) == identifier
        fixture_hashes = {str(p): sha(p) for p in (image, movie)}
        baseline_probe = probe(movie)
        baseline_packets = packet_signature(baseline_probe)
        output_parent = directory / 'output'
        output_parent.mkdir()
        session = Session(image)
        selected = 4
        pixels = session.reader.export(selected)
        selected_time = session.metadata.frames[selected].presentation_time
        old_marker = session.movie_info.photo_time
        assert selected_time != old_marker
        new_ids = set()
        try:
            for code in ('07', '08', '09'):
                folder, record = session.export(selected, get_method(code), output_parent)
                new_ids.add(record['identifier'])
                target_image = folder / (image.stem + ('.HEIC' if code == '09' else '.JPG'))
                target_movie = folder / movie.name
                got = probe(target_movie)
                assert packet_signature(got) == baseline_packets
                expected_time = old_marker if code == '07' else selected_time
                metadata_streams = {stream['index']: stream for stream in got['streams']
                                    if stream['codec_type'] == 'data'}
                marker_packets = [packet for packet in got['packets']
                                  if packet['stream_index'] in metadata_streams]
                assert len(marker_packets) == 1
                packet = marker_packets[0]
                pts = Fraction(packet['pts']) * Fraction(metadata_streams[packet['stream_index']]['time_base'])
                assert pts == expected_time
                assert inspect_live_photo_movie(target_movie).photo_time == expected_time
                assert read_content_identifier(target_image) == record['identifier']
                assert record['image']['typed_all_nonid_values_equal']
                assert record['image']['capture_tags_preserved']
                assert _capture(target_image, None) == capture
                assert record['encoded_audio_video_payloads_unchanged']
                ffmpeg, ffprobe = tools()
                with FrameReader(target_movie, ffmpeg=ffmpeg, ffprobe=ffprobe) as reader:
                    check_pixels = reader.export(selected)
                    assert check_pixels.size == pixels.size and check_pixels.tobytes() == pixels.tobytes()
                with Image.open(target_image) as decoded:
                    decoded.load()
                    assert decoded.size == pixels.size
                    if code != '09':
                        expected = io.BytesIO()
                        pixels.save(expected, 'JPEG', quality=100, subsampling=0)
                        with Image.open(io.BytesIO(expected.getvalue())) as reference:
                            assert reference.convert('RGB').tobytes() == decoded.convert('RGB').tobytes()
                result['cases'].append({'method': code, 'status': 'passed', 'output': str(folder),
                                        'output_id': record['identifier'], 'marker_time': str(pts),
                                        'encoded_packet_hashes_equal': True, 'selected_decoded_video_pixels_equal': True,
                                        'full_nonid_apple_metadata_preserved': True, 'record_sha256': sha(folder / '处理记录.json')})
                result['cases'][-1].update(complex_capture_metadata_exact=True,
                                          gps_version_absence_preserved=True)
        finally:
            session.close()
        assert len(new_ids) == 3
        assert {path: sha(Path(path)) for path in fixture_hashes} == fixture_hashes
        assert {path: sha(Path(path)) for path in old_hashes} == old_hashes
        result.update(status='passed', source_and_prior_fixtures_unchanged=True,
                      source_image=str(image), source_movie=str(movie), source_pair_sha256=fixture_hashes,
                      selected_frame=selected, selected_time=str(selected_time), old_marker_time=str(old_marker),
                      synthetic_note_fields=[1, 8, 17, 23, 65433])
    except BaseException as error:
        import traceback
        result.update(status='failed', error=str(error), traceback=traceback.format_exc())
    result['source_sha256_after'] = source_hashes()
    result['source_stable_during_run'] = result['source_sha256_after'] == started_hashes
    args.report.write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf8')
    print(json.dumps({k: v for k, v in result.items() if k not in ('source_sha256_before', 'source_sha256_after')}, ensure_ascii=True, indent=2))
    return 0 if result['status'] == 'passed' and result['source_stable_during_run'] else 1


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())

当前公开正文 UTF-8 SHA-256：6d684d79ffdd5cfc4f8980ab3ffe34a4909af6e079fe8b2c91985d1d386cfbb5 · 3504 字节（HTML 解码后，含正文换行）。

Live Photo 独立实验工具 · 第二轮保存修复1（2.0-experiment.2）

本包修复上一包在保存时出现“拍摄日期、时区、GPS 或相机信息未完整保留”的问题。
上一包在本机保存阶段失败，尚未产生可供手机验证的结果；不能由此判断 07/08/09 的导入兼容性。
本轮保留原始 GPS 字段及精确数值，不通过舍入或忽略校验放行。窗口标题应显示“保存修复1”。
请先关闭旧 demo，完整解压本包到新的文件夹，不覆盖旧程序目录。

上一轮 JPEG + 重封装 MOV demo 已由用户确认无法导入。本轮换用原机 Apple 信息与原机时间轨结构。
这是独立实验工具，不是 LiveViewer 新版本。所有结果只保存到新文件夹，不覆盖原件、不联网、不上传。

先试 08；若仍失败，再分别试 09 和 07。

08_原机时间轨_JPG.exe
新 JPG 保留原片完整 Apple MakerNote 信息；沿用原片有效的时间轨结构，将标记移到所选帧。
配对 ID 由 ExifTool 更新为新的 UUID，音视频不重新编码。

09_原机时间轨_HEIC.exe
视频方法与 08 相同，新封面改用真正的 HEIC，用于比较图片容器。
新 HEIC 是视频帧编码，不保留原相机 HEIC 的增益图或其他辅助图像。

07_保留原视频时间_JPG.exe（对照）
新 JPG 保留完整 Apple MakerNote 信息，MOV 只更新配对 ID，保留旧关键帧时间。
此方法用于判断“新图片是否能配对导入”；即使导入成功，也不能据此认定关键帧时间已经正确更新。

使用步骤

1. 完整解压后保留三个 EXE 和 _internal 文件夹的位置与名称，运行对应编号程序。
2. 选择原始、曾能正常导入的 HEIC/JPG + MOV 中的一个文件；应位于同一文件夹且同名。
3. 拖动时间轴或按左右键选择关键帧。三种方法请尽量选同一帧，方便比较。
4. 点击“保存这张关键帧的实验副本”，选择输出位置。每次生成独立的新文件夹、新 UUID。
5. 用爱思“导入实况照片”仅导入这一结果文件夹中的一组。不要混选原组、旧 v1 或其他方法结果。

请反馈：方法编号；是否导入为一个 LIVE；长按能否播放；关键照片是否正确；声音是否正常。
失败时可提供结果文件夹的处理记录.json／失败记录.txt，无需上传私人照片。

技术边界

完整复制的是原拍摄 MakerNote 字段和值；拍摄日期、时区和 GPS 从原始 EXIF 精确保留。
不带入旧缩略图，封面的方向、尺寸和修改时间按新图片更新；ExifTool 读回与原始字段检查均须通过。
ExifTool 可能规范化内部对齐及偏移，处理记录区分物理字节与字段值保留。
除了配对 ID，MakerNote 字段保留源值，包括 LivePhotoVideoIndex；不把未知字段猜作所选帧编号。
它们仍是源拍摄记录，不是视频帧的新相机测量。JPG 有损编码；本实验不评估 HDR 保真。
08/09 保留原标记的编码、类型、媒体时间基和持续时间，只移动对应视频时间；混合轨的其他元数据保持原时间。
若遇到无法精确处理的时间基或结构，程序报错停止，不近似、不删除轨道绕过检查。

自动校验只证明本机的文件结构、数据保留与元信息一致；是否能被爱思/iPhone 接受，仍以你单独导入结果为准。
本轮尚未通过手机验收，不将任何候选提前加入 LiveViewer。
